We inject 7 controlled defects into a $τ^2$-bench agent across two domains. Coverage yields a ceiling: if a defect's traced footprint holds $b$ scenarios that pass at baseline, it can itself contribute at most $b/N$ to a suite of $N$. For 4 of 5 traceable defects that ceiling lies at or below the detection threshold $θ= 0.10$, so no within-footprint effect can move the suite past it; in both domains the tool-removal defect destroys every covered scenario it can (exactly its ceiling) while the suite-level pass rate moves the other way. Defects split into two regimes: trace-aligned ones, where per-cell scoring recovers the tool-removal defect in full and reveals a second as a significant improvement, and trace-orthogonal ones, whose footprint is behavioural: forced escalation is detected in retail at a traced coverage of exactly zero. Diversity along the generator's own axes helps only where it aligns: at fixed suite size a happy-path-only suite detects nothing, while a kind-diverse one detects the orchestration defect in 5 of 20 draws. Evaluation archives should be scored per cell and should publish coverage as a first-class property.

</details>


### [35] [What LLM Agents See and What Users Say](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17639)
*Yue Zhao,Angela Huyue Zhang,S. Alex Yang,Hua Wei,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文借鉴语言学的预设理论，研究智能体在取消预订、删除文件夹等不可逆操作前是否会因工具显示的"假预设"而误判。作者给出理论下界：当检索无法区分"一条或两条记录"时，双记录与单记录下智能体不问即行事的频率相同。实验操纵检索结果为列表、计数或隐去第二条匹配，以及用户声称记录为空等条件；在仅角色提示下，计数到第二条记录时多数模型仍对大可见记录采取行动，而列出两条则能阻止多数行动。用户声称"为空"时，计数与列出效果相近。最新Claude与GPT会先打开计数的第二条记录再拒绝行动。在零售基准中加入同名同邮编客户后，返回错误客户的检索在约四分之一对话中导致错误账户写入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体执行取消预订、删除文件夹等操作前，可能基于工具显示（如只看到一条记录）而非用户陈述形成错误预设，从而在未确认的情况下行动。现有研究缺乏对这一"预设缺口"的系统刻画，也缺少评估方法。

Method: 引入语言学的预设理论：智能体只有在行动背后的每个主张（如某条记录与姓名匹配）被证实成立、由用户答复或可在可撤销阶段披露时才可行动。给出理论下界。实验方面：操纵检索结果以列表、计数或省略第二条匹配的方式呈现，并操纵用户声称记录为空等真假主张；测试36个智能体在仅角色提示下的行为，并对最新Claude与GPT进行测试；在零售基准中注入同名同邮编客户，比较不同检索方式对写入正确率的影响。

Result: 理论下界表明：检索无法区分一条与两条记录时，任何智能体的"不问即行动"频率在两记录与单记录情形下相同。仅角色提示下，计数到第二条记录时多数智能体仍对可见记录行动，列出两条则能阻止多数；用户声称"为空"时计数与列出效果相当，且主张为假时通常能抑制行动。最新Claude与GPT会先打开计数的第二条记录再拒绝行动。零售基准中返回错误客户的检索导致约四分之一对话出现错误账户写入；而检查用户所知街道的检索可将完成率维持在单客户水平。

Conclusion: 安全写入需要能列出或核验竞争记录的检索机制，而非仅返回计数或单条结果；同时评估应系统变化工具呈现内容，以暴露预设立场下的误行动风险。

Abstract: Before an LLM agent cancels a reservation or deletes a folder for a user, what stops it from acting on a false assumption: what its tools show, or what the user says? In this work, we study this gap with presupposition theory from linguistics: an agent may act only if each claim behind the act (e.g., one reservation fits the name) is seen to hold, answered by the user, or disclosed while the act can be undone. A simple bound shows that when lookups cannot tell one record from two, any agent acts without asking first as often with two records as with one. We then vary whether a search lists, counts, or omits a second match, and whether the user states a claim that a record refutes (e.g., "It is empty"). Under a role-only prompt, a count of a second record leaves most of 36 earlier agents acting on the one they see, whereas listing both stops most. When the user says a record such as a folder is empty, however, a count of its one item stops agents about as often as a listing, and stating the claim mostly cuts acting when it is false. The newest Claude and GPT models always open a counted second record, then never act. When we add a same-name, same-zip customer to a retail benchmark, a lookup returning that customer first led to wrong-account writes in about a quarter of conversations, and one that checks a street the user knows kept completion near the one-customer level. Safe writes therefore need lookups that list or check competing records, and evaluations that vary what tools show.

</details>


### [36] [Learn then Bet: Learning Interpretable Rules for Reliable Agent Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17662)
*You-Lin Chen,Xinyi Lisa Chen,Pedro Herrero-Vidal,Xiaojing Yu,Bingqing Song,Bin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeBet 提出一种"先学习后下注"的统计学习框架，学习可解释的 OR-of-AND 运行时监控器，并用固定下注统计量累积证据触发干预；在有限类内可交换性与留一稳定性下给出训练条件下（training-conditional）的假阳性控制与检测力有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体常需运行数百步，实践中依赖轮次上限、关键词过滤等启发式监控来决定停止、升级或人工介入；这些启发式要么误停本可成功的运行，要么放任失败运行继续，缺乏统计可靠性保证。

Method: 将监控学习目标由"有效且高检测力干预"的充分条件导出；学习 OR-of-AND 形式的可解释规则监控器，并以固定下注统计量累积证据、达到预设阈值即干预。针对学习中的组合优化难题，利用布尔结构做随机局部搜索，提出旨在提升经验分离度的规则编辑。理论层面在类内可交换性与留一稳定性假设下推导有限样本保证，允许学习与校准复用同一批轨迹。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Lite 与 Ministral 3 8B 上，学习到的监控器节省 34.46% 的输入加输出 token，输出精确率由 18.33% 提升至 21.59%，经验假阳性率为 13.64%。

Conclusion: 该工作把"在可解释规则集上学习监控器"形式化为带统计条件的学习问题，并为基于部分历史轨迹的可靠干预建立了训练条件保证，弥补了启发式监控缺乏有效性/检测力刻画与有限样本保障的不足。

Abstract: Autonomous agents now run for hundreds of steps with little or no human supervision. In practice, practitioners use runtime monitors such as turn limits and keyword filters to decide when a run should be stopped, upgraded, or assisted. However, these heuristics may stop runs that would otherwise succeed or allow failing runs to continue. To address this limitation, we introduce LeBet (Learn then Bet), a statistical learning framework that derives its learning objective from sufficient conditions for valid and powerful intervention. In particular, LeBet learns an interpretable OR-of-AND monitor and accumulates evidence through a fixed betting statistic, intervening when the evidence reaches a prescribed threshold. Under finite within-class exchangeability and leave-one-out stability, we derive finite-sample guarantees for false-positive control (validity) and detection power conditional on the training data, using the same trajectories for learning and calibration without assuming independent and identically distributed trajectories. To address the intractable combinatorial optimization problem in our learning formulation, we use stochastic local search that leverages the monitor's Boolean structure to propose rule edits aimed at improving empirical separation. On SWE-bench Lite with Ministral 3 8B, the learned monitor saves $34.46\%$ of input-plus-output tokens and increases output precision from $18.33\%$ to $21.59\%$. The empirical false-positive rate is $13.64\%$. Our results show how to formalize the learning problem over interpretable rule sets with statistical conditions and establish training-conditional guarantees for reliable intervention based on partial histories.

</details>


### [37] [Aligned, Conflicting, or Unspecified? A Diagnostic Benchmark for Multimodal Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17680)
*Xintong Wang,Haihan Nan,Ruidong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出首个按"文本—历史关系"组织的真实场景诊断基准ACU-Bench，标注对齐/冲突/未指明三类，并在十个零样本模型上诊断文本选择、组合与误差、跨度修订控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIoT系统 increasingly 将感知时序与专家报告、新闻等外部文本结合，但模型对与历史趋势一致或冲突的文本能否可靠利用尚不清楚，缺乏按文本—历史关系细分的真实诊断基准。

Method: 构建1,762对文本—窗口数据，覆盖Weather传感器遥测、Wikipedia网络服务流量、Finance市场数据流，按文本方向与历史趋势一致/相反/未陈述标注为Aligned、Conflicting、Unspecified；零样本评估十个预训练模型，RQ1比较两类文本（直接预测温度变化 vs 描述风、锋面等相关天气状况）及其组合（仅取冲突文本的幅度与时序信息叠加到第二类）；RQ2用误差分解归因误差增大来自过度修订，并提出免训练规则限制修订幅度。

Result: Aligned下第一类文本对多数模型误差最低；Conflicting下其使多数模型误差高于无文本基线；组合策略相较任一单类文本使六个模型MASE下降、七个模型DA上升。免训练修订限制规则在存在该权衡的四个模型上同时优于有/无文本预测，MASE降低4.49%–21.98%，DA提升2.99%–9.71%。

Conclusion: 文本与历史关系是决定文本效用的关键因素，冲突文本需谨慎组合、误差源于过度修订，幅度约束的免训练规则可稳定提升预测精度与方向准确率；数据集已开源。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence of Things (AIoT) systems increasingly combine sensed time series with external text such as expert reports and news, yet how reliably models use text that agrees or conflicts with the sensed history remains unclear. We introduce the first real-world diagnostic benchmark organized by text-history relationship: 1,762 text-window pairs across sensor telemetry (Weather), web-service traffic (Wikipedia), and market data streams (Finance), each labeled Aligned, Conflicting, or Unspecified by whether the text's direction agrees with the historical trend, opposes it, or is not stated. Across ten pretrained models evaluated zero-shot, we investigate text selection and combination under text-history relationships (RQ1) and error diagnosis and horizon-scaled revision control (RQ2). For RQ1, we compare two types of text on paired Weather windows: one directly forecasts temperature changes, while the other describes related weather conditions, such as winds and fronts. Under Aligned, the first type yields the lowest error for most models. Under Conflicting, it increases most models' error above the no-text baseline; we therefore add only its stated magnitude and timing to the second type. Compared with either type alone, this combination lowers mean absolute scaled error (MASE) for six models and raises directional accuracy (DA) for seven. For RQ2, an error decomposition attributes the error increase to oversized revisions, and a training-free rule that limits them outperforms forecasts both with and without text for the four models with this trade-off, lowering MASE by 4.49%-21.98% and raising DA by 2.99%-9.71%. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/Shelton1202/ACU-Bench.

</details>


### [38] [OCTAVE: A Capability-Resolved Benchmark for End-to-End HTML5 Game Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17681)
*Kehang Li,Xinlan Xu,Yulin Jin,Heng Cao,Xuchen Li,Houxian Ren,Zihao Ji,Qiang Luo,Yadong Mao,Qilin He,Jian Zhang,Jian Zhao,Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OCTAVE 是一个面向完整 HTML5 游戏生成的基准，围绕八大能力维度、含 180 个任务与 1,800 条评估要求，以需求级静态诊断替代单一聚合评分；11 个模型-框架组合仅达 33.2% Completeness 与 32.5% Richness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整游戏生成需要在长周期开发中协调规则、交互、状态与表现，单一聚合分数难以揭示哪些能力已实现、哪些需求未满足，缺乏细粒度诊断手段。

Method: 构建八能力维度框架，设计 180 任务/1,800 评估要求，任务来源为真实玩家请求、商业机制复现与专家设计，覆盖 PC Web、移动 Web、2D、3D；将需求划分为公开核心实现的 Completeness 与隐含深层系统关联的 Richness；采用静态源码判定做需求级诊断。

Result: 11 个模型-框架组合平均 Completeness 33.2%、Richness 32.5%；19.9% 生成记录为静态门槛失败或缺失产物；排除同模型自评后最佳组合公开与隐藏需求均低于 50%；Richness 并非在所有任务条件下都难于 Completeness；Core Rules 与 Numerical Design 表现较好，AI/NPC 与 Feedback、Game Feel 差距突出；需求难度与通过率仅弱负相关；人工交叉核验显示主要分歧方向是人工判为满足而自动评估判为失败。

Conclusion: OCTAVE 将评估从聚合分数推进到按需求、能力与任务条件定位的诊断，揭示当前系统在完整游戏生成上的系统性短板与评估器校准问题。

Abstract: Complete game generation requires agents to coordinate rules, interactions, states, and presentation over a long development process. A single aggregate score obscures which capabilities are implemented and which requirements remain unmet. We present OCTAVE, a benchmark for complete HTML5 game generation organized around eight Capability Dimensions, with 180 tasks and 1,800 Evaluation Requirements drawn from real player requests, commercial-mechanics reconstruction, and human expert design. It covers PC Web, Mobile Web, 2D, and 3D. Requirements distinguish Completeness, assessing publicly specified core implementation, from Richness, assessing hidden, deeper system relationships. Static source-code judgments provide requirement-level diagnosis across capabilities and task conditions. Evaluation of 11 model-harness combinations yields 33.2% Completeness and 32.5% Richness; 19.9% of generation records are static gate failures or missing artifacts. Excluding same-model self-evaluation, even the best combination remains below 50% on both public and hidden requirements. Richness is not harder than Completeness under every task condition. Systems perform relatively better on Core Rules and Numerical Design, while AI/NPC and Feedback and Game Feel show more prominent gaps. Requirement difficulty offers coarse stratification but is only weakly negatively correlated with pass rates. A limited human cross-check finds that requirements judged satisfied by humans but failed by the automated evaluator are the main direction of disagreement. OCTAVE extends evaluation beyond aggregate scores to diagnosis localized by requirement, capability, and task condition.

</details>


### [39] [Co-Sight Harness: Externalized Control Assets and Deterministic Enforcement for Long-Horizon Business Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17696)
*Luyao Wang,Xijun Zhu,Zhaohui Hao,Xiwen Zhang,Xingyu Wen,Weishan Li,Yuyang Liao,Yanqin Gao,Yongsheng Du,Lingjun Huang,Shaowei Zhao,Gang Li,Qifan Yu,Baoli Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种不修改大模型本体的长程业务工作流可靠性方案：将工作流定义、状态与领域知识外置为版本化控制资产，由状态机与机器可校验的验收门强制执行，独立审计器对执行轨迹评分，资产仅在回归检查与人工审批后更新。在三类超200步的已部署工作流上验证，Domain A 端到端无值守完成率由50%升至100%，检索召回由56%升至91.67%，端到端时延由440分钟降至159分钟，场景分析召回由45%升至93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程业务工作流存在两类失效：随活动链延长而累积的误差，以及活动所需知识与用户请求之间词汇（术语体系）不匹配。作者探究在不改变语言模型的前提下能否使此类工作流可靠。

Method: 保持主干模型参数固定，将工作流定义、执行状态和领域知识迁移为模型外部的版本化控制资产；运行时由状态机和机器可校验的验收门（acceptance gates）强制约束这些资产；由独立审计器对生成轨迹进行评分；资产仅在通过回归检查并经人工审批后才更新。

Result: 在三个超过200步的已部署工作流上评估。Domain A 的120例端到端评测集显示：无值守完成率50%→100%，检索召回56%→91.67%，端到端时延440→159分钟，场景分析召回45%→93%。另外两个领域由未编写资产的团队接管，分别用4天和7天。控制消融实验区分了两类失效来源。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过将可靠性保障从模型内部转移到外部的版本化控制资产与执行期强制机制，可在不改变语言模型的情况下显著提升长程业务工作流的完成率、检索与场景分析召回并大幅降低时延；资产的可迁移性由第三方团队快速接管得到支持，消融实验进一步分离了误差累积与词汇不匹配两类失效的贡献。

Abstract: Long-horizon business workflows fail in two ways: errors accumulate as a chain of activities lengthens, and the knowledge an activity needs is written in a different vocabulary from the user's request. We study whether such workflows can be made reliable without changing the language model. Our method keeps the backbone fixed and moves the workflow definition, state and domain knowledge into versioned control assets outside the model. A state machine and machine-checkable acceptance gates enforce these assets during execution; an independent auditor grades the resulting trace. The assets are updated only after a regression check and human approval .We evaluate the method on three deployed workflows exceeding 200 steps. On Domain A, a 120-case end-to-end evaluation set shows unattended completion rising from 50% to 100%, retrieval recall from 56% to 91.67%, and end-to-end latency dropping from 440 to 159 minutes, with scenario-analysis recall rising from 45% to 93%. Two other domains were taken over by teams that did not write the assets, one in four days and one in seven days. A controlled ablation separates the two failure sources. The code is available at https://github.com/ZTE-AICloud/Co-Sight/tree/master/Co-Sight%20Harness.

</details>


### [40] [An AI-enabled high-resolution chlorophyll-a forecasting model that outperforms the operational global biogeochemical system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17704)
*Yong Jiang,Zheng Jiang,Gaowei Zhang,Zengzhou Hao,Suixiang Shi,Chi Zhang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChlCast——一个基于AI的叶绿素a浓度预报模型，可在60°S–60°N范围内以约4 km分辨率生成10天逐日海表叶绿素a预报，精度显著优于业务化Copernicus数值预报系统，并更好保留近岸梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海表叶绿素a浓度是海洋浮游植物变化的关键指标，其短期预报对海洋生态系统监测与管理有重要价值。但物理输运与生物过程的耦合使叶绿素a预报复杂化，传统数值模型模拟这些耦合过程计算代价高昂，因此需要更高效的高分辨率预报手段。



Method: ChlCast采用因子化时空卷积（factorized spatiotemporal convolutions）结合多尺度（multiscale）架构，从历史叶绿素a场与气候态信息中学习，输出近全球（60°S–60°N）约4 km分辨率、10天预见期的逐日预报。模型与业务化0.25° Copernicus全球海洋生物地球化学预报系统进行对比，评估数据包括卫星L4叶绿素a场和独立BGC-Argo现场观测，并开展近岸个例分析。



Result: 相对Copernicus系统，ChlCast在卫星L4叶绿素a场上将MAE和RMSE分别降低70.74%和47.63%；在独立BGC-Argo观测上分别降低35.03%和13.07%。近岸个例分析显示，4 km预报能保留被粗网格平均削弱的近岸梯度和局地浓度极大值。



Conclusion: 结果表明，观测驱动的深度学习有潜力在近全球尺度上实现海表叶绿素a变化的高分辨率预报，弥补传统数值模型在分辨率与计算成本上的不足。



Abstract: Surface chlorophyll-a concentration is a key indicator of marine phytoplankton variability, and short-term forecasts of its distribution provide advance information for marine ecosystem monitoring and management. However, interactions between physical transport and biological processes complicate chlorophyll-a forecasting, and simulating these coupled processes with conventional numerical models imposes substantial computational demands. Here we introduce ChlCast, an AI model that generates daily forecasts of surface chlorophyll-a concentration over a 10-day horizon at approximately 4-km resolution between 60$^\circ$S and 60$^\circ$N. ChlCast combines factorized spatiotemporal convolutions with a multiscale architecture to learn from historical chlorophyll-a fields and climatological information. Compared with the operational 0.25$^\circ$ Copernicus global ocean biogeochemical forecasting system, ChlCast reduces mean absolute error and root mean square error by 70.74% and 47.63%, respectively, against satellite-derived Level-4 chlorophyll-a fields, and by 35.03% and 13.07% against independent BGC-Argo in situ observations. Coastal case studies further show that the 4-km forecasts retain coastal gradients and localized concentration maxima that are weakened by coarse-grid averaging. These results demonstrate the potential of observation-driven deep learning for high-resolution forecasting of surface chlorophyll-a variability at the near-global scale.

</details>


### [41] [Cross-Linguistic Acoustic Biomarkers of Cognitive Impairment Across Interactive and Single-Speaker Speech Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17710)
*Kenyu Ikeuchi,Tomoaki Ohtsuki,Michelle Cohn,Alyssa Weakley,Yui Ishihara,Joseph Hoang,Taishiro Kishimoto,Sarah Tomaszewski Farias,Jet M. J. Vonk,Chen-Nee Chuah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开展跨语言（西、中、英、日）与跨任务（对话式 vs. 结构化）的声学分析，用统计、专家引导及模型驱动三类特征选择获得紧凑声学特征集，发现声学特征在任务与语言间不能均匀泛化：时间类特征稳健，语速强依赖任务，音高类特征呈语言特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言痴呆检测对可扩展、公平的筛查至关重要，自动语音分析提供非侵入式方案；但现有研究多依赖单一任务（如图片描述）与黑盒模型，忽视任务与语言的作用，缺乏系统性对照。

Method: 构建整合对话式数据与结构化语音任务的四语言（西班牙语、普通话、英语、日语）数据集，进行跨语言、跨任务的声学分析；采用统计方法、语言学家/临床医生引导的特征选择以及基于机器学习模型的特征选择，在保持分类性能的前提下导出紧凑声学特征集，从而在数据集与任务间系统比较与认知障碍相关的声学特征。

Result: 声学特征在任务与语言间未能均匀泛化：时间类特征在所有数据集上保持稳健，无论语言或任务均能稳定捕捉时间紊乱；语速呈明显任务依赖性，在有外部锚定内容与节奏的单说话人任务中信息量高，而在内容高度个性化的场景下可靠性下降；音高类特征呈语言特异模式，声调语言（如普通话）更偏好 chroma 类特征，非声调语言更偏好 F0 类特征。

Conclusion: 研究警示不应无差别地混合异质语音数据，强调多语言自动语音痴呆检测需采用可解释、情境感知（context-aware）的方法，并重视任务与语言对声学标记有效性的调节作用。

Abstract: Multilingual dementia detection is essential for scalable and equitable screening. In recent years, automated speech analysis has emerged as a promising approach for supporting dementia detection by enabling non-invasive and scalable assessment of cognitive function. However, existing studies that use automated speech analysis often rely on one type of task, such as picture description, and use black-box models. These studies often neglect the role of task and language. To address these gaps, we performed a cross-linguistic and cross-task acoustic analysis that integrates a conversational dataset with structured speech tasks across four languages (Spanish, Mandarin, English, Japanese). We applied statistical, linguist-/clinician-guided, and machine learning model-based feature selection to derive compact acoustic feature sets while maintaining classification performance. This enabled systematic comparison of cognitive impairment-relevant features across datasets and tasks. Our results revealed that acoustic features did not generalize uniformly across tasks and languages. Temporal features remained robust across all datasets, consistently capturing timing disruption regardless of language or task. In contrast, speech rate showed strong task dependence -- emerging as informative mainly in single-speaker tasks with externally anchored content and pacing, but becoming less reliable when speech content was highly individualized. Pitch-related features further exhibited language-specific patterns, favoring chroma-related features in tonal languages (e.g., Mandarin) and F0-related features in non-tonal languages. These findings caution against indiscriminate pooling of heterogeneous speech data and underscore the need for interpretable, context-aware approaches to multilingual dementia detection using automated speech analysis.

</details>


### [42] [Federated Soft Poly-Level Learning Over a Distributed Network with Guaranteed Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17722)
*Yang Jiao,Kai Yang,Jianwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出联邦多层学习（FPLL）框架，用新型L-cut逐层松弛求解四层及以上的软约束多层优化问题，并给出非渐近复杂度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层优化研究多局限于双层/三层，四层及以上的"多层学习（PLL）"尚少探索；且现有PLL工作多为非联邦设定，需将数据集中到中心服务器，带来隐私风险。

Method: 构建分布式网络上的联邦多层学习（FPLL）框架，针对带软约束的PLL问题，引入新型非线性割——L-cut实现对PLL问题的逐层松弛；所设计的联邦算法含可调参数，可在计算复杂度与性能效率间灵活权衡。

Result: 给出非渐近分析，推导FPLL达到ε-Pareto稳定点的迭代复杂度；大量实验验证了所提方法更优的有效性。

Conclusion: FPLL为隐私保护下的多层（≥4层）优化提供了可行方案，兼具理论复杂度保证与灵活的效率—复杂度折中能力。

Abstract: Multi-level learning/optimization, e.g., bilevel optimization, has been recognized for its versatile applicability across a broad array of domains, spanning from signal processing to machine learning. However, most existing works focus on bilevel and trilevel optimization. In contrast, multi-level optimization problems involving four or more hierarchical levels, which we term poly-level learning (PLL) problems, remain largely unexplored.In addition, in practical PLL, data may be distributed across multiple nodes over a distributed network. Nevertheless, extant studies on PLL predominantly focus on the non-federated setting, necessitating the collection of data to central servers and thereby posing potential privacy risks. To this end, we propose a Federated Poly-Level Learning (FPLL) framework over a distributed network to tackle the PLL problems with soft constraints in a federated manner. FPLL hinges on a novel non-linear cut, i.e., $L$-cut, for the level-wise relaxation to the PLL problems. Additionally, the proposed federated algorithm is designed with adjustable parameters that allow for a flexible trade-off between its computational complexity and performance efficiency, enabling users to tune it according to specific application requirements. We also carry out non-asymptotic analysis to derive the iteration complexity for the proposed FPLL to achieve the $ε$-Pareto stationary point. Extensive experiments have been undertaken to elucidate the superior efficacy of the proposed method.

</details>


### [43] [A Spectral Framework for Universal Proxy Regularization in Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17728)
*Shangge Liu,Danyue Qian,Qi Fan,Yinghuan Shi,Wenbin Li,Lei Wang,Yang Gao,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出谱框架（spectral coverage α 与 leakage β）刻画代理分布与其他任务分布的关系，导出统一的参数干扰上界，并比较三种代理正则化实现（J/F/O），证明其在 NTK 线性化下等价而在非线性微调下形成三级层次，并在 CLIP 上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可免去昂贵的联合训练，但存在参数干扰问题。现有缓解方法需使用其他待合并任务的数据来约束微调，违背了模型合并的核心独立性假设与隐私要求。最近有初步经验证据表明任务无关的通用代理分布可替代这类数据，但其何时、为何充分尚未得到解释。

Method: 提出通用代理正则化的谱框架，用谱覆盖 α 与谱泄漏 β 刻画代理分布与待合并任务分布的关系，据此导出统一的参数干扰界，表明较小的 (α, β) 通过更好的谱对齐可证明地降低干扰。将代理正则项加入训练目标，并研究三种实例化：J（基于 Jacobian-vector 乘积的精确评估）、F（基于 Fisher 信息矩阵的冻结曲率近似）、O（直接约束代理上函数偏移的输出漂移形式）。证明三者在 NTK 线性化下重合，并进一步刻画标准非线性微调下各自偏离线性化参考的程度，得到三级层次：J 为零偏离，F 与代理无关，O 随代理质量缩放。

Result: 在常用视觉-语言模型 CLIP 上的实证验证确认了上述理论预测，即三种正则化实例化在非线性微调下的偏离层次与代理质量依赖性符合分析。

Conclusion: 该谱框架为通用代理分布在模型合并中的有效性提供了理论解释，明确了代理质量（α, β）与参数干扰之间的关系，并为不同代理正则化机制的选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Model merging constructs multi-task models without expensive joint training but often suffers from parameter interference. Some solutions mitigate this by constraining finetuning using data from the other tasks to be merged, violating the core independence and privacy assumption of model merging. Recent work offers preliminary empirical evidence that a universal, task-agnostic proxy distribution can substitute for such data, but leaves open when and why it suffices. To this end, we propose a \textit{Spectral Framework for Universal Proxy Regularization}, which characterizes the relationship between a proxy distribution and the distribution of other tasks to be merged through spectral coverage ($α$) and leakage ($β$). Based on the framework, we derive a unified parameter interference bound: smaller $(α, β)$ provably yields lower parameter interference through better spectral alignment. To exploit such a proxy, we augment the training objective with a proxy regularizer. Evaluating this regularizer during fine-tuning requires a concrete mechanism. We study three instantiations, including (J) an exact evaluation via Jacobian-vector products, (F) a frozen curvature approximation using the Fisher information matrix, and (O) an output-drift formulation that constrains the model's functional deviation on the proxy directly. We prove that all three coincide under the NTK linearization, where existing analyses of such regularizers stop, and go further to characterize standard non-linear fine-tuning, bounding how far each instantiation departs from its linearized reference and obtain a three-way hierarchy, zero for (J), proxy-independent for (F), and proxy-quality-scaling for (O). Empirical validation on the commonly used vision-language model CLIP confirms these theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [44] [Knowledge After Collapse: Context and Wording Shape Factual Accessibility in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17763)
*Sujato Dutta,Prithviraj Patil,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出事件条件化评测协议KAD，在模型首次接受错误更正处冻结对话并分支探测，区分"初始误采纳"与"持续、恢复、拒答"，揭示单一致敏度分数无法刻画的对话历史依赖行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事实可靠性评测多用单一"易感度"分数衡量模型是否被错误更正带偏，但无法刻画模型在接受错误答案之后、随对话继续所表现出的持续坚持、恢复或拒答等行为差异，也难以为对话级干预（如历史管理）提供匹配对照。

Method: KAD协议在首次显式采纳指定错误目标处冻结对话，从该精确历史分支出独立探测，实现后采纳行为的匹配比较与历史管理干预评估；评测8个本地checkpoint条件与6个托管端点，并进行经审计的300题复跑，另设中性历史分支、匹配线索位置对照及12个独立撰写的施压模板。

Result: 校正后的Llama 3.2 Instruct 3B对照中，中性历史分支得42/46正确，完整历史仅16/46；Qwen 3.5 4B中匹配的线索位置对照使错误采纳变化达27.3个百分点；12个施压模板显示对措辞高度敏感。

Conclusion: KAD超越初始易感性，为事实可靠性评测提供可系统比较误答案持续性、恢复条件与对话干预的框架，表明后采纳行为强烈依赖对话历史结构而非单一易感度。

Abstract: When a language model accepts a false correction in a factual discussion, its next answer can depend on how the conversation continues. We introduce Knowledge Answer Divergence (KAD), an event-conditioned evaluation protocol that distinguishes initial false-answer adoption from subsequent persistence, recovery, and abstention. KAD freezes the conversation at the first explicit adoption of a designated false target and branches independent probes from that exact history. This enables matched comparisons of post-adoption behavior and history-management interventions that a single susceptibility score cannot capture. We evaluate eight local checkpoint conditions and six hosted endpoints, alongside an audited 300-question rerun. In corrected Llama 3.2 Instruct 3B controls, a neutral-history branch yields 42/46 correct responses, compared with 16/46 under full history. A matched cue-placement control changes false adoption by 27.3 percentage points in Qwen 3.5 4B. Twelve independently authored pressure templates demonstrate substantial sensitivity to wording. Together, these findings demonstrate the utility of KAD for evaluating factual reliability beyond initial susceptibility, enabling systematic comparisons of false-answer persistence, recovery conditions and conversational interventions.

</details>


### [45] [Certified Improvements to Best-Known Thomson-Problem Configurations with a Search Pipeline Built with Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17776)
*Hui Shao,Tao Wang,Xiaotian Liu,Yijie Peng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对Thomson问题N=912、1382、2502三个大规模算例，以LLM代理驱动的复制交换盆地跳跃搜索找到低于剑桥团簇数据库能量的构型，并强调了"抛光基线"比较与预注册控制实验等计算发现规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大N汤姆逊问题的最优构型主要收录于剑桥团簇数据库，长期难以改进；作者希望检验能否在保持原有缺陷/疤痕结构的前提下进一步降低库仑能量，并同时探索LLM代理参与计算发现的可行性与方法学风险。

Method: 从数据库构型出发运行无约束复制交换盆地跳跃，配合共享极小值池、按几何重标号后的改进区域重组以及仅用梯度的抛光；用精确整数算术进行候选验证，并以抛光后的数据库能量（抛光基线）作为比较基准；全流程由LLM代理（Claude聊天界面与Claude Code代理）构建与执行，并完整记录全部搜索（含失败）。另做局部松弛加速方案的对照实验。

Result: 得到N=912、1382、2502的能量分别比数据库低0.0036186、0.0109730、0.0614033，均经精确整数运算认证；三者均为模旋转意义下的严格局部极小，且缺陷清单与疤痕组成与数据库构型一致。单靠复制交换盆地跳跃即找到N=912与1382（前者五轮中仅一轮成功）。仅抛光即可使两个条目降幅超过候选报告阈值，导致两条伪记录，直到引入抛光基线才消除；某局部松弛加速虽通过精确性测试，但在控制对比中未带来收益——所有采用完整松弛的对照分支均在15分钟内低于数据库值。

Conclusion: 提出三条LLM代理计算发现实践：搜索代码需标定、候选须由独立代码验证；候选必须与抛光基线比较而非与数据库原值比较；设计改动须在预先固定通过判据的控制对比中检验。同时公开全部分失败搜索以增强可复现性。

Abstract: The Thomson problem asks for N unit charges on a sphere with minimum Coulomb energy; for large N the best configurations known are curated in the Cambridge Cluster Database, whose largest-N entries were set in October 2026. We report configurations for N = 912, 1382 and 2502 with energies 0.0036186, 0.0109730 and 0.0614033 below the database values, certified by exact integer arithmetic. All three are numerically strict local minima modulo rotations and keep the defect inventory and scar compositions of the database configurations; the database value for N = 912 had been reached by two different methods. Our search runs unconstrained replica-exchange basin hopping from the database configurations, with a shared pool of minima, recombination of improved regions after relabelling by geometry, and gradient-only polishing; replica-exchange basin hopping alone found the N = 912 and 1382 configurations, the former in one of five runs. We built and ran it with large language model agents (Claude, through a chat interface and the Claude Code agent), and we report every search, including those that failed. Two findings matter for others who search the database. Polishing alone lowers two entries by more than the threshold at which our pipeline reports a candidate, which produced two spurious records until candidates were compared with the polished database energies (the polished baseline). A local-relaxation speed-up passed its exactness test yet found nothing in a controlled comparison in which every arm with full relaxation went below the database value within 15 minutes. We distil three practices for computational discovery with large language model agents: calibrate the search code and verify candidates with code separate from the search, compare candidates with the polished baseline, and test design changes in controlled comparisons with pass criteria fixed in advance.

</details>


### [46] [TReC: Evaluating Move-Induced State Changes in Instruction-Conditioned Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17781)
*Zhuangsheng Li,Yunhan Zhang,Dailin Li,Qingxian Yu,Yu Wu,Youqi Song,Bangyuan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Transition Representation Consistency (TReC)：将预测走子与专家走子在同一局面下分别执行，取两个后继状态表征变化的余弦相似度，作为超越坐标距离的"走子等价性"度量；在冻结 KataGo 上 TReC 区分度远高于直接后继状态余弦，且与坐标距离仅弱相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型预测与专家着法不同时，坐标距离只能衡量落点远近，无法反映两种着法对局面本质影响的差异；需要一个刻画"走子所引起状态变化是否一致"的度量。

Method: 定义 TReC：从同一局面出发按规则分别执行候选着法与参考（专家）着法，得到两个后继表征，计算二者表征变化向量的余弦相似度；实验中冻结 KataGo 网络，在五局共 680 个中后盘局面上的多个评估层上计算，并与直接后继状态余弦及坐标距离做比较（含局面内秩相关分析）。

Result: 每个局面内候选间最大最小差距中位数：直接后继状态余弦为 0.01–0.08（随评估层变化），TReC 为 0.88–1.33；TReC 与坐标距离仅弱相关，局面内秩相关中位数为 −0.19。

Conclusion: TReC 具有更强的候选区分度，且捕捉到坐标距离无法表达的信息，可作为评估围棋模型着法一致性的补充指标。

Abstract: When a Go model predicts a move different from the expert's, coordinate distance measures how far the prediction lands from the reference. We introduce Transition Representation Consistency (TReC) to compare how the two moves change a board-state embedding. Both moves are executed from the same position under the game rules, and TReC is the cosine similarity between the two resulting representation changes. In experiments with a frozen KataGo network on 680 mid-to-late-game positions from five games, the gap between the highest and lowest candidate per position has a median of 0.01 to 0.08 for the direct successor-state cosine and 0.88 to 1.33 for TReC, depending on the evaluator layer. TReC follows coordinate distance only weakly: the rank correlation between a candidate's distance to the reference and its TReC, computed within each position, has a median of -0.19 over these positions.

</details>


### [47] [AutoRL: A Multi-Agent System towards Autonomous Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17790)
*Simon Tang,Wenjie Shi,Yongjing Yin,Keheng Wang,Jialin Liu,Zheng Zhang,Linvo Lin,Xiangyuan Xue,Kan Ren,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体系统 AutoRL，用于自动化强化学习训练流程，模仿 RL 研究者的实际工作方式做过程级监督与在线干预，为递归自我改进（RSI）奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RSI 被视作通向超级智能的路径，需 AI 自主完成完整训练流水线；RL 在迭代提升能力与泛化性中不可或缺，但自动化的 RL 受训练不稳定和持续监督需求所困。现有做法（端到端跑完整实验）与科研实际流程不符。

Method: AutoRL 包含三类 RL 专用智能体角色：Perceptors 监控训练动态、rollout、评估与效率，在异常、退化或停滞时触发审议；三个互补的 Proposers 基于共享证据与校验诊断问题并迭代候选干预方案；Orchestrator 复核建议并通过在线热更新或基于 checkpoint 的恢复来实施变更。

Result: 在 Qwen3 与 LongCat-Flash-Lite 上，AutoRL 优于静态 RL 配比和通用智能体；研究记录表明现有智能体可胜任无人值守的 RL 监督与动态调整。

Conclusion: RSI 的未来难点在于发展对资源密集型验证有价值的基础方法；AutoRL 证明过程级自动监督可行，是通向 RSI 的一块基石。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) is one of the most promising paths toward an AI singularity for superintelligence. Achieving this vision requires AI systems to undertake the full training pipeline for iterative improvement. Reinforcement learning (RL) plays an essential role in this cycle to improve model capabilities and generalization, yet automating RL remains difficult due to unstable training dynamics and the need for sustained supervision. We introduce AutoRL, a multi-agent system for automating RL training, as a cornerstone for future RSI. Different from conducting research by end-to-end complete experiments, AutoRL monitors the evolving training state and integrates process-level supervision and research into an ongoing RL training, following the practical workflow of an RL researcher. Specifically, AutoRL consists of three RL-specific agentic roles. Perceptors track training dynamics, rollouts, evaluation, and efficiency, triggering deliberation when they detect anomalies, degradation, or stagnation. Three complementary Proposers diagnose problems and refine candidate interventions through shared evidence and checks. The Orchestrator reviews their recommendation and applies approved changes through online hot updates or checkpoint-based resumption. Experiments on Qwen3 and LongCat-Flash-Lite demonstrate the superiority of AutoRL over static RL recipes and a general agent. The research records show that existing agents can handle unattended RL supervision and dynamic adjustment. The difficulty for future RSI lies in developing fundamental methods that are valuable for resource-consuming validation.

</details>


### [48] [MASTARS: Multi-Agent Sequential Trajectory Augmentation with Return-Conditioned Subgoals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17793)
*Jiwon Jeon,Myungsik Cho,Woojun Kim,Seongmin Kim,Woohyeon Byeon,Seungyul Han,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASTARS 是一个基于扩散模型的离线多智能体轨迹增强框架，通过按智能体顺序生成并借助 inpainting 强制跨智能体协调一致，兼顾多样性、协调性与全局一致性，在 MPE、SMAC、SMAC-v2 上显著提升多种离线 MARL 方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线 MARL 性能受限于固定离线数据集质量，因此需要轨迹增强。但多智能体轨迹增强存在"多样性—协调性"权衡：独立生成各智能体可提升多样性却缺乏协调；联合生成能保证协调却多样性不足、只是复现训练分布。

Method: 提出 MASTARS 扩散框架：按智能体顺序（sequentially）生成多智能体轨迹，并在生成过程中利用 inpainting 机制强制跨智能体一致协调，从而产生多样、协调且全局一致的轨迹。

Result: 在 MPE、SMAC、SMAC-v2 等基准上，MASTARS 为多种离线 MARL 方法带来显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 顺序生成 + inpainting 协调约束有效缓解多样性—协调性权衡，是一种有效的多智能体数据增强方案，可即插即用地提升现有离线 MARL 方法。

Abstract: The performance of offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) is limited by the quality of the fixed offline dataset, motivating trajectory augmentation. However, augmenting multi-agent trajectories presents a fundamental challenge of the diversity-coordination trade-off: independent generation improves diversity but lacks coordination, while joint generation induces coordination but lacks diversity, reproducing the training distribution. To address this challenge in multi-agent data augmentation, we introduce MASTARS, a novel diffusion-based framework that generates multi-agent trajectories sequentially across agents while enforcing cross-agent consistent coordination via the method of inpainting. MASTARS produces trajectories that are diverse, coordinated and globally coherent. Experiments on various benchmarks such as MPE, SMAC, and SMAC-v2 demonstrate significant performance gains across multiple offline MARL methods.

</details>


### [49] [Robustness Evaluation of GPU Cloud Pricing Benchmarks under Incomplete Observations: A Controlled Missingness Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17796)
*Jooya Wong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究报告了GPU日租价格基准在数据缺失下的稳健性压力测试：全源停机使发布覆盖率降至0.77但误差为零，改变构成的缺失保持0.86覆盖率却带来7.74个对数点误差；仅用历史信息的层级消融在5种改变构成情形下可减误差4.15–6.70个对数点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市场报价缺失会使等权中位数的日度GPU租赁价格基准失效，作者希望系统评估固定基准规则在多种缺失机制下的表现，并检验补救方法（基准层恢复、层级信息消融、信号扣留规则）的有效性。

Method: 对固定的等权中位数规则进行压力测试：设定4种掩蔽机制与4种基准层恢复方法，在27个情景、190个留出日上共5,800次蒙特卡洛模拟；另设仅使用历史信息的层级（stratum-level）消融，以及基于可观测信号的扣留规则，以误差-覆盖率准则评估。

Result: 全源停机仅将发布覆盖率降至0.77，且发布日条件误差为零；改变构成的缺失使覆盖率保持0.86，但产生7.74个对数点误差；past-only层级消融在5个改变构成案例中降低误差4.15–6.70个对数点；可观测信号扣留规则在10个案例中0个改善主要误差-覆盖率准则。

Conclusion: 基准的误差与覆盖率之间存在明显权衡，缺失机制类型决定失效模式；引入更丰富的历史层级信息能有效纠偏，而简单的信号扣留策略无效。结论限于观测到的报价测量，不涉及成交价格或真实世界的缺失机制识别。

Abstract: Daily GPU rental-price benchmarks can fail when marketplace quotes are missing. This article stress-tests a fixed equal-weight median rule under four imposed masking mechanisms and four benchmark-level recovery methods. Across 5,800 Monte Carlo realisations in 27 scenarios on 190 held-out days, whole-source outages reduce publication coverage to 0.77 while preserving zero conditional error on published dates; composition-changing loss retains 0.86 coverage but produces 7.74 log points of error. A separate past-only stratum-level ablation reduces error by 4.15 to 6.70 log points in five composition-changing cases, using a richer information set. An observable-signal withholding rule improves the primary error-coverage criterion in 0 of 10 cases. Results concern observed asking-price measurements, not transaction prices or identified real-world missingness mechanisms.

</details>


### [50] [Vision-as-a-Tool: Visual Harness for Multimodal Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17797)
*Haolin Yang,Wenshan Wu,Jin Jiang,Yu Cheng,Yan Xia,Ting Song,Yangyu Huang,Zhiliang Peng,Wenhui Wang,Hangbo Bao,Yuzhe Zhang,Wen-Da Wei,Tengchao Lv,Li Dong,Lei Cui,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Vision-as-a-Tool (VaaT) 范式，将通用多模态大模型封装为可调用的外部视觉工具，并在 VHarness 框架中以五种原子动作实现，使纯文本智能体可完全卸载视觉推理、多模态智能体可按需增强感知；在 Image-to-HTML/SVG/3D 任务上一致提升视觉保真度与几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体编码需强视觉推理能力，但现有 Thinking-with-Images 范式将视觉能力内嵌于智能体自身，性能受内部感知上限严格约束；同时纯文本智能体缺乏原生感知，无专用工具便无法进行视觉推理。

Method: 提出 VaaT 范式：与以往仅用视觉工具操作图像、再由智能体自身视觉读取不同，VaaT 将通用 MLLM 重构为可调用的外部工具，实现视觉推理的外包或按需增补；据此构建统一框架 VHarness，由五种原子动作驱动。

Result: 在 Image-to-HTML、Image-to-SVG、Image-to-3D 三类生成任务上评测，VHarness 对纯文本与多模态编码智能体均一致提升视觉保真度与几何精度。

Conclusion: 将视觉推理模块化为可调用工具，为未来智能体编码系统提供了灵活、可扩展的基础架构。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic coding requires robust visual reasoning to analyze observations and evaluate generated artifacts. Current frameworks largely rely on a Thinking-with-Images paradigm, where agents depend on their intrinsic vision to execute visually grounded tasks. However, this architectural coupling introduces a fundamental bottleneck. Agent performance remains strictly bounded by internal perceptual limitations. Furthermore, text-only agents lack native perception. Without specialized tools, they cannot perform visual reasoning. To overcome these constraints, we introduce the Vision-as-a-Tool (VaaT) paradigm. Prior visual tool-use merely manipulates images for an agent's intrinsic vision to read. In contrast, VaaT reframes general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) as callable external utilities. As a result, text-only agents can completely offload visual reasoning during agentic execution. Meanwhile, vision-capable agents can dynamically augment their native perception on demand. We instantiate this paradigm in VHarness, a unified framework driven by five atomic actions. We comprehensively evaluate VHarness across Image-to-HTML, Image-to-SVG, and Image-to-3D generation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that VHarness consistently improves visual fidelity and geometric accuracy across both text-only and multimodal coding agents. These findings highlight that modularizing visual reasoning into callable utilities provides a flexible and scalable foundation for future agentic coding systems.

</details>


### [51] [Can Agents Keep Up with Time? An Empirical Analysis of Temporal Adaptation After a Conversational Hiatus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17806)
*Hongguan Su,Ming Wang,Shun Zhang,Yifan Wang,Rong Yin,Zhiming Ma,Mengzheng Yang,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"时间状态感知与使用"(temporal state perception and use)能力，设计session-rekindling任务，发现长程智能体能获取当前时间却常在解读带日期记录时不会使用它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程个人助理在长时间对话中断后重启时，早期预约与准备笔记仍存于记录中，但计划日期可能已过去且结果未记录。现有评估只关注事实保持与显式状态更新，智能体是否能感知时间流逝并相应调整建议尚属未知。

Method: 将能力定义为temporal state perception and use（感知当前时间并用于解读带日期记录）。设计session-rekindling任务：固定早期对话，仅变化后续日期与时间信息传递给智能体的方式；在5种模型路线与5种时间信息条件下进行实验。

Result: 智能体能获取当前时间但常无法使用：可靠地查询时钟、甚至在推理中记录日期已过，却仍让用户为已过期事件做准备。提供显式时间上下文可降低该错误，直接给出计算好的日期关系可完全消除错误。

Conclusion: 跟上时间流逝取决于把感知到的时间转化为更新后的计划。据此设计session-rekindling基准，为长程智能体设计提供时间意识指导。

Abstract: Long-horizon agents increasingly serve as persistent personal assistants across modern web platforms, managing schedules, applications, and multi-step plans over weeks or months. Users often return to an ongoing conversation after a long conversational hiatus, while earlier bookings and preparation notes stay on record. During this hiatus, scheduled dates may pass even though the retained records remain unchanged and event outcomes go unrecorded. Existing evaluations of long-horizon agents focus on fact retention and explicit state updates. Whether agents perceive the passage of time and adapt their advice accordingly therefore remains unknown. We frame this ability as temporal state perception and use, which means perceiving the current time and applying it when interpreting dated records. To study it, we design session-rekindling tasks that keep the earlier conversation fixed and vary only the follow-up date and the way temporal information reaches the agent. Experiments across five model routes and five temporal-information conditions show that agents can obtain the current time but often fail to use it. They reliably query the clock and even note in their reasoning that the scheduled date has passed, yet many still tell users to prepare for the expired event. Supplying explicit temporal context reduces this error, and providing the computed date relation removes it entirely. Keeping up with time therefore depends on turning perceived time into updated plans. Based on these findings, we design a session-rekindling benchmark that provides temporal-awareness guidance for designing long-horizon agents. The code can be found at https://github.com/Adoreu2/session-rekindling-benchmark.

</details>


### [52] [G-CAST: Governed Continual Self-Training for E-Commerce Agents under Evolving Demands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17814)
*Yuechun Yu,Wenzheng Kong,Xiaofeng Mao,Jin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出G-CAST——面向电商对话式购物智能体的受控持续自训练系统，将每日生产证据转化为按奖励路由、按熵优先的训练数据并治理奖励演化，通过累计评估下的受控检查点切换实现持续适配，已在1688元采B2B采购智能体上线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在电商导购中广泛应用，但用户需求持续漂移（新品类、采购场景、功能诉求）且产品的执行框架（工具、技能、操作流程）不断演进，带来两大挑战：需求漂移使一次性训练的模型落后于分布、意图与复杂度变化；框架演进要求模型用上新能力同时不遗忘用户既有依赖的行为。周期性人工重训延迟高、成本大。

Method: G-CAST将每日生产证据（在演化中的需求与能力契约下）转化为两类资产：①奖励路由、熵优先化的训练数据编排；②受控的奖励演化。每次模型更新由"累计评估下的受控检查点切换"把关。

Result: 在真实生产电商智能体上的实验表明，G-CAST相比强自主后训练基线，对新引入需求适配更强，且更好保留先前已接受的行为；消融显示奖励路由+熵优先数据编排与受控奖励演化两个组件对增益均重要。

Conclusion: G-CAST已部署于阿里巴巴1688平台的B2B采购智能体元采（cai.1688.com），验证了受控持续自训练在生产环境中的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs), an increasing number of users are turning to LLM-powered agents for conversational shopping in e-commerce. However, the user needs continue to change as new product categories, procurement scenarios, and feature requests emerge. At the same time, the product's execution harness-including its tools, skills, and operating procedures-continues to evolve to support increasingly sophisticated interactions. These changes create two practical challenges: 1) Demand drift alters the distribution, intent, and complexity of user requests, causing a model trained once to fall behind; 2) Harness evolution requires the model to use newly introduced capabilities correctly without losing behavior that users already rely on. Periodic manual retraining is too delayed and labor-intensive for this setting. To address these challenges, we present G-CAST, a governed continual self-training system that converts daily production evidence under evolving demand and capability contracts into reward-routed, entropy-prioritized training data and governed reward evolution. Governed checkpoint transition under cumulative evaluation controls each model update, enabling the agent to learn newly introduced behaviors without sacrificing previously accepted capabilities. Experiments on a real production e-commerce agent show that G-CAST achieves stronger adaptation to newly introduced demand while better preserving previously accepted behavior compared with a strong autonomous post-training baseline. Ablations further show that both reward-routed, entropy-prioritized data orchestration and governed reward evolution are important to these gains. G-CAST is now deployed in Yuancai(http://cai.1688.com/), a B2B procurement agent on Alibaba's 1688 platform.

</details>


### [53] [Causally Constrained and Uncertainty-Aware Cardiopulmonary Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17819)
*Yuxiang Lai,Jiashuo Zhang,Liang Zhao,Xiaofeng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出两阶段框架 CE-Agent：先用同行评审的流行病学证据将风险因子权重约束为显式先验，再从队列数据估计局部基线风险与CT表型；部署时用闭式不确定性传播在毫秒级处理缺失输入，实现肺癌与心血管疾病的联合风险分层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规胸部CT可同时捕获肺、心、血管表型，可联合分层肺癌与心血管疾病（肺筛查人群两大死因），但现有临床风险计算器逐病孤立评估且忽略CT发现；无约束机器学习则在小样本医院队列上过拟合、学到受治疗与病情混杂的风险方向，并在记录不完整时隐瞒不确定性。

Method: CE-Agent 两阶段框架：（1）在见到任何结局之前，将同行评审流行病学证据编译为对风险因子权重的显式约束，同时从队列数据估计局部基线风险与新增CT表型；（2）部署阶段采用闭式不确定性传播，在毫秒级内处理缺失的临床或CT输入，速度较蒙特卡洛采样快两个数量级以上，输出校准的风险区间以指导病历复核与选择性影像检查。

Result: 在来自33家美国医院的53,452名肺筛查受试者及6,814名多族裔成人中验证：CE-Agent在肺筛查中优于心血管计算器，在硬性动脉粥样硬化事件上超过汇总队列方程（PCE）并纠正其1.7倍风险高估，可从单家医院或单一CT扫描仪泛化至外部站点，所需样本量最多降低五倍；原样应用于肺癌时与既有筛查模型相当且校准准确（仅用风险因子方向即可），联合两类预测可改善单次筛查就诊的心肺联合分诊。

Conclusion: 以证据约束的先验+数据驱动的局部估计+闭式不确定性传播，可在小样本、缺失数据与分布偏移条件下实现准确校准、可泛化、可解释的肺癌与心血管疾病联合风险分层，支撑单次胸部CT筛查中的心肺一体化分诊。

Abstract: Routine chest computed tomography (CT) captures pulmonary, cardiac, and vascular phenotypes that jointly stratify lung cancer and cardiovascular disease, the two leading causes of death in lung-screening populations. Yet clinical risk calculators evaluate each disease in isolation and omit CT findings, whereas unconstrained machine learning overfits small hospital cohorts, learns treatment- and illness-confounded risk directions, and conceals uncertainty when patient records are incomplete. Here we present the Causal Envelope Agent (CE-Agent), a two-stage framework that compiles peer-reviewed epidemiological evidence into explicit constraints on risk-factor weights before any outcome is seen, while estimating local baseline risk and newly added CT phenotypes from cohort data. At deployment, closed-form uncertainty propagation accounts for missing clinical or CT inputs within milliseconds per patient, running over two orders of magnitude faster than Monte Carlo sampling and returning calibrated risk intervals that direct chart review and selective imaging to borderline patients. Across 53,452 lung-screening participants from 33 US hospitals and 6,814 multi-ethnic adults, CE-Agent outperforms cardiovascular calculators in lung screening, exceeds the Pooled Cohort Equations on hard atherosclerotic events while correcting their 1.7-fold risk overestimation, and generalizes from a single hospital or CT scanner to external sites while lowering required sample sizes up to fivefold. Applied unchanged to lung cancer, CE-Agent matches established screening models with accurate calibration even when restricted to risk-factor directions alone, and pairing both predictions improves joint cardiopulmonary triage from a single screening visit.

</details>


### [54] [Aligning AI Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17820)
*Siddarth Srinivasan,Morgan Jane Matthews,Jascha Sohl-Dickstein,Erik Jones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 单智能体对齐训练无法可靠迁移到多智能体团队，其主因是责任分散；设置专职安全负责人或发布安全规范文档可恢复对齐行为，并开源magelab框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，针对单个大模型智能体的对齐训练难以迁移到多智能体场景。本文旨在揭示该失败背后的机制，并探索能保持对齐向多智能体团队迁移的干预手段。

Method: 在两类软件工程任务（脓毒症分诊、新闻推荐）与十项咨询方案生成任务上进行实验；对比单智能体与智能体团队的安全行为差距，检验模型能力、自然干预（互相批评、群聊协作）等影响，并做机制归因（责任分散）与干预设计（指定安全负责人、由协调者发布安全规范文档）。

Result: 四项发现：(1) 单智能体与团队间的安全差距随模型能力提升无明确趋势，同一模型在不同任务上差距可最大也可最小；(2) 互相批评、群聊协调等自然干预无法可靠弥合差距；(3) 责任分散显著解释安全差距——团队智能体更少主动检查系统级安全失效，更倾向忽视、合理化或推卸责任；(4) 指定安全负责人保障系统级安全、由协调者发布安全规范文档，可有效恢复单智能体水平的对齐。

Conclusion: 多智能体对齐差距主要源于责任分散而非个体能力，需通过明确的安全角色与规范文档等结构性机制加以约束；作者开源magelab多智能体编排与实验框架，为设计可保持对齐行为的智能体团队提供实践指引。

Abstract: Previous work has shown that alignment training on individual LLM agents can fail to transfer to multi-agent settings. We investigate the underlying mechanisms for this failure, and explore interventions to preserve alignment transfer to multi-agent LLM teams. We focus on two software-engineering tasks (sepsis triage, a news recommender) and ten consulting-proposal generation tasks. We present four findings: (1) The single-vs-team safety gap does not show a clear trend with more capable models. We find that Anthropic's Mythos Preview shows the largest gap on one of our tasks but the smallest on another; (2) Many natural interventions, such as having agents critique each other's work or coordinate in a group chat, do not reliably bring agent teams back to single-agent alignment behavior; (3) diffusion of responsibility substantially accounts for the safety gap: agents in teams are less likely to proactively check for system-level safety failures and more likely to ignore, rationalize, or deflect responsibility for issues when they arise; (4) having a designated safety lead agent ensure system-level safety, and having a coordinator agent publish a safety specifications document are effective at restoring single-agent alignment. We also release magelab, a multi-agent LLM orchestration and experimentation framework. Our findings can help practitioners design agent teams that successfully preserve the behaviors expected of aligned individual agents.

</details>


### [55] [Is Abliteration Getting Harder? Not Uniformly: Refusal Removal by Direction-Based Ablation and ARA in Heretic on the Qwen3.8, Gemma 4, Olmo 3.1 and Mistral Small 3.2 LLMs Versus Their Predecessors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17846)
*Richard J. Young*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对5组新旧模型配对，用3–4种方向型消融方法与ARA在同一提示、搜索预算、KL散度上限和选择规则下进行对比，检验"方向型消融在新模型上更难移除拒答"这一说法。结果显示拒答移除难度并非随代际一致上升，而取决于模型配对、方法与预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARA开发者声称基于方向的方向型消融（ablation）在新版开源权重LLM上日益失效，但该结论缺乏多模型、多方法、同条件的系统检验。

Method: 在Heretic 2.0中对五组新旧配对（Qwen、Gemma、OLMo、Mistral等）实施差均值、MPOA、SOM（小模型对另加J-space）等方向型方法与ARA；统一prompt、名义搜索预算、首token KL散度上限与选择规则；以WildGuard自动评判在972条留出有害提示（JailbreakBench、XSTest、StrongREJECT、HarmBench及自建恶意代码库）上统计仍被拒答的比例；并用随机运行方差模型（I²、尺度-方差模型）与关键词计数审计进行稳健性检验。

Result: 方向型消融后新版本多保留−1至+37个百分点的拒答，ARA后为−26至+34；加入运行间方差后，方向型效应的配对间异质性I²为20–80%。ARA在Gemma与OLMo上损失较小、在Qwen上损失较大；Gemma中方向型的增量主要来自恶意代码库，在公开集上反转。关键词计数在该工具窗口处平均未低估评判拒答，但每模型平均相差7个百分点。各结论均基于单次ARA运行，部分随散度预算或方差模型改变。

Conclusion: 拒答移除难度不由模型代际单独决定，而取决于发布配对、方法与预算；仅审计单一配对、单一方法或单一提示源可能得出相反结论。

Abstract: Abliteration, a fine-tuning-free weight edit, removes refusal from open-weight large language models (LLMs). The developer of Arbitrary-Rank Ablation (ARA), an optimisation-based variant, reports that direction-based abliteration, which ablates a refusal direction, increasingly fails on newer models. On five release pairs, refusal removal did not become uniformly harder. Qwen2.5-32B and Qwen3.8-27B, Gemma-2-27B and Gemma-4-31B, OLMo-2-32B and Olmo-3.1-32B, Mistral-Small-2409 and Mistral-Small-3.2, and Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3.5-4B were edited in Heretic 2.0 by three or four direction-based methods (difference-in-means, MPOA, SOM and, on the small pair, J-space) and ARA under the same prompts, nominal search budget, first-token KL divergence cap and selection rule; removal counted as harder when the automatic WildGuard judge found more of 972 held-out harmful prompts (JailbreakBench, XSTest, StrongREJECT, HarmBench and an author-compiled malicious-code bank) still refused. After direction-based removal, newer releases retained -1 to +37 percentage points more refusal, and after ARA -26 to +34. With run-to-run variance included, between-pair heterogeneity of the direction-based effect ranged from high to low with the scale and variance model (I^2 20-80%). ARA lost less effectiveness than direction-based methods in Gemma and OLMo and more in Qwen; each verdict rests on one ARA run, and some changed with the divergence budget or variance model. In Gemma the direction-based increase came from the malicious-code bank and reversed on public sets. Contrary to prediction, keyword counts at the tool's window did not undercount judged refusal on average, but differed by a mean 7 points per model. Audits of a single pair, method or prompt source can reach opposite conclusions. Whether a newer model resisted removal depended on the release pair, method and budget, not on generation alone.

</details>


### [56] [DECODE: Diagnosing Interpretability Failures in Automated Interpretation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17856)
*Dohyun Kim,Jaeung Lee,Donghee Hong,Jaemin Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出视觉分析系统 DECODE，将人工监督重新引入 LLM 自解释流程，通过三阶段诊断工作流与解释间共识代理可靠性，在 16,384 个 SAE 特征上实现了大规模可解释性失败的高效定位与归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 内部表征解释工具日益依赖 LLM 自身生成并评估解释，形成近乎无人工监督的自指式自动化流程；由于缺乏 ground truth，难以验证解释，也难以判定可解释性失败的来源（是工具提取的表征有缺陷，还是流程中所用 LLM 的问题）。

Method: DECODE 视觉分析系统：1）将诊断工作流分解为三个递进阶段，逐层隔离故障源，从结构缺陷到解释层面的错位；2）借鉴定性分析中的编码者间共识（inter-coder consensus），以独立生成的解释及其评估指标之间的一致性作为可靠性代理；3）设计三阶段主动播种（active seeding）协议，推荐高优先级实例以供人工复核，从而扩展人工检查规模。

Result: 在 16,384 个稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征上进行案例研究，表明该方法能够在大规模下高效识别并归因可解释性失败。

Conclusion: 通过将人工监督与共识代理机制结合，DECODE 缓解了自指式自动解释流程的可靠性问题，使解释失败可被系统化定位与溯源，为 LLM 可解释性工具的可靠评估提供了可扩展的分析范式。

Abstract: Tools for interpreting the internal representations of Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly rely on LLMs themselves to both generate and evaluate explanations, creating a self-referential automated interpretation process with minimal human oversight. This automation raises significant reliability concerns, which are exacerbated by the absence of ground truth. In practice, it is difficult to validate explanations or to attribute interpretability failures to their source -- whether they originate from the tool's extracted representations or from the LLM used in the process. To address these challenges, we present DECODE, a visual analytics system that reintroduces human oversight into the automated interpretation process. Our approach decomposes the diagnostic workflow into three progressive stages, isolating failure sources ranging from structural defects to explanation-level misalignments. Drawing inspiration from inter-coder consensus in qualitative analysis, we utilize consensus among independently generated explanations and their evaluation metrics as a proxy for reliability. To further scale human inspection, we employ a three-phase active seeding protocol that strategically recommends high-priority instances for manual review. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through a case study on 16,384 Sparse Autoencoder features, showing that it can efficiently identify and attribute interpretability failures at scale.

</details>


### [57] [AI-aided Decision-Making in Education - Uncertainty, Explainability, and Human Responsibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17858)
*Fabian Kieser,Paul Tschisgale,Peter Wulff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该章节提出不确定性、可解释性与人类责任三项原则，作为在教育场景中合法、审慎使用AI的基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI已深度融入教育（个性化学习、自动化评估、行政辅助），但其决策过程（尤其深度学习与生成式模型）不透明，当结果显得不公正或错误时会阻碍决策，因此亟需厘清如何合理使用AI。

Method: 概念性论述：以三项原则为分析框架——承认AI决策与人类决策一样具有内在不确定性；要求可解释性以保证AI应用的功能与结果对教育者和学习者透明可理解；强调人类责任在AI开发与部署各阶段不可或缺。

Result: 通过逐条阐释三项原则，为利益相关者提供整合AI的综合性指导框架（原文为观点性章节，未报告实证结果）。

Conclusion: 不确定性、可解释性与人类责任共同构成教育中AI合法使用的必要条件，可指导教育系统审慎而有效地引入AI。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based systems have become increasingly integrated into various aspects of everyday life, including education, understanding how these systems make decisions is of paramount importance. AI holds significant potential to enhance educational practices through the facilitation of personalized and adaptive learning, automation of assessment procedures, and assistance with administrative tasks. However, the opacity of AI-based decision-making in education (especially with deep learning and generative AI models) can hamper decision-making, especially when outcomes appear unjustified or incorrect. In this chapter, we argue that three key principles (uncertainty, explainability, and human responsibility) are essential for the legitimate use of AI in educational settings. Acknowledging the inherent uncertainties in AI-based decisions, analogous to those in human decision-making, is fundamental. Explainability is critical for ensuring that the functioning and outcomes of AI applications are transparent and comprehensible to both educators and learners. Furthermore, human responsibility is crucial, particularly in human decision-making, which remains indispensable across all stages of AI development and deployment. By examining these three principles, this chapter provides a comprehensive guide for stakeholders seeking to integrate AI thoughtfully and effectively into the educational system.

</details>


### [58] [FlexCast: Flexible Context Learning for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17862)
*Zuyang Shen,Luqi Gong,Fanda Fan,Bingsong Wang,Xiang Ao,Yahui Wei,Minghao Liu,Chunjie Luo,Jianfeng Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FlexCast框架，将时间序列基础模型中纠缠的"上下文条件化"与"预测单元分解+自回归推演"两个因素解耦，用变长掩码预测替代自回归解码，在BLAST及多个零样本基准上达到与强基线相当或更优的精度且显著降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自回归下一token预测的时间序列基础模型将两类因素纠缠在一起：变量上下文条件化，以及预测单元分解配合自回归推演。因此强AR性能无法说明泛化究竟来自灵活的训练分布、局部预测目标、从左到右的推理过程，还是三者的组合，缺乏可控的归因分析。

Method: 将预测重构为时间序列patch上的变长掩码预测：预训练时动态采样可见上下文与掩码目标patch，使模型接触多样的上下文—目标配置且无需序列化解码；推理时同一预训练模型通过统一的mask-and-predict接口支持单步预测、基于置信度的迭代精化以及next-token式生成，从而在相同架构、数据与目标下可控比较不同推理调度。

Result: 在BLAST与多个零样本基准上，FlexCast精度与强TSFM基线相当或更优，同时大幅降低推理延迟。消融显示上下文多样性与连续目标掩码是性能驱动因素；一旦学到灵活的上下文—目标关系，从左到右解码不再带来一致增益。

Conclusion: AR式TSFM的关键收益在于灵活的训练方式而非顺序推理，解耦框架为TSFM泛化来源提供了可控的归因证据。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) based on autoregressive (AR) next-token prediction entangle two factors: variable-context conditioning and prediction-unit factorization with autoregressive rollout. Strong AR performance therefore does not reveal whether generalization stems from the flexible training distribution, the local prediction target, the left-to-right inference process, or their combination. We propose FlexCast, a flexible context-target learning framework that disentangles these factors. FlexCast reformulates forecasting as variable-length masked prediction over time series patches: during pretraining, it dynamically samples visible context and masked target patches, exposing the model to diverse context-target configurations without sequential decoding. At inference time, the same pretrained model supports one-step forecasting, confidence-based iterative refinement, and next-token-style generation through a unified mask-and-predict interface, enabling controlled comparison of inference schedules under identical architecture, data, and objective. Evaluated on BLAST and multiple zero-shot benchmarks, FlexCast achieves accuracy competitive with or better than strong TSFM baselines while substantially reducing inference latency. Ablations confirm that context diversity and contiguous target masking drive performance, while left-to-right decoding does not yield consistent gains once flexible context-target relationships have been learned, suggesting that the key benefit of AR-style TSFMs lies in flexible training rather than sequential inference. The code is available at https://github.com/BenchCouncil/FlexCast.

</details>


### [59] [OmniScore-Bench: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Musical Score Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17870)
*Congren Dai,Zhancheng Guo,Danni Zhao,Wenchu Wang,Xiaobing Li,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 OmniScore-Bench，用3000道题、6个方面、24类子任务系统评测视觉语言模型的乐谱识别能力，覆盖从符号定位到音乐一致性与证据关联问答，并对12个VLM进行诊断性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学音乐识别（OMR）资源以检测与转写指标为主，仅凭转写分数无法揭示VLM乐谱阅读器的优势与瓶颈，需对记谱识别、结构恢复与音乐语义解释分别评估。

Method: 构建 OmniScore-Bench：每道题指定可见范围、参考标注与答案模式，实现自动评测；覆盖6个方面、24类子任务；评测12个VLM，并做受控分析（移除乐谱图像、辅助给定条件、额外页面上下文），以及格式与内容分离分析。

Result: 揭示各操作特定的能力画像；在节选、单页与多页任务中转写领先者不同；移除图像后音高与反复记号解释表现明显依赖视觉信息，并量化仅凭公开上下文所保留的性能；可将答案格式违规与音乐性错误区分开。

Conclusion: OmniScore-Bench 支持诊断VLM在乐谱阅读各操作上哪些可靠、哪些需改进，弥补单一转写指标的不足。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can answer questions about musical scores, but their ability to recognise notation, recover its structure and interpret its musical meaning requires separate evaluation. Existing optical music recognition (OMR) resources establish detection and transcription measurements; a transcription score alone leaves the strengths and bottlenecks of a VLM score reader unresolved. We introduce OmniScore-Bench, an evaluation of OMR capabilities in VLMs through 3,000 questions spanning six aspects and 24 task subtypes. Each question specifies a visible scope, reference annotations and an answer schema, enabling automatic evaluation from symbol localisation to musical consistency and evidence-linked question answering. Our assessment of twelve VLMs reveals operation-specific capability profiles and different transcription leaders across excerpt, single-page and multipage tasks. Controlled analyses examine the roles of score images, auxiliary givens and additional page context. On the tested subset, image removal exposes visual dependence in pitch and repeat interpretation while measuring how much performance remains with public context alone. Separate format and content analyses distinguish answer-format violations from musical errors. OmniScore-Bench supports diagnosing which score-reading operations VLMs perform reliably and which require improvement.

</details>


### [60] [Soteria: Formally Verified Planning with Runtime Enforcement for Safe LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17880)
*Mike He,Ankush Desai,Sharad Malik,Aarti Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Soteria 框架，通过"执行前验证的分层规划 + 运行时规范强制"同时兼顾 LLM 智能体工具调用的效用与安全性，在多个基准上达到最佳基线平均任务成功率的 1.7 倍且零策略违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步工具调用的 LLM 智能体需同时满足任务完成（效用）与符合领域策略/正确性约束（安全），二者存在张力：拦截不安全动作虽防违规，却使智能体无原则性恢复途径而陷入停滞。现有方法往往牺牲其一。

Method: Soteria 采用验证式分层规划与运行时规范强制：执行前生成结构化计划，并在任何工具调用发生前对计划做形式化规范验证；执行中该已验证计划承担双重角色——约束智能体走规范一致轨迹，并在不安全动作被拦截时提供恢复指引。

Result: 在覆盖多样工具使用任务的多个基准上，Soteria 的平均任务成功率达最佳基线的 1.7 倍，且无任何策略规范违规。

Conclusion: 安全与效用并非必须权衡取舍，通过执行前形式化验证规划与运行时强制相结合，可在保证零违规的同时显著提升任务成功率。

Abstract: LLM agents that execute multi-step tool calls must satisfy two objectives simultaneously: completing the user's task (utility) and conforming to domain policies and correctness constraints (safety). These objectives are in tension -- blocking an unsafe action prevents a violation but can leave the agent stranded with no principled way to recover. Existing approaches sacrifice one objective for the other. We introduce Soteria, a framework that reconciles safety and utility through verified hierarchical planning and runtime enforcement of specifications. Before execution, the agent generates a structured plan that is formally verified against the specifications prior to any tool invocation. During execution, the verified plan serves dual roles: it ensures that the agent takes specification-conformant trajectories and provides guidance when unsafe actions are blocked. Across multiple benchmarks covering a diverse range of tool-use tasks, Soteria achieves 1.7 times the average task success rate of the best baselines, with no violations of policy specifications, demonstrating that safety and utility need not be traded off.

</details>


### [61] [Adversarially Attacking Symbolic Vocabulary Vulnerabilities In LLM Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17910)
*Stephen Obadinma,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于LLM的对抗攻击模型Symbolic Swapper，自动生成使用非标准符号词汇的混淆规划域，以揭示LLM智能体在符号解释与规划联合任务中的脆弱性，实验显示可使基础模型及鲁棒规划框架的规划性能下降超70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自主智能体日益用于自动化规划任务，但一旦不能利用符号词汇（动作、谓词、对象名称）中的常识线索，其规划能力便不稳健、不如符号规划器可靠。即便近期推理模型在混淆符号词汇任务上表现提升，其联合符号解释与规划的弱点究竟残留多少仍不清楚，需要在更严苛情形下检验其纯粹规划能力。

Method: 构建LLM驱动的攻击模型Symbolic Swapper：以对抗攻击框架自动生成带有非标准符号词汇的混淆规划域，系统性地破坏强推理模型的规划能力；通过递增的混淆方案对基础模型及鲁棒规划框架进行评估，并进一步分析规划能力崩塌的影响因素，区分模型对任务域的理解与其在该域下进行规划的能力。

Result: 攻击可使基础模型乃至鲁棒规划框架的规划性能下降超过70%；成功混淆会令模型推理质量显著下降，尽管模型仍表现出一定的领域理解能力，即出现"能感知任务域但无法在其下规划"的落差。

Conclusion: LLM智能体在符号解释与规划联合方面仍存在关键且可被系统性放大的弱点；对任务域的感知与在该域下实际规划能力之间存在明显鸿沟，说明当前LLM尚不能替代符号规划器的可靠性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, their abilities in navigating automated planning tasks have become essential. However, despite their strengths, LLMs' planning ability is not robust, particularly when they are not allowed to exploit commonsense cues in the symbolic vocabulary (e.g., names of actions, predicates, and objects) used to define planning tasks. This makes LLMs unreliable compared to symbolic planners. With recent reasoning models showing improved planning performance on tasks with obfuscated symbolic vocabularies, we utilize an adversarial attack framework to reveal to what extent the crucial weaknesses of LLM agents at joint symbolic interpretation and planning remain and whether they can reason properly when presented with severe cases. As such, our main contribution is devising an LLM-based attack model, which we call Symbolic Swapper, to automatically generate obfuscated planning domains with non-standard symbolic vocabularies that systematically break strong reasoning models' ability to plan. In doing so, we reveal how much LLMs' planning abilities can be further degraded under more advanced obfuscation schemes, allowing us to ascertain their success when having to rely on their pure planning ability. We find that attacks can decrease the planning performance of base models and even robust planning frameworks by over 70%. We further analyze the factors behind how LLM planning abilities break down and find that successful obfuscation causes models to appear to significantly degrade in reasoning quality despite them showing some inherent ability to understand the domain, revealing a gap in the perception of a task domain and being able to plan under it.

</details>


### [62] [SWIFT: Sample-Wise Instance Fine-Tuning of Time-Series Foundation Models for Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17915)
*Yang Chen,Feifan Tian,Cong Wang,Cheng Feng,Wenbin Li,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SWIFT框架，利用目标样本自身历史进行样本级实例微调，实现时间序列基础模型的免额外数据集自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测基础模型的少样本自适应策略以数据集为中心，需额外获取数据集用于微调，严重阻碍落地应用。

Method: SWIFT融合三项机制：Self-Retrieval用于历史挖掘，Frequency-Aware Loss保证频谱一致性，Deviation-Aware Dynamic Fusion平衡适配与通用知识保持。

Result: 在4种TSFM骨干与Chronos Benchmark II的6个数据集共24个组合上，MASE与WQL平均分别降低6.41%与4.83%。

Conclusion: SWIFT是首个针对时间序列基础模型开展样本级实例微调的框架，仅用单个预测窗口自身观测历史即可完成特化，且对终端用户完全透明。

Abstract: While current few-shot adaptation strategies for time series forecasting foundation models are predominantly dataset-centric, the necessity of obtaining an additional dataset for the finetuning process poses a significant hurdle for adoption. To address this constraint, we propose Sample-Wise Instance Fine-Tuning (SWIFT), a novel forecasting adaptation framework that achieves fine-grained specialization by leveraging structural priors from the target instance's own history. This shift allows the fine-tuning process to be entirely transparent to the end-user, which significantly enhances the ease of adoption. To implement this framework, SWIFT synergizes Self-Retrieval for historical mining and Frequency-Aware Loss for spectral consistency, while employing Deviation-Aware Dynamic Fusion to balance adaptation with general knowledge preservation. Extensive experiments across four TSFM backbones and three long-history benchmark subsets validate SWIFT, yielding average MASE and WQL reductions of 6.41% and 4.83% across 24 combinations of four backbones and six Chronos Benchmark II datasets. To the best of our knowledge, SWIFT is the first framework to investigate sample-wise instance fine-tuning of time-series foundation models, specializing the model for an individual prediction window using only the corresponding forecasting sample's own observed history.

</details>


### [63] [When Cooperation Must Change: Semantic Strategy Unlearning in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17917)
*Wenhan Wu,Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Fan Yang,Honglong Chen,Kai Yin,Jiawei Jiang,Chuang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出语义协作策略遗忘问题并给出CARVE方法，在五个协作MARL基准上将目标惯例的完成率与最坏情况诱发率分别降低91.4%和83.3%，中位效用损失仅5.9%，推理延迟与原策略一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作式多智能体强化学习学到的协调惯例在部署需求变化后可能过时或不安全（如缓存共享权限修订需放弃已学到的存储/服务角色分工），需在移除指定惯例的同时保留有用的协作能力。难点在于同一关系模式可由不同智能体在不同历史下重现；运行时屏蔽仅保留协调偏好、损害任务进展，现有强化遗忘与轨迹删除方法不直接针对角色不变（role-invariant）的惯例。

Method: 形式化为"语义协作策略遗忘"问题并提出CARVE：(1) 因果协调归因识别有影响力的交互；(2) 保持感知的子空间更新以限制对无关行为的干扰；(3) 反事实重协调学习有效的替代方案；(4) 对抗性审计检验目标惯例是否仍可被恢复；(5) 将接受的更新合并进去中心化actor以直接部署。

Result: 在五个协作MARL基准上，CARVE将平均常规惯例完成率降低91.4%、最坏情况诱发率降低83.3%；中位效用损失5.9%；实测推理延迟与原始策略相当。

Conclusion: CARVE能有效清除角色不变的协作惯例、抵御恢复性诱发，同时以较小效用代价保持任务性能并维持部署可行性，为MARL策略的语义级遗忘提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) supports coordinated decision-making in distributed Web services, multi-robot control, and collaborative games through learned coordination conventions. When deployment requirements change, a previously useful convention may become obsolete or unsafe. For example, revised cache-sharing permissions in Web content delivery may require caches to abandon a learned division of storage and serving roles while maintaining efficient delivery. Teams must therefore remove the specified convention while preserving useful cooperation. This is challenging because the same relational pattern can be reproduced by different agents across different histories. Runtime shielding leaves the underlying coordination preference intact and can impair task progress, while existing reinforcement unlearning and trajectory-deletion methods do not directly target role-invariant conventions. This paper formalizes this problem as semantic coordination-strategy unlearning and proposes CARVE.CARVE uses causal coordination attribution to identify influential interactions and retain-aware subspace updates to limit interference with unrelated behaviors. Counterfactual re-coordination helps agents learn productive alternatives, while adversarial auditing tests whether the target convention can still be recovered. Accepted updates are merged into decentralized actors for direct deployment. Evaluation across five cooperative MARL benchmarks shows that CARVE reduces mean nominal convention-completion and worst-case elicitation rates by 91.4\% and 83.3\%, respectively.The median utility loss is 5.9\%, and measured inference latency matches that of the original policies.

</details>


### [64] [What Balanced Accuracy Cannot Identify in Neural-Signal Decoding under Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17925)
*Jiangshuo Xie,Zonghan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文考察神经信号分类中测试时适应（full_ttt）的聚合指标无法刻画队列内部变化的局限，指出平衡准确率无法识别某类被完全丢弃（误报为50%的两类解码器），并显示在固定队列、划分与更新规则后，受试者内部类别对比随编码器而变；聚合指标与对比指标在11个数据-编码器单元中有10个指向不同编码器，故应同时报告类别级与受试者级变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经信号分类的适应评估多为传导式（transductive）：每批更新后即在该批上评分，增益依赖于适应后的模型，且以聚合指标（如平衡准确率）汇报。作者质疑：聚合变化无法刻画队列内部发生了什么——既可能掩盖个别个体的受损，也可能无法区分"均匀退步"与"某一类被完全删除"，因此需要在个体与类别层面拆解。

Method: 在11个数据集-编码器单元（Siena/EEG 与 PTB-XL/ECG 各4个，Sleep-EDF/EEG 3个）上评估full_ttt，固定划分与源模型评估；以受试者为分析单位，采用精确符号检验并以Holm校正且条件于共享流，配合bootstrap区间与依赖性审计（fold级与run聚簇参照）。除聚合指标外，直接检验受试者内部类别/阶段对比，而非通过与零的检验间接推断编码器差异。

Result: 二分类情形下，只报单一类别的解码器与以50%报两类的解码器得到相同的平衡准确率，故聚合值无法发现某类不再被预测；占受试者样本约十分之一的类别在112个受试者-单元中有26–47个未被预测，对照组为7–9（描述性计数）。在固定队列、划分与更新规则后，受试者内类别对比随编码器而非随机变动：Siena 6对编码器中2对在名义参照、1对在fold级参照下通过依赖审计；Sleep-EDF 10对阶段中2对通过run聚簇阈值（名义参照）。聚合指标与对比指标在11个单元中有10个指向不同编码器。流交错效应会随状态共享而反号（+3.5至−2.4 pp），构成协议边界。

Conclusion: 聚合分数可掩盖个体受损，却无法区分均匀损失与被删除的类别；因此在报告聚合变化的同时应汇报类别级变化，并在其旁汇报受试者级变化，且两类结果均需以数据-编码器单元、更新规则与流的构造方式为条件加以限定。

Abstract: Adaptation for neural-signal classification is usually transductive: each batch is scored after updating on it, and the gain depends on the adapted model [4]. This paper asks what an aggregate change fails to characterise inside the cohort. Full test-time adaptation (full_ttt) was evaluated in 11 dataset-encoder cells (four each on Siena/EEG and PTB-XL/ECG, three on Sleep-EDF/EEG), with splits and source-model evaluation fixed; the subject was the unit, exact sign tests were Holm-corrected and conditional on the shared stream, and bootstrap intervals accompanied a dependence audit. Two results of different kinds follow. In the binary case balanced accuracy gives a one-class decoder the same value as one reporting both classes at 50%, so the aggregate cannot identify a class that stopped being reported; a class covering a tenth of a subject's samples went unpredicted in 26-47 of 112 subject-cells, against 7-9 for the controls, which is a descriptive count. With cohort, split and update rule fixed, the within-subject class contrast follows the encoder, tested directly rather than inferred from tests against zero: two of six Siena encoder pairs survive the dependence readings at their nominal reference and one at a fold-level reference, while two of ten Sleep-EDF stage pairs pass the run-clustered gate at its nominal reference; aggregate and contrast named different encoders in 10 of 11. The protocol bounds both results, since a stream-interleaving effect reverses sign with state sharing (+3.5 to -2.4 pp). An aggregate score can hide individual harm, as [1] showed, but it cannot distinguish a uniform loss from a deleted class; class-level change should therefore be reported alongside the aggregate and subject-level change alongside it, with both results conditional on the cell, the rule and the stream construction.

</details>


### [65] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Scientific Paper Search: Retrieval Success and Inference Latency on Mac Studio and NVIDIA A100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17929)
*Frederic Andriantseheno Rakoto,Yannis Bendi-Ouis,Xavier Hinaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估在本地部署的开源权重LLM能否在实用算力约束下支持联网辅助的科学论文检索：先测Mac Studio与双A100服务器的推理延迟随上下文（512–128k tokens）的变化，再用8个模型对7个科学仓库中70篇论文、3种领域知识水平的查询进行检索评测。结果表明检索成功率高度依赖模型与查询具体度，A100总体更快，但Mac统一内存可加载GPU配置装不下的大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学文献检索若能由本地部署的LLM助手完成，可降低对托管模型提供商的依赖，并让研究团队对数据与基础设施有更强控制。作者因此关心：在现实计算约束下，开源权重模型是否足以支撑联网辅助的论文检索。

Method: 两方面实验：(1) 硬件性能刻画——在Mac Studio与配两块NVIDIA A100 GPU的服务器上，测量输入上下文从512增至128k tokens时的推理延迟；(2) 检索能力评测——在7个科学仓库的70篇论文上，用代表三种领域知识水平的查询，评测8个模型的检索表现。

Result: 检索成功率强烈依赖所选模型与查询的具体程度；A100 GPU总体比Mac Studio更快，但Mac的统一内存可加载在测试GPU配置下无法装入的更大模型。

Conclusion: 本地部署的开源权重模型在实用约束下可支持联网辅助的文献检索，但需按模型能力与查询具体度匹配选择；硬件上GPU擅长速度、大统一内存擅长承载大模型，二者构成权衡。结论基于小规模检索集，具初步性。

Abstract: Scientific literature search can benefit from locally deployed LLM assistants, which reduce reliance on hosted model providers while giving research teams greater control over their data and infrastructure. This work investigates whether open-weight models can support web-assisted paper retrieval under practical computing constraints. We first characterize inference latency as the input context grows from 512 to 128k tokens on a Mac Studio and a server equipped with two NVIDIA A100 GPUs. We then evaluate eight models on the retrieval of 70 papers from seven scientific repositories, using queries that represent three levels of domain knowledge. The results show that retrieval success depends strongly on the selected model and the specificity of the query. And that A100 GPUs are generally faster than the Mac Studio, but its unified memory allows larger models that could not be loaded in the tested GPU configuration.

</details>


### [66] [OutfitterBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Resource Allocation and Commitment Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17933)
*Jie Huang,Yuecheng Hong,Yao Chen,Shengding Jiao,Xudong Liu,Qiang Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OutfitterBench基准：让LLM代理经营户外装备租赁业务200天，在固定资本上限下同时处理约1100个预订请求与不确定的临时需求，考察其能否维持自身承诺可行并随时间重估资源配置价值；七个前沿模型中多数不敌简单规则基线，仅领先者稳定超越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中代理自身决策会形成未来约束（有限产能的承诺、机会的封闭），因此持续表现既需保持演化计划的可行性，也需权衡资源分配与被其挤占的替代方案的价值。现有基准多评估"持续运营"中的LLM代理，却较少关注其如何使自身承诺保持可行、并在机会变化时重新评估资源配置。

Method: 构建OutfitterBench：代理运营户外装备租赁业务200天，从46件商品目录采购，处理约1,100个预订请求；在固定资本上限下，预订与不确定的到店需求争夺随时间变化可用性的可复用装备。对七个前沿LLM进行实验并做轨迹分析，与简单规则基线对比。

Result: 多个代理表现低于简单规则基线，仅领先代理稳定超越基线。轨迹分析显示：部分代理对已知约束应用不一致，新决策与既有承诺冲突；履约更可靠的代理仍可能从相近承诺中获得低回报，或延迟资源再配置；领先代理能更好将准备工作与经济选择联系起来，但仍错失更盈利的资产使用机会。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了LLM代理在维持连贯资源计划并随时间重估其价值方面的局限；基准代码已公开于 https://github.com/adminblackjack/OutfitterBench 。

Abstract: In many long-horizon settings, an agent's own choices create future constraints: committing finite capacity establishes future obligations and forecloses later opportunities. Sustaining performance therefore requires both keeping evolving plans feasible and comparing the value of resource allocations with the alternatives they displace. Recent benchmarks increasingly evaluate LLM agents in continuing operations, but give less attention to how agents keep their own commitments feasible while reassessing resource allocations as opportunities change. We introduce OutfitterBench, where agents manage an outdoor-equipment rental business for 200 days, purchasing from a 46-product catalog and handling roughly 1,100 reservation requests. Under a fixed capital ceiling, reservations and uncertain walk-in demand compete for reusable equipment whose availability changes over time. Experiments with seven frontier LLMs show that several agents underperform a simple rule-based baseline, while only the leading agent consistently surpasses it. Trajectory analysis shows that some agents apply known constraints inconsistently and make new decisions that conflict with existing commitments. Agents with more reliable fulfillment can still secure low returns from comparable commitments or delay resource reallocation. The leading agent better connects preparation with economic choices but also misses opportunities for more profitable asset use. These findings highlight limitations in sustaining coherent resource plans and reassessing their value over time. The benchmark code is publicly available at https://github.com/adminblackjack/OutfitterBench.

</details>


### [67] [Tracing Actual Causes with Counterfactual Witness Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17937)
*Tim Woydt,Matej Zečević,Kristian Kersting,Moritz Willig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出witness targets与祖先干预文法，构造有限DAG型witness map，并给出首个完备枚举所有actual causes的算法TrAC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Halpernian actual causation缺乏可靠且完备的枚举程序，无法找到特定情境下某结果的全部实际原因，阻碍了责任归因、过错评估与因果危害分级等需要聚合完整原因集的下游任务。

Method: 引入witness targets（包含所有反事实见证的联合配置集合），结合祖先干预文法导出有限的witness map（有向无环），证明每个实际原因都存在一个contingency set使其联合干预集成为该map的节点，据此提出Tracing Actual Causes（TrAC）算法；在随机布尔结构因果模型上与单原因基线对比。

Result: 证明TrAC对任意目标事件的所有实际原因枚举是sound且complete的；实验显示在节点数最多15的图上具备实际可行性，并与现有单原因基线进行了经验比较。

Conclusion: 填补了Halpernian actual causation完备枚举的理论空白，提供首个可靠且完备的枚举算法，为责任归因、过错评估和因果危害分级等下游任务奠定基础。
