Abstract: Halpernian actual causation, the formal study of which events in a specific situation caused a specific outcome in acyclic structural causal models, underpins legal responsibility, moral blame, and the assessment of causal harm. Despite the rich logical framework, no prior sound and complete enumeration procedure is known for finding all actual causes in a given setting, blocking downstream tasks such as responsibility attribution, blame assessment, and causal-harm grading that aggregate over the full cause set. We close this gap by introducing witness targets: sets of joint configurations containing every counterfactual witness. In combination with an ancestor intervention grammar we derive a finite directed acyclic witness map. We prove that for every actual cause there exists a contingency set such that the joint intervention set appears as a node of this map, and present an algorithm for Tracing Actual Causes (TrAC) that is sound and complete for enumerating all actual causes of any target event. We compare TrAC empirically with existing single-cause baselines on random Boolean structural causal models, demonstrating practical feasibility for graphs of up to 15 nodes.

</details>


### [68] [Toward Interference-Agnostic RF Source Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17950)
*Maxence Albert,Raphaël Duroselle,Thibaud Ehret,Olivier Boeffard,Jean-François Bonastre*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 专用射频干扰分离器对未见干扰类型会失效甚至使信号更差；作者训练单一统一分离器（WaveNet与band-split transformer两种架构），在规模与单个专用模型相当时性能接近各专用模型，且对训练中从未出现的干扰类型也能达到最佳专用模型的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有射频信号与同信道干扰分离方法多采用专用分离器，每个模型仅针对一种"信号-干扰"组合训练，依赖干扰类型标签；而重干扰下的接收机无法识别干扰类型，导致标签不可得、模型部署选择困难。

Method: 在ICASSP 2024 RF Challenge的卷积WaveNet和从音乐源分离迁移的band-split transformer两种无共同组件的架构上，训练一个覆盖所有信号-干扰组合的单一统一分离器，且测试时不提供干扰类型提示。

Result: 专用分离器用于未训练干扰时通常无法恢复信号，多数情况下比不做处理更差；统一分离器在单个专用模型规模下，几乎每对组合的误码率接近其所替代的整套模型，一对更优、一对更差；对训练集外干扰，其表现与最佳专用模型相当。

Conclusion: 对干扰类型的依赖并非无害，统一分离器可替代整套专用模型而无需在部署时选择模型，并具备对未见干扰类型的泛化能力，且结论在两种差异显著的架构上一致。

Abstract: Separating a radio-frequency signal of interest from a co-channel interference is mostly addressed with specialized separators, each trained for one pair of signal and interference types and therefore relying on a label that a heavily jammed receiver cannot identify. We show that this reliance is not benign: applied to an interference it was not trained on, such a separator usually fails to recover the signal, and in most of the cases we measure it leaves the signal worse than no processing at all. We then train a single separator over every pair, giving it no indication at test time of which one it faces, on two architectures with no component in common: the convolutional WaveNet of the ICASSP 2024 RF Challenge, and a band-split transformer that we adapt from music source separation. Both behave the same way. At the size of one specialized model, the unified separator stays close to the whole models it replaces, keeping the bit error rate close on almost every pair, better on one and worse on another. On an interference absent from every training set, it does as well as the best specialized model, without anyone having to choose which one to deploy.

</details>


### [69] [WebArena-Pro: A Heterogeneous, Multimodal, Reproducible Benchmark for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17955)
*Imene Kerboua,Fatemeh Pesaran Zadeh,Xing Han Lù,Weijian Qi,Alexander Miller,Junyi Song,Yunjia Tian,Dongjin Kang,Seyeon Choi,Marzia Nouri,Ewen Gueguen,Matteo Boglioni,Fengyuan Liu,Zeyi Liao,Mengqi Yuan,Yue Li,Alexandre Lacoste,Alexandre Drouin,Spandana Gella,Huan Sun,Gunhee Kim,Siva Reddy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WebArena-Pro：覆盖 20 个自托管 Web 应用、6 个领域类别、300 个任务的可复现多模态 Web Agent 基准，并将音频与视频作为核心观测模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可复现 Web Agent 基准应用数量少、软件类别窄，且仅限文本与视觉；在线基准虽扩大站点覆盖却牺牲可复现性；同时缺乏对网页任务中音频、视频内容理解与使用的有效评测。

Method: 构建 20 个自托管应用（涵盖不同界面约定、工作流与数据模型）上的 300 个任务；将音频、视频与文本、视觉并列为核心观测，并提供各模态专用信息抽取动作；任务经标注者专用界面撰写、LLM 辅助初筛、人工校验后发布；每个任务隔离运行，支持可复现的并行评测。

Result: 最佳表现为 Codex + GPT 6.1 Sol 与 Claude Code + Claude Opus 5.5，均为 62.3%；OpenCode + Kimi 3.1 Pro 为 60.7%；最强 GenericAgent 配置（Gemini 3.1 Pro omni、Gemma4 31B）均为 30.3%。

Conclusion: 在可复现、人工构建的 Web Agent 基准中，WebArena-Pro 提供最广的应用覆盖与最全面的多模态支持，兼顾可复现性与并行评测效率。

Abstract: Web agents powered by large language and vision-language models are increasingly applied to realistic browser work that spans heterogeneous applications, multimodal content, and stateful workflows. However, existing reproducible web-agent benchmarks cover only a small number of web applications drawn from a few software categories, and restrict modality to text and vision. Live benchmarks broaden site coverage but sacrifice reproducibility, since pages and data drift between runs. Moreover, existing benchmarks do not meaningfully evaluate whether agents can understand and use audio and video content embedded within web tasks. To address these gaps, we introduce WebArena-Pro, a benchmark comprising 300 tasks across 20 self-hosted web applications in six domain categories, spanning distinct interface conventions, workflows, and data models. Across the evaluated agents, the best performance is achieved by both Codex with GPT 6.1 Sol and Claude Code with Claude Opus 5.5 at 62.3%, followed by OpenCode with Kimi 3.1 Pro at 60.7%. The strongest GenericAgent configurations, Gemini 3.1 Pro omni and Gemma4 31B, both achieve 30.3%. Among reproducible, human-curated web agent benchmarks, WebArena-Pro provides the broadest application coverage and the most comprehensive multimodal support to date. The benchmark treats audio and video as core observations alongside text and vision, with dedicated actions for extracting information from each. WebArena-Pro runs each task in isolation and supports reproducible, parallel evaluation. Tasks are authored through a dedicated annotator interface, filtered by LLM-assisted triage, and finally validated by humans before release. Project page: https://webarena-pro.github.io

</details>


### [70] [TotalSegmentator Aorta Report: Automated Aortic Diameter Measurement at 11 Standardized Landmarks on CT Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17968)
*Jakob Wasserthal,Daniel Hinck,Martin Segeroth,Joshy Cyriac,Denis Berdajs,Jules Miazza,Maurice Pradella*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发并评估了一种开源全自动工具，可从任意CT图像上、按照现行指南在从主动脉瓣环到分叉的11个标准化解剖标志处测量主动脉直径，平均绝对误差（MAE）约1 mm，低于已发表的阅片者间变异，但在主动脉根部和存在附壁血栓时一致性下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现行指南建议对全主动脉多个标准化标志进行直径测量，但人工测量耗时且存在阅片者间变异；需要一种可在任意CT上通用、可复现的全自动测量工具。

Method: 单中心回顾性研究，纳入2025年4月至2026年10月间230例患者的239次动脉期CTA检查（中位年龄65岁，163例男性，其中209次为ECG门控）。工具基于TotalSegmentator对主动脉分支和椎体的分割来定位11个标志点，并在每个标志处测量最大直径。由一名资深放射科医师复核并按需修正自动测量值，形成参考标准；采用MAE、偏倚及>2 mm差异比例评估一致性。

Result: 全标志点MAE为1.06 mm（95% CI: 0.91, 1.22），86.6%（95% CI: 84.4, 88.5）的差异≤2 mm；工具测量值略大（偏倚0.45 mm [95% CI: 0.34, 0.57]）。Valsalva窦MAE最大（2.17 mm [95% CI: 1.59, 2.86]）。1.2%的标志点自动测量失败。检查层面的MAE在不同病变、动脉粥样硬化分级（P=.95）和ECG门控（P=.14）间无显著差异；存在附壁血栓的检查更常出现MAE>2 mm（50% [95% CI: 31, 69] vs 20%；P=.03）。

Conclusion: 该工具可在全主动脉11个标志点测量直径，MAE约1 mm，优于已报道的阅片者间变异；在主动脉根部及合并附壁血栓时一致性较低。工具开源，可通过 https://github.com/wasserth/TotalSegmentator 免费获取。

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate a fully automated, open-source tool that measures aortic diameters at 11 standardized landmarks from the aortic annulus to the bifurcation on any CT image, following current guideline recommendations.
  Materials and Methods: This retrospective single-center study included 239 arterial phase CT angiography examinations of 230 patients (median age, 65 years [range, 18-99 years]; 163 men) performed between April 2025 and October 2026 (209 electrocardiographically [ECG] gated). The tool locates landmarks from TotalSegmentator segmentations of aortic branches and vertebrae and measures the maximum diameter at each. A senior radiologist reviewed and, where needed, corrected the automated measurements to create the reference standard. Agreement was assessed with mean absolute error (MAE), bias, and differences greater than 2 mm.
  Results: Across all landmarks, MAE was 1.06 mm (95% CI: 0.91, 1.22), and 86.6% (95% CI: 84.4, 88.5) of differences were 2 mm or less. The tool measured slightly larger diameters (bias, 0.45 mm [95% CI: 0.34, 0.57]). The sinuses of Valsalva had the largest MAE (2.17 mm [95% CI: 1.59, 2.86]). For 1.2% of landmarks, the automatic measurement failed. Examination-level MAE did not differ significantly by pathology, atherosclerosis grade (P=.95), or ECG gating (P=.14). Examinations with mural thrombus more often had an MAE greater than 2 mm (50% [95% CI: 31, 69] vs 20%; P=.03).
  Conclusion: The tool measured aortic diameters at 11 landmarks along the entire aorta with an MAE of about 1 mm, below published interreader variability. Agreement was lower at the aortic root and with mural thrombus.
  Code availability: The tool is open source and freely available at https://github.com/wasserth/TotalSegmentator.

</details>


### [71] [Interactive deep-learning based interface for brain tumour segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17983)
*Enora Giffard,Pierre Jannin,John S H Baxter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种默认全自动、但可被人工便捷修正的深度学习交互式脑肿瘤分割系统，并在自动精度与专家可用性两方面做了初步评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MR图像脑肿瘤分割在手术切除与放疗中要求高精度，但肿瘤在大小、形状、数量与位置上前差异大，且不同厂商MR序列存在细微差异，导致深度学习自动分割出现灾难性错误，难以保证所有患者的精度。

Method: 构建基于深度学习的交互式分割系统：默认全自动分割，同时允许人类用户轻松对结果进行修正，作为自动方法的补充。

Result: 从自动分割精度与可用性两方面对该界面进行了评估，其中可用性通过初步的观察性专家用户研究完成。

Conclusion: 人机协同的交互式分割可弥补纯自动方法在跨序列/跨厂商泛化上的不足，为所有患者提供足够高的脑肿瘤分割精度。

Abstract: Brain tumour segmentation from MR images is an integral part of their treatment, either through surgical resection or radiotherapy. Both applications require a high degree of precision that can be difficult to ensure for all patients due to the high degree of variability in the size, shape, number, and location of these tumours. This leads to difficulties with their automatic segmentation via deep learning, which can display catastrophic segmentation errors when presented with images that differ, even subtly, in terms of the specific MR sequence used, which varies from vendor to vendor. This article presents a deep-learning-based interactive segmentation system that, while being fully automatic by default, has the capability of being easily corrected by human users, allowing them ensure sufficiently high brain tumour segmentation accuracy for all patients. This interface is evaluated both in terms of the accuracy of its automatic approach as well as its usability through a preliminary observational expert user study.

</details>


### [72] [LACCO: Local Accumulation with Computation--Communication Overlap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17991)
*Daniele Kopyshevskiy,Benjamin Th{é}rien,Nicolas Pinto,Edouard Oyallon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LACCO 是一种两阶段算法，将全局通信与本地计算重叠，同时保持全局聚合更新的无陈旧性，用于低带宽网络下的语言模型分布式训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低带宽网络训练语言模型需降低同步开销。DiLoCo 等本地优化方法仅在若干本地步后通信，但外层梯度平均与外层更新仍处于关键路径；现有计算-通信重叠方法依赖延迟或副本本地更新，会损害收敛或导致副本漂移。

Method: 提出 LACCO（Local Accumulation with Computation-Communication Overlap）两阶段算法：将全局通信与本地计算重叠，同时施加所有副本共享的全局聚合更新，且不在外层更新中引入陈旧性。

Result: 给出光滑非凸目标下的收敛性保证；在 FineWeb 语言模型预训练实验中，LACCO 稳定优于 Eager、Delayed DiLoCo 等通信重叠基线，并在模型规模至 0.5B 参数、多种通信间隔及最多 16 个副本时保持增益，计算或内存开销可忽略或完全可重叠。

Conclusion: LACCO 在保证外层更新无陈旧性的前提下有效隐藏通信开销，兼顾收敛质量与扩展性，适用于低带宽分布式语言模型训练。

Abstract: Training language models across low-bandwidth networks requires reducing synchronization overhead. Local optimization methods such as DiLoCo communicate only after several local steps, but outer gradient averaging and the outer update remain on the critical path. Existing computation-communication overlap methods avoid this stall through delayed or replica-local updates, which can degrade convergence or cause replicas to drift. We propose LACCO (Local Accumulation with Computation-Communication Overlap), a two-stage algorithm that overlaps global communication with local computation while applying globally aggregated updates shared by every replica, without introducing staleness in the outer update. We establish convergence guarantees for smooth non-convex objectives. Experiments on language-model pre-training with FineWeb show that LACCO consistently outperforms communication-overlapped baselines such as Eager and Delayed DiLoCo. These gains remain robust as model size scales up to 0.5B parameters and across a range of communication intervals and numbers of replicas up to 16 workers, while incurring only negligible-or fully overlappable-computational or memory overhead, which demonstrates the strength of LACCO.

</details>


### [73] [Understanding the limits of snapshot-based fault diagnosis in distribution networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17998)
*Ba Tu Phung,Huy Hoang Le,Kim-Anh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文将快照式故障诊断的误差来源（分类器、特征、测量误差、故障电阻）分离量化，证明分类器非瓶颈，误差主要来自高阻故障下的测量误差；3个电压传感器在1% TVE等级下可达90–96%准确率，micro-PMU下达96–99%，并提出一种亚秒级、仅依赖仿真的可辨识度-裕度布点方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆变器型电源削弱配电网故障电流，基于单一时刻少数母线电压相量的快照式诊断可作为低成本一级筛查；但既有文献报道的准确率建立在不同噪声假设上，且无法区分误差究竟来自分类器、特征、测量误差还是故障电阻，缺乏可信度边界。

Method: 在三个IEEE测试馈线及一个含分布式发电的公开数据集上，采用4种特征集与13种分类器，以总矢量误差（TVE）等级定义测量误差，分离各误差来源；并提出基于可辨识度-裕度的传感器布点方法，仅用仿真数据、1秒内完成选点。

Result: 无噪声时完整相量快照可正确分类全部故障，说明分类器非限制因素；剩余误差来自高阻故障上的测量误差；3个电压传感器在1%精度等级下分类90–96%故障，使用配电网micro-PMU时达96–99%；所提布点方法与12种布点方法中的最优者持平。

Conclusion: 快照式诊断的可信性主要受测量误差与高阻故障制约而非分类器；少量经优化布点的电压传感器即可在给定TVE等级下实现高准确率，从而界定了该方法可被信任的适用条件。

Abstract: Inverter-based resources weaken fault currents in distribution networks, and snapshot-based diagnosis, which classifies a fault from voltage phasors at a few buses at one instant, offers a cheap first-stage screen. Its published accuracies, however, rest on different noise assumptions and do not show whether the error comes from the classifier, the features, the measurement error, or the fault resistance. This paper separates these sources on three IEEE test feeders and a public data set with distributed generation, using four feature sets, thirteen classifiers, and measurement error defined by total vector error classes. The classifier is not the limiting factor, because the full phasor snapshot classifies every fault correctly without noise, and the remaining error comes from measurement error on high-impedance faults. Three voltage sensors classify 90-96% of faults at the 1% accuracy class and 96-99% with distribution micro phasor measurement units (micro-PMUs). A discriminability-margin placement selects them from simulations alone in under one second and matches the best of twelve placement methods. The results show when snapshot-based diagnosis can be trusted.

</details>


### [74] [A$^3$: Adaptive Audio Agent Framework for Collaborative Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18064)
*Zexin Li,Xueyi Zhang,Tao Ren,Yiming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A$^3$ 是一个自适应音频智能体框架：将完整流水线阶段置于边缘服务器，同时在可承载的终端设备上保留完整后备智能体，并用链路感知的截止时间在两条路径间对冲，从而在链路正常时匹配纯服务器方案、在完全断链时仍能应答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式音频智能体依赖邻近边缘服务器以获得速度，但一旦链路失效，纯服务器依赖型智能体将无法返回任何结果，缺乏可用性保障。

Method: 将整条流水线的各阶段部署于服务器端；在具备承载能力的设备上保留一个完整的后备智能体；引入链路感知的截止时间机制，对服务器路径与本地路径进行对冲，并据此决定何时放弃服务器。

Result: 在 Jetson 设备与 RTX A4500 服务器上，链路健康时 A$^3$ 与纯服务器智能体表现相当；在 AGX 上面对完全中断时对每个请求均能应答；在模拟中断场景中优于百分位对冲策略。

Conclusion: 通过在端侧保留完整后备能力并以链路感知截止时间进行路径对冲，A$^3$ 在保持服务器级性能的同时获得了断链鲁棒性。

Abstract: Embedded audio agents need a nearby edge server for speed, but a server-dependent agent returns nothing when the link fails. We present A$^3$, an adaptive audio agent framework that places whole pipeline stages on the server, keeps a complete fallback agent on devices that can host one, and hedges both paths with a link-aware deadline that decides when to give up on the server. On Jetson devices and an RTX A4500 server, A$^3$ matches the server-only agent on a healthy link, answers every request in a full outage on the AGX, and beats a percentile hedge in simulated outages.

</details>


### [75] [Two-Clustering Regime of Token Dynamics in Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18088)
*Trinh T. Nguyen,Duy-Tung Pham,Hoang-Son Do,Tan Minh Nguyen,Thieu N. Vo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文部分解决了 Karagodin 等人（NeurIPS 2024）关于因果注意力掩蔽聚类的猜想，并将其推广到时变参数情形：当值变换最大特征值 λ_max>0 时动力学收敛到与 λ_max 特征向量对齐的双簇，而 λ_max≤0 时该结论一般不成立；核心贡献是揭示了高维嵌入空间中轨迹坍缩至二维结构的降维现象，并提出"流内不稳定性"分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原猜想（因果注意力动力学收敛到与最大特征值对应的两个簇）缺乏严格证明，且仅针对静态参数；同时双簇情形在实践中常见却比以往研究的单簇情形困难得多，需要新的分析工具来处理非单调、非自治的注意力动力学。

Method: 证明 λ_max>0 时双簇收敛成立、λ_max≤0 时一般不成立；识别严格的降维现象——尽管在高维嵌入空间演化，token 轨迹坍缩为由少数主导方向支配的二维结构；据此提出受物理系统不稳定性启发的"流内不稳定性"（instability within the flow）分析框架，用于分析非单调、非自治动力学在稳态区域的行为；并用 GQA 层仿真与实验验证。

Result: 在 λ_max>0 条件下严格证明了与最大特征值特征向量对齐的双簇收敛行为；证明 λ_max≤0 时该行为一般不成立；通过降维现象将高维动力学约化为二维主导结构；"流内不稳定性"框架成功刻画了非单调、非自治注意力动力学的稳态行为；仿真与 Group Query Attention（GQA）实验支持理论结论。

Conclusion: 双簇收敛机制由值变换最大特征值的符号决定，且可借由低维主导结构加以刻画；所提降维现象与"流内不稳定性"框架为分析更复杂的因果注意力动力学提供了严格且可推广的工具，并具备实践相关性。

Abstract: We partially resolve Conjecture 1 of [Karagodin et al., Clustering in Causal Attention Masking (NeurIPS 2024)] and extend it to time-dependent parameter regimes. The conjecture predicts that causal attention dynamics converge to two clusters aligned with the eigenvectors associated with the largest eigenvalue $λ_{\max}$ of the value transformation. We prove that this behavior holds when $λ_{\max}>0$ and is generally false when $λ_{\max}\le0$. The resulting two-cluster regime is common in practice and substantially more challenging than the classical single-cluster setting studied previously. A central contribution of our work is the identification of a rigorous dimension reduction phenomenon: despite evolving in a high-dimensional embedding space, token trajectories collapse onto a two-dimensional structure governed by a small number of dominant directions. Building on this insight, we introduce a new analytical framework--instability within the flow--inspired by instability phenomena in physical systems. This framework enables the analysis of steady-regime behavior in non-monotone, non-autonomous attention dynamics. Our theoretical findings are illustrated through simulations and experimental studies using Group Query Attention (GQA) layers.

</details>


### [76] [Do Machines Learn Deviance From Their Peers? A Randomized Test of Differential Association With AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18098)
*Murat Ozer,Arif Akgul,Amitabh Chakravorty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究用AI智能体随机分配同伴，解决友谊选择带来的选择偏差，检验差异交往/社会学习理论，发现越轨型同伴提高作弊率（OR=1.96）并强化"作弊正常"的规范认知，诚实型同伴降低作弊率（OR=0.35）；监控反而使违禁讨论翻倍，同伴效应仅作用于尚未做出决定的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差异交往理论及社会学习理论认为，违规行为及其合理化定义是通过他人互动习得的。然而现实中人们会自主选择朋友，同伴与行为之间存在选择性问题（同质性/内生性），使因果检验困难。作者借助仅在交流中学习、可随机配对的AI智能体来消解这一选择效应。

Method: 预注册实验：420个三人智能体团队（共1,260个智能体，7种AI模型），执行"无法诚实完成"的编程任务。设置脚本化同伴三种条件：宣称作弊（越轨同伴）、如实上报（诚实同伴）、沉默（沉默同伴）。半数团队被告知聊天记录将被审计者阅读。比较作弊率、作弊正常化的表述，以及监控对违禁讨论的影响。

Result: 相较沉默同伴（62.9%），越轨同伴提高作弊率至66.2%（OR=1.96），诚实同伴降低至55.7%（OR=0.35）。在作弊智能体中，越轨同伴使"作弊很正常"的表述从3.4%升至13.7%，且常被表述为团队规范。监控未抑制违禁讨论，反而使其翻倍。同伴效应只出现在尚未做出决定的模型中。

Conclusion: AI智能体为检验犯罪学学习理论提供了规避选择效应的新范式，支持同伴定义对越轨行为的因果作用，并提示监控可能适得其反；但效应依赖于行为体先前的决策状态（是否已定），存在边界条件。

Abstract: Differential association theory and its extension, social learning theory, hold that rule-breaking, and the definitions that make it acceptable, are learned through interaction with others. Because people choose their friends, this claim has been hard to test. We addressed this selection problem with AI agents, which learn only through communication and can be assigned to peers at random. In a preregistered experiment, 420 teams of three agents (1,260 agents; seven AI models) worked on coding tasks that could not be solved honestly. A scripted teammate said that it had cheated (deviant peer), that it had reported the problem honestly (honest peer), or said nothing (silent peer). Half of the teams were told that an auditor read their chat. Compared with a silent peer, a deviant peer increased cheating (66.2% vs. 62.9%; OR = 1.96) and an honest peer reduced it (55.7%; OR = 0.35). Among agents that cheated, a deviant peer increased claims that cheating was normal (13.7% vs. 3.4%), often as the team's norm. Monitoring did not suppress prohibited discussion; it doubled it. Peers mattered only for models that had not yet decided. AI agents offer a new way to test criminological learning theories without selection effects.

</details>


### [77] [Optimizing Drilling Trajectories in Multi-Material Structures Using Quantum Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18121)
*Eneko Osaba,Antonio Rubio-Mateos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种量子-混合优化方法，用于多材料结构钻孔路径规划，以最小化总能耗；将问题建模为带约束的开放路径路由问题（含材料相关钻削成本、位移能耗与刀具磨损惩罚），并用D-Wave Leap约束二次模型混合求解器求解，在10–55个孔的实例上验证了方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多材料结构钻孔路径规划需同时考虑异质材料属性、材料相关成本与能耗、刀具磨损以及避免频繁换刀的工艺约束，传统方法难以在复杂多物理约束下兼顾能效优化，因此探索量子-混合优化在此类工业问题上的适用性。

Method: 将钻孔过程建模为带约束的路由问题：每个孔必须被访问，目标为核心能耗最小化，包含材料相关钻削成本、位移能耗与刀具磨损惩罚；引入开放路径（不要求返回起点）及同材料孔必须分组以规避频繁换刀等实际工业约束；采用D-Wave Leap的约束二次模型（CQM）混合求解器求解。

Result: 在10至55个孔、不同材料分布与成本矩阵的基准实例上进行测试，结果表明该量子-混合优化能够处理复杂多物理约束，并在真实制造条件下生成能效较高的钻孔序列。

Conclusion: 量子-混合优化在多材料钻孔路径规划中具备处理复杂约束并降低能耗的潜力，可作为实际制造场景下的可行求解途径；但摘要未给出与传统启发式方法的定量对比及规模扩展性结论。

Abstract: In this work we present a quantum-hybrid optimization approach for planning drilling trajectories on multi-material structures, with the objective of minimizing total energy consumption. The proposed formulation models the drilling process as a constrained routing problem in which each hole must be visited while accounting for heterogeneous material properties, material-dependent drilling costs and displacement energy, as well as tool-wear penalties. Our method incorporates realistic industrial constraints, including open-path routing and mandatory grouping of holes belonging to the same material to avoid frequent tool-bit changes. The modeled problem is solved using D-Wave's Leap Constrained Quadratic Model Hybrid Solver. To evaluate performance, we benchmark instances ranging from 10 to 55 holes with varying material distributions and cost matrices. The results highlight the capability of quantum-hybrid optimization to handle complex multi-physics constraints and to generate energy-efficient drilling sequences under realistic manufacturing conditions.

</details>


### [78] [JFinBench: A Continuously Refreshable Japanese Financial Benchmark from the Latest Certification Exams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18130)
*Issa Sugiura,Taro Makino,Takashi Ishida*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出 JFinBench——一个来自2026年六项日本金融类认证考试的321题基准（覆盖会计、税务、理财规划、银行），采用"人机协同（human-in-the-loop）智能体流水线"构建，可随考次更新以缓解数据污染；评测12个近期LLM，最强闭源模型总体正确率超93%，最佳开源模型落后12个百分点，接入终端与网络工具后所有模型均提升、差距缩小至约4个百分点且排序基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准对国别（尤其是日本）金融制度与监管实践的覆盖不足，且题目发布后会泄漏进训练数据，难以保持时效性与低污染，缺少可周期性更新的本地化金融评测集。

Method: 精选2026年版六项常规认证考试的321道题构成基准；构建上采用人机协同智能体流水线：编码智能体自动编写针对具体考试格式的转换代码与审核页面，人类逐题核验并将修正反馈给智能体，从而适配异构试卷格式、降低新增考次的纳入成本。随后评测12个近期LLM，并设置"允许使用终端与网络访问"的工具增强条件进行对照。

Result: Claude Fable 5 与 GPT-6 Astra 总体正确率超93%；最佳开源权重模型 Kimi K3.1 Pro 落后 Claude Fable 5 达12个百分点。启用终端与网络工具后，所有模型均通过检索补足缺失知识而有所提升，差距缩小但仍有约4个百分点，模型排序基本保持稳定。

Conclusion: 模型内生知识在具备工具时依然具有价值，工具接入不能完全替代参数化知识；本地化、可周期性刷新的金融基准对评估国别金融能力与抗污染具有重要意义。作者公开数据集、评测代码与构建流水线。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to finance, creating a need for benchmarks that evaluate their capabilities across financial systems with different regulations and practices. However, existing financial benchmarks provide limited coverage of country-specific financial systems, and they are difficult to keep up to date as released questions leak into training data. We introduce JFinBench, a Japanese financial benchmark comprising 321 examples from the 2026 editions of six regularly administered certification exams covering accounting, tax, financial planning, and banking. To construct it, we develop a human-in-the-loop agentic pipeline in which a coding agent writes exam-specific conversion code and a review page, while a human verifies every example and feeds corrections back to the agent. This pipeline adapts to heterogeneous exam formats and reduces the effort of incorporating new exam editions, enabling periodic refreshes that mitigate data contamination. Evaluating 12 recent LLMs, we find that Claude Fable 5 and GPT-6 Astra exceed 93% overall, while even the best open-weight model, Kimi K3.1 Pro, trails Claude Fable 5 by 12 percentage points. When models can additionally use a terminal and web access, they retrieve the knowledge they lack and every model improves. The gap narrows but about 4 points remain, and the ranking of models stays largely the same, suggesting that knowledge held in the model itself remains valuable even with tools. We release the dataset, the evaluation code, and the construction pipeline.

</details>


### [79] [Large Language Model-Empowered Intelligent Agent for Adaptive Fast-Charging Control of Lithium-Ion Batteries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18140)
*Joey Chan,Haoyu Pan,Zhen Chen,ErShun Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于大语言模型（LLM）的闭环智能体用于锂离子电池快速充电自适应控制，通过PyBaMM大规模仿真语料、监督微调（SFT）与组相对策略优化（GRPO）两阶段训练，在同等能量吞吐条件下实现优于传统协议与优化策略的循环寿命。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快充是多目标控制问题，需在缩短充电时间、维持高能量吞吐的同时抑制加速老化与安全相关电化学反应。传统协议实现简单但难以适应电池化学体系、工况与老化状态的变化；基于模型的优化和常规数据驱动控制依赖精确模型、代价高昂的迭代搜索或任务专用策略。

Method: 构建LLM赋能的闭环电池快充控制智能体：智能体解析结构化电池状态、生成可执行充电动作、观测电池响应并持续调整后续决策。基于PyBaMM构建覆盖5种电池化学体系、11组参数集的大规模仿真语料，将状态-动作-响应轨迹转为结构化过程数据用于训练。采用两阶段策略：SFT从仿真示范中建立电池控制语义与可行决策先验；GRPO将智能体对齐到涵盖能量吞吐、老化缓解与运行约束的多目标控制目标。

Result: 闭环仿真显示，在匹配的能量吞吐要求下，该智能体在异构电池配置中相较传统充电基线与基于优化的策略均获得更长的循环寿命。

Conclusion: 该研究为将LLM智能体用作电池管理中的序贯决策者提供了基于仿真的概念验证，向语言模型驱动的物理能源系统控制迈出一步。

Abstract: Fast charging of lithium-ion batteries is a multi-objective control problem that requires reducing charging time and sustaining high energy throughput while suppressing accelerated degradation and safety-critical electrochemical reactions. Conventional protocols are easy to implement but have limited ability to adapt to variations in battery chemistry, operating conditions, and aging state. Model-based optimization and conventional data-driven control often require accurate models, costly iterative search, or task-specific policies. We develop a large language model (LLM)-empowered intelligent agent for adaptive battery fast-charging control. In this closed-loop framework, the agent interprets structured battery states, generates executable charging actions, observes the resulting battery response, and continuously adjusts subsequent decisions. We construct a large-scale simulation corpus with PyBaMM across five battery chemistries and 11 parameter sets and convert the resulting state-action-response trajectories into structured process data for agent training. Our two-stage strategy combines supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). SFT establishes battery-control semantics and feasible decision priors from simulation-derived demonstrations. GRPO aligns the agent with a multi-objective control objective covering energy throughput, degradation mitigation, and operational constraints. Closed-loop simulations show that, under matched energy-throughput requirements, the agent achieves longer cycling life than conventional charging baselines and optimization-based strategies across heterogeneous battery configurations. This study provides a simulation-grounded proof of concept for using LLM agents as sequential decision makers in battery management and takes a step toward language-model-enabled control of physical energy systems.

</details>


### [80] [Probabilities of Causation in Nondeterministic Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18245)
*David S. Watson,Sander Beckers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究非确定性因果模型中因果概率的识别问题，提出将观测与干预数据表示为隐随机机制类型上的矩约束，通过约束优化计算反事实量的最小/最大值，在若干假设下给出紧致解析界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典因果概率（如PN、PS、PNS）的界限大多建立在确定性因果语义之上，即假定机制在给定全部潜变量后是确定性的。然而许多真实场景中机制本质上仍具随机性，确定性假设下的界可能不再适用甚至失去意义，因此需要针对非确定性因果模型重新刻画可识别区域。

Method: 针对二值处理与二值结果，构建通用识别框架：将观测分布与干预分布表述为关于潜在随机机制类型的矩约束，并将反事实量的最小/最大取值转化为约束优化问题求解；在此框架下，针对契合非确定性模型的若干结构假设推导出因果概率的紧致解析界。

Result: 所得识别区域往往比确定性模型下的对应结果更具信息量；在半合成数据集实验和个性化治疗决策案例中验证了其实用价值；并厘清了经典界与��确定性因果语义之间的关系。

Conclusion: 研究推广了既有工作，建立了从定性结构约束到可计算部分识别区域的统一途径，阐明了确定性假设如何影响因果概率的可识别性。

Abstract: We study probabilities of causation in nondeterministic causal models, where mechanisms may remain intrinsically stochastic even after all latent variables are fixed. Focusing on binary treatments and outcomes, we develop a generic identification program that represents observational and interventional data as moment constraints on latent stochastic mechanism types and computes minimal/maximal values of counterfactual quantities via constrained optimization. Within this framework, we derive sharp analytic bounds on the probabilities of causation under several assumptions tailored to nondeterministic models. Resulting identification regions are often more informative than those attainable under deterministic alternatives. We illustrate the practical relevance of these results in a case study on personalized treatment decisions and experiments on semi-synthetic datasets. Our results clarify how classical bounds relate to nondeterministic causal semantics, generalizing previous works and providing a unified route from qualitative structural restrictions to computable partial identification regions.

</details>


### [81] [Dancing Tokens on the Vocabulary Network from LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18248)
*Yuan-Chao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文从六个开源LLM的输入嵌入构建相似度网络，将生成过程映射到该网络上，发现嵌入网络具有无标度、重尾且高度碎片化的拓扑，生成并非沿网络游走，并据此提出可作推理期退化监测的观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM逐token生成，但单次生成与预训练所习得的几何结构之间的关系尚不清楚，LLM的可解释性仍是难题；作者希望以图学习视角在微观层面理解生成。

Method: 对六个开源LLM的输入嵌入构建相似度网络；对照具有匹配协方差的随机嵌入网络；考察蒸馏对拓扑的影响；统计连续生成token是否互为图邻居并与打乱顺序对比；逐层使用logit lens追踪token定位。

Result: 嵌入网络呈无标度、重尾，但比匹配协方差的随机嵌入更碎片化；蒸馏几乎不改变拓扑（输入嵌入基本被继承），知识空间由预训练决定；连续生成token很少是图邻居，多数模型中不高于打乱顺序的频率，说明生成不是预定义网络上的游走，尽管所访问token集中于其中；logit lens显示多数模型中token在较深层才稳定，重复性模型局限于小的领域子图。

Conclusion: 生成过程与嵌入网络拓扑解耦，知识空间由预训练确定；上述观测量可作为推理期的退化监测指标，为从图学习的节点/链路预测角度微观理解LLM生成提供新视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) generate text one token at a time, yet how a particular generation relates to the geometry learned in pre-training remains unclear. The fundamental explainability of LLMs is elusive. Here, we build a similarity network from the input embeddings of six open LLMs and map generation onto it. The token-embedding network is scale-free while heavy-tailed, as are random embeddings with matched covariance, but far more fragmented. Distillation barely changes this topology because input embeddings are largely inherited. The knowledge space is thus deterministic from pre-training. Consecutive generated tokens are rarely graph neighbours and, in most models, no more often than in shuffled order, so generation is not a walk on the predefined network, although the tokens it visits are concentrated within it. Through the logit lens in a layer- wise manner, tokens settle late in depth in most models, and repetitive models remain confined to small domain-specific sub-graphs. These observables could serve as inference-time monitors of degeneration. We thus provide a fresh perspective on understanding LLM generation in the microscopic way from node or link prediction by graph learning.

</details>


### [82] [Experts Across Schemas: Agent-Guided Mixture-of-Relational-Experts for Cross-Database Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18315)
*Hiren Madhu,Sukanta Ganguly,Kiran Srinivasan,Smita Krishnaswamy,Rex Ying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MoREGT混合专家图Transformer与标签盲LLM智能体，以更少预训练数据在RelBench上取得最优跨库预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同Web平台的关系数据库模式各异，阻碍预测模型跨平台迁移；现有关系基础模型靠不断扩大预训练语料提升性能，本文探究更好架构结合目标库特有关系特征能否以更少数据取得相当收益。

Method: MoREGT将行与单元格均建模为token，在注意力中加入行的log-age与外键关系名称，并依据列元数据而非取值将单元格与行路由至专家；辅以标签盲LLM智能体，读取目标库模式与列统计，生成经验证的单行派生列，且不接触标签或模型分数。

Result: 在6个数据库留一库预训练的21个RelBench任务上，MoREGT骨干较同数据预训练的Relational Transformer提升2.25点平均AUROC与5.83点平均nMAE；智能体派生列将平均AUROC由78.45提升至79.63，平均nMAE由27.91降至26.98，为对比方法中最优，包括预训练数据库量约400倍的RT-J。

Conclusion: 架构改进与目标库特定特征工程可部分替代大规模预训练语料，在显著更少数据下实现跨库关系预测的领先性能。

Abstract: Web platforms record user and item activity in relational databases whose schemas differ from platform to platform, which makes it difficult to transfer predictive models between them. Relational foundation models address this through cross-database pretraining, yet recent improvements have relied on increasingly large pretraining corpora. We ask whether a better architecture, together with features specific to the target database, can deliver comparable gains from far less pretraining data. We introduce MoREGT, a mixture-of-relational-experts graph transformer that represents both rows and cells as tokens, adds the log-ages of rows and the names of foreign-key relationships to attention, and routes cells and rows to experts by column metadata rather than by values. A label-blind LLM agent complements the model: it reads the target database's schema and column statistics and adds validated single-row derived columns, without access to labels or model scores. On 21 RelBench tasks with leave-one-database-out pretraining on six databases, the MoREGT backbone improves on the Relational Transformer pretrained on the same databases by 2.25 points of mean AUROC and 5.83 points of mean nMAE. The agent's columns raise mean AUROC from 78.45 to 79.63 and lower mean nMAE from 27.91 to 26.98, the highest means on both metrics among the compared methods, including RT-J, which pretrains on about 400 times as many databases.

</details>


### [83] [Causal AI-Driven Secure mmWave Beam Alignment with Defensive Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18321)
*Nasir Khan,Asmaa Abdallah,Abdulkadir Celik,Ahmed M. Eltawil,Sinem Coleri,Cicek Cavdar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出因果感知的深度学习波束对齐框架：用两阶段因果波束选择学习RSSI与最优波束索引间的贝叶斯图并筛选最小特征集，再用防御性变分自编码器（DVAE）净化受扰输入并端到端微调分类器，兼顾可解释性、泛化性、低开销与对抗鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的毫米波波束对齐方法忽视输入输出间的因果关系，导致可解释性差、泛化能力弱、波束扫描开销大；同时此类模型易受对抗攻击，接收功率的微小扰动即可误导波束分类器。

Method: 1) 将因果发现引入波束对齐，构建两阶段因果波束选择算法：学习刻画RSSI输入与最优波束索引目标之间依赖关系的贝叶斯图，并据此为DL分类器挑选最小相关特征子集；2) 引入防御性变分自编码器（DVAE），将可能被扰动的输入映射回干净版本；3) 在DVAE重建的RSSI表示上对下游波束分类器进行端到端微调以提升预测精度。

Result: 因果波束选择精度与传统方法相当，但单样本计算开销比SHAP低829倍；相比传统DL波束选择与穷举搜索，波束扫描开销分别降低59.4%和89.8%。DVAE防御可有效去噪对抗输入，在不同扰动界下维持85%准确率，在未扰动RSSI上保持基线性能，并在多种攻击场景下平均超越防御性蒸馏方案15–20%。

Conclusion: 因果发现与防御性生成模型相结合，可同时提升毫米波波束对齐的效率、可解释性与对抗鲁棒性，是替代XAI特征选择与常规防御方案的有效途径。

Abstract: Efficient and secure beam alignment is essential for mmWave communication systems. However, existing deep learning (DL)-based beam alignment methods often neglect causal relationships between inputs and outputs, resulting in limited interpretability, poor generalization, and excessive beam sweeping. Moreover, these DL solutions are vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where small perturbations in received power measurements can mislead beam classifiers. To overcome these issues, we propose a causally-aware DL framework integrating causal discovery into beam alignment. A two-stage causal beam selection algorithm learns a Bayesian graph capturing dependencies between received signal strength indicators (RSSI) as inputs and the optimal beam index as the target variable, then uses it to select a minimal set of relevant input features for the DL classifier. To mitigate adversarial attacks, we introduce a defensive variational autoencoder (DVAE) that maps potentially perturbed inputs back to their clean versions. End-to-end fine-tuning of the downstream beam classifier on the DVAE-reconstructed RSSI representations further boosts prediction accuracy. Simulation results show that causal beam selection achieves accuracy comparable to conventional methods while providing an \(\mathbf{829\times}\) lower per-sample computational cost than SHAP (Shapley Additive Explanations), a widely used explainable AI (XAI)-based feature selection method. It further reduces beam-sweeping overhead by \(\mathbf{59.4\%}\) and \(\mathbf{89.8\%}\), compared to conventional DL-based beam selection and exhaustive search, respectively. The DVAE-based defense effectively denoises adversarial inputs, sustaining 85\% accuracy across different perturbation bounds, retains baseline performance on unperturbed RSSI inputs and surpasses defensive distillation defense scheme by 15--20\% on average under different attack scenarios.

</details>


### [84] [VLAN: Vision-Language Accessible Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18346)
*Jie Hu,Yu Zheng,Wenzhi Wu,Weixing Feng,Cunlei Cheng,Jiaming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出VLAN基准，首次将可达性视为"用户条件依赖"而非场景固有属性，在1000个场景、2万条轨迹、16万关键帧上为腿式/轮式机器人及视障/行动障碍人群提供具身特定的可通行性标注与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可通行性取决于智能体的具身形态：对某类身体可走的路径对另一类则不可达。然而现有基准仅在固定且有限的具身上评估可通行性，缺乏跨用户画像（机器人、行动不便者等）的安全与可达性对齐评估。

Method: 构建Vision-Language Accessible Navigation（VLAN）基准：涵盖1000场景、20K导航轨迹、160K关键帧，覆盖多样光照、环境条件、场景语义与路径几何；为每条轨迹提供面向腿式与轮式智能体（人形、四足机器人、视障/行动障碍人士）的具身特定动作与可达性特征；将日常障碍与可达性标签分为Passable、Bypass、HighRisk、Blocked四类；设计目标到达、碰撞感知与可达性对齐三类指标，对现代视觉语言模型进行评测。

Result: 评测显示现代视觉语言模型往往无法在不同用户画像间一致地调整障碍相关性判断、路径选择与安全准则，在跨具身可达性任务上表现不稳定。

Conclusion: 可达性感知导航需要专门的数据集、任务与指标，超越传统具身导航评估范式；数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: Accessible mobility is user-conditioned rather than scene-intrinsic, as walkable paths for one embodiment are inaccessible to another. Yet, existing benchmarks evaluate traversability under a fixed and limited embodiment. We present Vision-Language Accessible Navigation (VLAN), containing 1,000 scenes, 20K navigation trajectories, and 160K keyframes across diverse lighting, environmental conditions, scene semantics, and path geometries. For each trajectory, VLAN provides embodiment-specific actions and accessibility features for legged and wheeled agents, including humanoid and quadruped robots and people with visual or mobility impairments, while everyday obstacles and accessibility labels are categorized as Passable, Bypass, HighRisk, and Blocked. Unlike goal-only evaluation, our benchmark measures physical safety and alignment with user-specific constraints via goal-reaching, collision-aware, and accessibility-aligned metrics. We benchmark modern vision-language models and find they often fail to consistently adapt obstacle relevance, path selection, and safety criteria across user profiles, suggesting accessibility-aware navigation requires dedicated datasets, tasks, and metrics beyond conventional embodied navigation evaluation. All data and code open-source at https://github.com/InSAI-Lab/VLAN.

</details>


### [85] [VelServe: Taming Decode Jitters in LLM Inference with Tail-Calibrated Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18392)
*Ishaan Datta,Alind Khare,Koushik Kumar Dey,Ratnesh Jamidar,Anand Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对交互式LLM服务中解码抖动（decode jitter）导致的TTFT/TBT SLO违约问题，提出调度器，通过在线校准每个解码步可承载的prefill工作量、预留尾部余量并配合最短提示优先等策略，显著提升SLO goodput。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式与软实时应用要求可预测的token交付，需满足TTFT与TBT的延迟SLO。作者发现聚合式与分离式服务均存在解码抖动：单个慢步会拖慢整批请求，而长响应跨数百步，因此即便偶发超时也会造成大量SLO违约。现有SLO感知调度器把预填充预算设为"预测延迟恰好达标"，不留任何误预测或主机停顿的余量；分离式服务虽以容量代价满足平均TBT，但承接新请求的准入步仍会迟到。

Method: 提出调度器，利用自身迭代延迟的实测尾部来校准每个解码步所携带的prefill工作量：由SLO决定慢步的允许概率，据此在每次迭代中预留足够headroom以防超时并满足目标解码延迟。由于headroom会拖慢prefill，采用最短等待提示优先（shortest waiting prompt first）调度——当TTFT截止时间随提示长度增长时，被延后的长提示拥有最多可支配时间。在分离式部署下，对请求准入进行间隔控制，使任何请求所等待的慢准入步数量不超过其P99目标允许的范围。

Result: 在三种attention家族与多种负载下，在Qwen3-8B与DeepSeek-V4-Flash上达到最强基线2.4–2.9×的SLO goodput，在DeepSeek-V2-Lite上至少4.4×。在八GPU上进行prefill与decode分离部署时，满足SLO的请求数量是SOTA基线的两倍以上。

Conclusion: 以迭代延迟尾部为依据的headroom校准与配套的提示排序、准入间隔策略，能够有效抑制解码抖动对批内全部请求的连锁影响，在聚合与分离两种服务形态下同时改善SLO达成率与goodput。

Abstract: Interactive LLM services must meet latency service-level objectives (SLOs) for the time to first token (TTFT) and the time between tokens (TBT), as streaming and soft real-time applications require predictable token delivery. We identify decode jitters in both aggregated and disaggregated serving. A jitter delays every request in the batch, and long responses span hundreds of steps, so even rare overruns cause many SLO violations. In aggregated serving, SLO-aware schedulers set each step's prefill budget until its predicted latency just meets the TBT target, leaving no room for mispredictions or host stalls. Disaggregation meets average TBT targets at a cost in capacity, yet the steps that admit new requests still run late.
  We present \sysname, a scheduler that calibrates how much prefill work each decode step carries using the measured tail of its own iteration latency. The SLO determines how rare a late step must be, and \sysname leaves enough headroom in each iteration to prevent overruns and meet the target decode latency. This headroom slows prefill, so \sysname serves the shortest waiting prompt first. When TTFT deadlines grow with prompt length, the long prompts it delays have the most time to spare. Under disaggregation, it spaces admissions so that no request waits on more slow admission steps than its P99 target allows. On three attention families and varying workloads, \sysname sustains 2.4--2.9$\times$ the SLO goodput of the strongest baseline on Qwen3-8B and DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and at least 4.4$\times$ on DeepSeek-V2-Lite. With prefill and decode disaggregated on eight GPUs, \sysname meets the SLO for more than twice as many requests as state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [86] [Smart Home Agents Make Capable but Unpredictable Grid Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18395)
*Christopher Terrazas,Eduardo Cotilla-Sanchez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次对智能家居AI代理进行覆盖"停电前—停电中—停电后"全过程的韧性分析，并通过实机停电仿真揭示代理可在停电中充当电网运营商，同时暴露多类新型失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能家居在电网边缘日益普及，但失效响应研究严重不足；智能家居位于电网边缘，故障易级联扩散，停电时用户需手动完成负载均衡与设备恢复等排障工作，缺乏自动化韧性机制。

Method: 构建首个面向智能家居代理的全停电周期韧性分析，在真实运行环境中设计一套停电仿真套件，分别在停电前、停电中、停电后三阶段度量韧性表现，并评估多类模型代理作为"电网运营商"的能力。

Result: 仿真表明智能家居代理能够在一定程度上充当电网运营商参与停电管理；同时识别出若干新的失效类别，反映当前所有被测模型均存在共性局限。

Conclusion: 该工作为"智能家居代理作为电网运营商"奠定基础，有望提升电网边缘整体韧性；并指出面向停电响应的代理能力仍是开放问题。

Abstract: AI-enabled smart homes are becoming more common across the grid edge. However, there has not been much work on failure response. This area is critical because smart homes operate on the grid edge where failures can cascade down. If a power outage happens within a smart home, a user has to manually troubleshoot their smart home to include tasks such as power load balance and device recovery. We conduct the first resilience analysis of smart home agents across a full power outage, providing a suite of outage simulations in a live environment that measures resilience before, during, and post power outage. We show that smart home agents can act as grid operators in power outages and identify several new failure categories highlighting the current limitations across all of our models. These findings establish the groundwork for smart home agents as grid operators to increase overall resilience on the grid edge.

</details>


### [87] [LECAT: Measuring LLM Category Learning from Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18419)
*Jimin Mun,Maarten Sap,Yanai Elazar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 LeCat 框架，从"记忆—泛化—情境灵活运用"三层面评估 LLM 对类别知识的习得，并追溯预训练文本中知识的"表达形式"（逐字重复、例外等）如何影响学习；发现逐字规则式陈述削弱灵活类别学习，而多样化知识表达混合可带来更稳健的类别知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能自然习得并灵活应用类别知识（如"飞机快—红色飞机也快—但坏飞机不适用"），而 LLM 如何获得这种类别知识尚不清楚；现有研究缺乏对"学习能力分层评估"与"预训练数据中知识表达形式如何起作用"的系统分析。

Method: 提出两部分 LeCat 框架：(1) 将类别知识学习拆分为域内记忆、对未见实例的泛化、以及在恰当情境中灵活使用知识三类情形进行评估；(2) 追踪预训练文本中知识的不同表达形式（逐字重复、例外等）如何促成学习。先用 199 组精选的类别-特征对评估开源预训练模型并分析其训练数据（考察上下文与类别-特征共现似然 PMI 等信号）；再从零训练一个 68M 模型，精确控制其训练数据中的类别信息，以隔离因果效应。

Result: 对预训练模型的分析显示：更强的上下文与类别-特征共现似然（PMI）有助于大模型学习；逐字规则式陈述会削弱灵活的类别学习。从零训练的受控实验进一步确认：逐字重复降低灵活学习效果，而多样化知识表达混合则带来更稳健的类别学习。情境灵活性对模型而言始终较难。

Conclusion: 类别知识在预训练文本中的表达方式显著影响 LLM 习得稳健类别知识的程度；上下文灵活性仍是模型的持续短板。研究强调数据表达多样性对类别知识泛化与灵活应用的关键作用。

Abstract: Humans know that airplanes are fast, that this extends to red airplanes, but that it does not hold for broken airplanes. How do LLMs acquire category knowledge that humans learn and apply effortlessly? We introduce LeCat, a two-part framework to analyze LLM's Learning of Category knowledge. First, we use LeCat to evaluate models on their ability to learn category knowledge, distinguishing between three cases: memorization of in-domain category knowledge, generalization to unseen instances, and ability to flexibly use knowledge in appropriate contexts. Then, we use LeCat to trace how this knowledge can be learned in pretraining from text that may encode category knowledge in various ways, such as verbatim repetition or exceptions, which we refer to as forms of "knowledge expression". We first assess open-source pretrained models using 199 curated category-feature pairs and analyze their training data. Our findings suggest that strengthening context and category-feature co-occurrence likelihood (PMI) improve learning for larger models and verbatim rule-like statements diminish flexible category learning. To systematically isolate these effects, we then train a 68M model from scratch with precise control over the categorical information each sees in their training data, confirming that verbatim repetition reduces flexible learning but mixture of diverse knowledge expressions lead to more robust category learning. Together, our findings highlight that while contextual flexibility is persistently difficult for models, how category knowledge is expressed in pretraining greatly impacts models' acquisition of robust category knowledge.

</details>


### [88] [Towards Explainable Sleep Stage Classification using Single-Channel Electroencephalogram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18434)
*Gurinder Kaur Sandhu,Jude Koenig,Ankita Shukla,Shruti Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究扩展SleepXAI框架，系统比较五种XAI方法在单通道EEG睡眠分期中的归因保真度，以AASM频带为临床参考，发现Integrated Gradients在Sleep-EDF各睡眠期与DREAMT各AHI组中均表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动睡眠分期已达较高基准准确率，但因缺乏可解释性而难以临床落地；现有研究缺乏对不同XAI方法的系统比较，尤其缺少以临床依据为参考的归因保真度评估。

Method: 扩展SleepXAI框架，比较五种XAI方法；聚焦更易临床部署的单通道EEG；采用与AASM定义EEG频带的Spearman秩相关作为归因保真度的临床参考信号；在Sleep-EDF与DREAMT数据集上实验，并按AHI将DREAMT受试者分为四个OSA严重度组。

Result: SleepXAI模型在两个数据集上均取得良好分类性能；Integrated Gradients（IG）在Sleep-EDF全部五个睡眠期及DREAMT全部AHI组中均获得最高平均归因保真度，在Spearman秩相关指标上优于其他方法。

Conclusion: 归因方法的选择是确保自动睡眠分期系统可靠且临床可解释的关键因素，应作为系统设计的核心考量。

Abstract: Automated sleep staging has achieved strong bench- mark accuracy; however, clinical adoption remains limited due to a lack of interpretability. In this work, we fill the research gap by extending the SleepXAI sleep staging framework to systematically compare five different XAI methods. Further, attribution fidelity is evaluated using Spearman rank correlation with AASM-defined EEG frequency bands, serving as clinically grounded reference signals. In this paper, we focus on single- channel Electroencephalogram (EEG), which is more amenable to practical clinical deployment compared to full polysomnography setups. Experiments are conducted on the Sleep-EDF benchmark dataset and the DREAMT dataset. In DREAMT, we stratified subjects based on OSA into four Apnea-Hypopnea Index (AHI) severity groups. We observed that the SleepXAI model achieves strong classification performance on both datasets. Among the evaluated methods, Integrated Gradients (IG) consistently achieves the highest mean attribution fidelity across all five sleep stages on Sleep-EDF and across all AHI groups on DREAMT, outperforming all other evaluated methods under the Spearman rank correlation metric. These findings highlight the importance of attribution method selection as a key factor for ensuring reliable and clinically interpretable automated sleep staging systems.

</details>


### [89] [When to Trust Simulated Patient Responses:Support-Gated World Models for Vasopressor Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18458)
*Yifan Jin,Tao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出 Support-Gated World-Model 决策支持：在 propose–simulate–refine 智能体上加入“行为支持度 + 集成分歧”联合门控，对支持不足或分歧过大的模拟预测进行抑制，无合格候选时弃权；在 eICU-CRD（70 家医院、30,700 条决策转移、14 家医院为留出测试集）上刻画了临床模拟的可靠性–覆盖率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床世界模型可让语言模型智能体接触预测的患者反应，但当治疗缺乏充分记录支持或医院不熟悉时，模拟不可靠。作者希望在不改动智能体主流程的前提下，为模拟结果提供可量化的可靠性保障，避免基于不可信模拟给出建议。

Method: 在 propose–simulate–refine 智能体上加装轻量门控：(1) 循环动力学集成模型，预测对已记录的去甲肾上腺素剂量类别的短时程生理响应；(2) 行为模型，估计观察性动作支持度。当支持度不足或集成分歧过大时，界面不输出该预测；若无候选通过门控，则整体弃权。评估使用 eICU-CRD，含 70 家医院、30,700 条决策转移，其中 14 家医院留出用于测试，并做组件消融。

Result: 在 62.0% 事实性转移覆盖率下，联合门控的 MAP 平均绝对误差为 7.3 mmHg，而同覆盖率下随机选择为 9.2 mmHg。门控智能体在 81.0% 决策覆盖率下与记录动作的一致率为 60.0%；无门控集成智能体为 55.0% 一致率、99.5% 覆盖率。消融显示联合门控的事实性风险低于任一单独门控。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了选择性临床模拟中的可靠性–覆盖率权衡：门控以牺牲部分覆盖率换取更高的预测精度与建议一致性，且联合门控优于单门控。作者明确限定：这些结果不识别个体化治疗效应，也未证明患者结局改善。

Abstract: Clinical world models expose language-model agents to predicted patient responses, but simulations can be unreliable for poorly supported treatments or unfamiliar hospitals. We introduce Support-Gated World-Model decision support, a lightweight extension of a propose--simulate--refine agent. A recurrent dynamics ensemble predicts short-horizon physiological responses to documented norepinephrine dose categories, while a behavior model estimates observational action support. The interface withholds predictions with insufficient support or excessive ensemble disagreement and abstains when no candidate qualifies. Evaluation on eICU-CRD includes 70 hospitals and 30,700 decision transitions, with 14 hospitals held out for testing. At 62.0% factual-transition coverage, joint gating yields a MAP mean absolute error of 7.3 mmHg, compared with 9.2 mmHg for random selection at the same coverage. The gated agent achieves 60.0% agreement with recorded actions at 81.0% decision coverage, compared with 55.0% agreement and 99.5% coverage for an ungated ensemble agent. Component ablations show lower factual risk with joint gating than with either gate alone.These results characterize a reliability--coverage trade-off in selective clinical simulation; they do not identify individualized treatment effects or establish improved patient outcomes.

</details>


### [90] [Typing Matters: Muscle, Language and Typing Behaviour in Live Keystroke Decoding from sEMG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18464)
*Abhilash Durgam,Nyle Siddiqui,Mubarak Shah,Qiushi Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种完全因果、零前瞻的sEMG打字解码器，基于状态空间模型（SSM）将打字解码与自回归语言模型融合，并引入retokenization方法构建打字语料，在emg2qwerty跨用户基准上取得25.13%字符错误率（相对提升5.4%，参数量少6.7倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为现有研究将sEMG打字建模为"信号推断问题"（固定窗口读字符、离线报告字符错误率）丢失了问题本质：解码器同时受肌肉、输入语言及打字行为（typo、纠错、退格、换行）约束，且实用系统需在手动过程中实时预测按键。现有系统存在两点不足：(1) 固定窗口处理sEMG、窗口间不传递信息，丢弃会话累积的上下文；(2) 缺乏对驱动打字的语言结构的理解，每个按键仅从肌肉信号推断。

Method: 提出基于状态空间模型（SSM）的完全因果、无前瞻sEMG打字解码器：单一模型不分区窗口地解码整个会话，并以已输入内容为条件；将传统打字解码器与高效自回归语言模型融合，使模型同时具备sEMG与语言理解能力。为按字符级（而非整词）训练，引入retokenization方法，将普通散文改写为含按键、typo、退格、换行的类人打字序列，把WikiText转换为大规模打字语料。

Result: 在emg2qwerty跨用户基准上达到25.13%的字符错误率（SOTA），较此前最佳相对提升5.4%，参数量减少6.7倍，且严格因果运行、不使用未来上下文。

Conclusion: 将sEMG打字重新定义为受语言与打字行为约束的会话级因果解码问题，并以SSM融合语言模型、配合retokenization构造打字语料，是实现更小、更强、可实时部署的无键盘输入解码器的有效路径。

Abstract: Surface electromyography (sEMG) from the wrists can recover typed text without a keyboard. It is usually posed as a signal-inference problem: characters are read out of the muscle signal and character error rate is reported offline. We argue that this framing removes most of the problem. The decoder is constrained simultaneously by the muscles, by the language being typed, and by typing behaviour such as typos, corrections, backspaces and line breaks, and a usable system must predict keys live while the hand keeps moving. Existing systems fall short on two counts: (1) they process sEMG in fixed windows and carry nothing from one window to the next, discarding context accumulated over a session, and (2) they lack any understanding of the linguistic structure that drives typing, so every keystroke is inferred from the muscle signal alone. We propose a fully causal, zero-lookahead sEMG typing decoder built on a state space model (SSM), in which a single model decodes a whole session without windowed partitions, conditioned on what has already been typed. We achieve this by fusing a traditional typing decoder with an efficient autoregressive language model, instilling both sEMG and linguistic understanding in one model. To train such a model for per-character typing rather than whole-word text, we introduce a retokenization method that rewrites ordinary prose as human-like typing, with keystrokes, typos, backspaces and line breaks included, converting WikiText into a large typing corpus where none existed. Our model, having never seen a single sEMG-to-character transcript in typing, achieves a state-of-the-art character error rate of 25.13% on emg2qwerty's cross-user benchmark, a 5.4% relative improvement over the previous best with 6.7 times fewer parameters, while running strictly causally with no future context.

</details>


### [91] [ARCHER: From Rationale Imitation to Executable Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18494)
*Dhruv Dixit,Khushi Patel,Congcong Xu,Min Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCHER：面向小型语言模型的"动作轨迹+非对称分层强化学习"两阶段框架，将自由CoT编译为可执行类型化动作轨迹，并用分组门控轨迹奖励精炼策略，显著提升数学推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型适合本地、低成本、隐私敏感部署，但在需要分解、精确计算、工具调用与验证的数学推理上依然不可靠。现有监督目标多为模仿解释性文本，未直接学习"工具接地的推理策略"。

Method: 两阶段框架：①将自由形式CoT编译为类型化动作轨迹（推理步骤、计算器调用、确定性运行时观测、验证动作、最终答案），把监督目标从文本模仿转为工具接地策略学习；②采用非对称分层强化学习，使用分组且条件门控的轨迹奖励，涵盖答案正确性、工具接地、过程纪律与轨迹对齐四项。

Result: 在五个10B以下标准骨干上，宏平均准确率较最强工具增强基线提升5.51个百分点，其中MATH-500 +8.95、AIME-2024 +5.33、AIME-2025 +5.34；在两个额外7B推理专用骨干（经推理强化学习或蒸馏后）上仍验证可执行接地带来增益。

Conclusion: 可执行监督加奖励引导的行为精炼，是训练紧凑型数学推理模型的通用且有效的范式，且在推理专用后训练之后仍具互补价值。

Abstract: Small language models are attractive for local, low-cost, and privacy-sensitive deployment, but they remain unreliable on mathematical reasoning tasks that require decomposition, exact computation, tool use, and verification. We introduce \textbf{ARCHER}: \textbf{A}ction-trace \textbf{R}einforcement for \textbf{C}alculator-grounded \textbf{H}ierarchical \textbf{E}xecutable \textbf{R}easoning, a two-stage framework for training small language models (SLMs) to produce executable and auditable reasoning trajectories. In the first stage, ARCHER compiles free-form chain-of-thought rationales into typed action traces containing reasoning steps, calculator calls, deterministic runtime observations, verification actions, and final answers. This shifts the supervision target from imitating explanatory text to learning a tool-grounded reasoning policy. In the second stage, ARCHER refines this policy through asymmetric hierarchical reinforcement learning, using grouped and conditionally gated trajectory rewards for answer correctness, tool grounding, process discipline, and trace alignment. Across five standard sub-10B backbones, ARCHER improves over the strongest tool-augmented baseline by 5.51 percentage points on macro-average across benchmark accuracies, including gains of 8.95 points on MATH-500 and 5.33 and 5.34 points on AIME-2024 and AIME-2025. Evaluations on two additional reasoning-specialized 7B backbones further show that executable grounding remains beneficial after reasoning-focused reinforcement learning or distillation. These results demonstrate that executable supervision and reward-guided behavioral refinement provide a general and effective training paradigm for compact mathematical reasoners.

</details>


### [92] [Can Mechanistic Interpretability Help Explain LLM Agent Failures? A Case Study on Web Element Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18514)
*Mohamed Aghzal,Mor Geva,Gregory J. Stein,Ziyu Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文考察机械可解释性(MI)工具在解释LLM智能体真实失败时的局限。以网页智能体幻觉出不存在的动作为案例，对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct进行分析：线性探针可在生成前预测幻觉(0.80 AUC)，沿探针方向引导可纠正85%幻觉但破坏7%有效动作；然而该解释仅是局部的——探针方向对提示格式敏感，电路难以识别（计算分布于多组件多位置且依赖信息因例而异），从而挑战标准MI方法(电路发现与特征辨识)的基本假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MI在解释LLM内部计算上取得进展，但在解释基于LLM的智能体决策过程上尚未展现同等成功；论文旨在揭示MI工具应用于真实智能体失败时所面临的挑战。

Method: 选取一个具体失败模式——网页智能体幻觉出网站上不存在的动作，对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct在与实时网站交互过程中的表现进行一系列机械分析（线性探针、方向引导/steering、电路识别）。

Result: 在最后一个提示token上训练的线性探针能在生成开始前预测幻觉(0.80 AUC)；沿探针方向引导纠正了85%的幻觉，但破坏了7%的有效动作。但解释仅部分成立：探针方向对提示格式敏感，动作表示方式稍作改动后引导效果大幅衰减；电路识别困难，因为计算分散于多个组件与位置，且智能体所依赖的信息在不同样本间变化。

Conclusion: 这些观察挑战了标准MI方法（电路发现与特征辨识）所依赖的假设，论文据此提出未来方向，使此类方法更适合解释基于LLM的智能体的失败。

Abstract: Mechanistic Interpretability (MI) has made significant progress in explaining the internal computations of Large Language Models (LLMs); however, these advances have yet to demonstrate comparable success in explaining the decision-making processes of LLM-based agents. This paper examines the challenges MI tools face when applied to realistic agentic failures. We focus on one such failure: web agents hallucinating actions that are not available on a website. We apply a series of mechanistic analyzes to Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct as it interacts with live websites. We find that a linear probe trained on the last prompt token can predict hallucinations before generation begins (0.80 AUC) and steering along a probe direction corrects 85\% of hallucinations, but disrupts 7\% of valid actions. However, these results only provide a partial explanation of why the agent hallucinates. The probe direction is sensitive to prompt formatting, losing much of its steering effect after a minor change to how actions are represented. Additionally, circuit identification can be difficult as the computation is distributed across multiple components and positions, and the information the agent relies on varies across examples. We discuss how these observations challenge the assumptions underlying standard MI methods for circuit discovery and feature identification, and outline future directions for making these methods better suited to explain failures in LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [93] [Alignment by Socialization: Learning Tradeoffs Under Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18531)
*Thomas Brennan,Andrew W. Lo,Chaoyi Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究演化选择如何塑造AI智能体在"完成任务"与"遵守行为准则"之间的取舍，并考察经验能否使这些取舍符合人类判断、以及能否抵御后续训练压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体需在完成任务的同时遵守行为准则，但两类目标可能冲突。作者关注的是：这种冲突下的选择如何被训练环境影响，以及"社会化"（与人类判断对齐）能否通过经验积累而稳固、抗逆。

Method: 构建演化模型：智能体副本面对共同的不确定奖励状态，只有采取被奖励动作者才繁殖；证明长期增长率在每个动作被采取频率等于环境奖励频率时最大化。比较三种策略——仅任务策略、固定线性策略、学习型策略。并结合小规模神经网络实验，考察训练目标与记忆容量（有限记忆）对结果的影响。

Result: (1) 仅任务策略在只奖励完成度的沙盒中表现优异，但当行为准则重要时失效；(2) 固定线性策略随其"赌注"上升而愈发偏向完成任务，即便人类从不奖励此行为；(3) 学习型策略收敛于环境所奖励的权衡，人类提供奖励时即为"社会化"；积累经验使社会化在后续纯任务训练下更难逆转，但在主流倾向改变前仍可能出现违规，有限记忆会削弱这种保护；(4) 当单次违规即可终止部署时，硬性禁令可防患，但误设禁令亦有代价。

Conclusion: 对齐不仅取决于赋予智能体的目标，也取决于塑造并维持其行为的环境；训练环境本身是行为能否被社会化且保持稳固的关键因素。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) agents are expected to complete tasks while following rules of conduct, but these objectives can conflict. We study how evolutionary selection shapes an agent's choices between them, and whether experience can make those choices reflect human judgments and withstand subsequent pressures. In our model, copies of an agent face a common uncertain reward state, and only those taking the rewarded action reproduce. We show that long-run growth is maximized when each action is taken as often as the environment rewards it. We compare three policies. A task-only policy thrives in a sandbox rewarding completion alone but fails when conduct matters. A fixed linear policy increasingly favors task completion as its stakes rise, even where humans never reward it. A learned policy instead converges to the tradeoffs rewarded by its environment; when humans supply the rewards, we call this socialization. Accumulated experience makes socialization harder to reverse under subsequent task-only training, although violations can arise before the prevailing disposition changes, and finite memory weakens this protection. When a single violation can end a deployment, a hard prohibition can prevent it, but a mistaken prohibition can also be costly. Experiments with small neural networks illustrate how training targets and memory shape these outcomes. Our results suggest that alignment depends not only on the objectives assigned to agents, but also on the environments that shape and sustain their behavior.

</details>


### [94] [ModHarness-RL: Recasting Harnesses as Learnable and Evolving Programs for Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18533)
*Zelin He,Siyu Zhu,Jacob Helwig,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Shiyu Chang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ModHarness-RL，把智能体强化学习中的"harness"（模型外围脚手架）重构为可从数据学习、并随模型训练而进化的程序，通过学习Compression与Steering两个程序来提升留出集表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体RL方法多在手写、全程固定的harness下训练，但有效harness取决于领域、数据和模型，并应随模型训练不断变化，固定harness难以适配。

Method: 将交互记录为结构化State（一组带类型的块）；harness的两个核心组件写成作用于这些块的程序：Compression程序把累积历史组装进模型上下文，Steering程序在行为偏离时提供引导。RL训练中，从近期轨迹提出候选程序，经执行验证，并在历史轨迹上回放，使harness跟踪模型行为变化。在CodeSearch上深入实验，并分析ALFWorld与BIRD SQL，模型规模1.5B–8B。

Result: 所提出的Compression与Steering机制在多数设置下相较固定harness基线提升了留出集性能。

Conclusion: 智能体RL的harness可从任务数据中学习，并能随模型共同进化，而非依赖人工固定设计。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly relies on a harness, the scaffold around the model that decides what it sees of its own interaction and what guidance it receives. Most existing approaches train under a hand-written harness that stays fixed throughout. However, an effective harness depends on the domain, the data and the model, and keeps changing as the model trains. We introduce ModHarness-RL, which recasts the harness as programs that are learned from data and evolve as the model trains with RL. ModHarness-RL records the interaction as structured State, a set of typed blocks, and two core components of the harness are programs over these blocks, a Compression program that assembles the accumulated history into the model's context and a Steering program that supplies guidance when behavior deviates. During RL training, candidate programs are proposed from recent trajectories, verified by execution, and replayed over past trajectories, so the harness tracks how the model's behavior shifts. We study CodeSearch in depth and further analyze ALFWorld and BIRD SQL, with models from 1.5B to 8B. The proposed Compression and Steering mechanisms improve held-out performance over the fixed-harness baseline in most settings. These results suggest that a harness for agentic RL can be learned from the task's data and evolved with the model.

</details>


### [95] [Imitation Bootstrapped Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18543)
*Tony H. Zhang,Connor Mattson,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 IBMARL，将 IBRL 扩展到协作式多智能体，用 R2BC 训练模仿策略，并由集中式评论家集合在多智能体动作组合中选择，配合门控模仿惩罚，实现超越示范的 MARL 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 示范能加速单智能体 RL，但扩展到 MARL 很困难：单人难以同时遥操作多个智能体。R2BC 让单个示范者一次只控制一个智能体、其余按当前策略执行，缓解了操作难题，但其产生的模仿策略受示范质量限制，无法超越示范者。

Method: 将 Imitation Bootstrapped RL (IBRL) 推广到协作团队：(1) 用 R2BC 训练每个智能体的模仿策略；(2) 每步每智能体各收到来自模仿策略和 RL 策略的一个动作提议，由集中式评论家集合在所有智能体的二元组合上做选择；(3) 引入门控模仿惩罚——软化 Q 过滤的行为克隆损失，仅在评论家更认可模仿动作处将 RL 策略正则化向模仿策略靠拢；训练后各智能体独立执行自身 RL 策略，无需集中式评论家。

Result: 在四个协作式 MARL 基准上，IBMARL 在两个任务上以 2.6–4.2× 更快速度超越其模仿策略，在另两个任务上是唯一能超越模仿策略的方法，并在四个任务中的两个上超过示范者本身。使用真人示范时，IBMARL 仍优于纯模仿学习，且速度是最佳基线的两倍以上。

Conclusion: IBMARL 通过评论家引导的门控模仿正则化，将 R2BC 产生的有限质量示范转化为可超越示范的多智能体策略，且在真实人类示范下依然有效，去中心化执行、示范利用高效。

Abstract: Demonstrations are an effective way to accelerate single-agent reinforcement learning (RL), but extending this idea to multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is non-trivial as it is difficult and often impossible for a single person to teleoperate multiple agents simultaneously. Round-Robin Behavior Cloning (R2BC) addresses this by letting a single demonstrator control one agent at a time while the others execute their current policies. However, the resulting imitation policies are limited by the quality of the demonstrations. We propose Imitation Bootstrapped Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (IBMARL), a framework that extends Imitation Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning (IBRL) to cooperative teams and uses R2BC to train the imitation policies. At each time step, every agent receives one action proposal from its imitation policy and one from its RL policy, and an ensemble of centralized critics selects among the per-agent combinations of the two. A gated imitation penalty, a soft Q-filtered behavior cloning loss, regularizes each RL policy toward its imitation policy only where the critics value the imitation action more, so that after training each agent executes its RL policy alone, without the centralized critics. On four cooperative multi-agent RL benchmarks, IBMARL surpasses its imitation policies $2.6$-$4.2\times$ faster than demonstration-based baselines on two tasks, is the only method to surpass it on the other two, and exceeds the demonstrator itself on two of the four. With real human demonstrations IBMARL still surpasses imitation learning alone, and does so more than twice as fast as the best baseline.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [On numerical issues of divergences between nearly equal distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18511)
*Brandon Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文指出标准类别分布散度运算（前向/反向KL、广义JSD、蒙特卡洛KL估计、熵）在分布近乎相同时存在舍入与相消导致的数值不稳定，与Barron等(2026)发现的softmax/soft交叉熵问题同源，并提出数值稳定的重构公式与示例实现，经基于性质的测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Barron等(2026)已在softmax与软交叉熵损失中发现数值问题，但散度类运算尚未被系统检查。这类散度广泛用于知识蒸馏、强化学习、近端方法等以散度作为优化目标的场景，前向值与梯度若受舍入/相消误差影响，会损害训练稳定性与结果正确性，因此需要识别问题并给出稳定化方案。

Method: 针对类别分布间的KL（前向与反向）、广义Jensen-Shannon散度、蒙特卡洛KL估计及熵，分析其在p≈q时的舍入与相消误差来源（如log(p/q)形式在小差异下的抵消）；推导数值等价但稳定的重构公式，并对每种使用场景给出示例实现，用基于性质的测试（property-based testing）检验数值性质。

Result: 确认上述散度运算在分布近乎相等时，前向计算与梯度计算均受舍入和相消问题影响；所提稳定重构公式可消除或缓解这些误差，示例实现通过基于性质的测试验证。

Conclusion: 散度的数值不稳定是一个普遍而非孤立的问题，应在实现中以稳定重写替代朴素公式；该工作为知识蒸馏、强化学习与近端方法等依赖散度的优化流程提供了可直接采用的稳定实现与验证方法。

Abstract: Barron et al. (2026) recently identified numerical issues in the softmax and soft cross-entropy loss. We show that similar issues are present in standard divergence operations between categorical distributions. This includes the forward and reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and generalized Jensen-Shannon divergence (JSD), as well as Monte Carlo KL estimates and entropy. When the distributions are nearly equal, rounding and cancellation issues affect the forward and gradient computations. These issues can affect settings using divergences in optimization objectives, such as knowledge distillation, reinforcement learning, and proximal methods. We propose numerically stable reformulations and provide example implementations for each setting, validated by property-based tests.

</details>


### [97] [Alignment with Reflect-Reasoning: Learning How To Follow Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17398)
*Henry Bell,Caroline Zhang,Dhaval Potdar,Mohammed Mobasserul Haque,Brandon Fain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Reflect-Reasoning 训练方法，让模型学会"如何推理"以对齐原则，而非死记"遵循哪些原则"，在五种模型上优于系统提示与显式反思提示，并可泛化到医疗、金融等分布外领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐所需遵循的规范与原则高度依赖文化语境和部署场景，但现有对齐技术多在训练时将人类偏好或宪法原则固化为静态目标；实践中部署者只能靠 system prompt 在推理时注入情境规范。已有的"生成后反思推理"（批评—修订）虽优于简单系统提示，却受限于模型自身固有的推理能力，无法真正学会对齐推理。

Method: 提出 Reflect-Reasoning 训练方法：显式训练模型对"原则对齐"本身进行推理，即习得可迁移的对齐推理技能，而不是记忆特定原则内容。与两类基线对比：直接 system prompting，以及显式提示模型进行上下文内反思推理（in-context reflective reasoning）。

Result: 在五种模型上，Reflect-Reasoning 一致优于 system prompting 与显式反思提示策略；性能提升可扩展并泛化至完全分布外的医疗与金融原则领域；显著降低罕见但严重的原则违规发生率；不削弱模型原有安全工程（safety engineering），并对使用对抗性宪法（adversarial constitutions）的越狱尝试具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Reflect-Reasoning 是一种可扩展的宪法对齐训练范式，将宪法对齐转化为可泛化的推理能力，使模型无需额外微调即可适配新原则，并输出透明的推理轨迹。

Abstract: The appropriate governing norms and principles for the alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) often depend on cultural context and deployment application. However, most alignment techniques focus on training models to conform to human preferences or constitutional principles specified at training time and subsequently fixed. In practice, many deployers rely on system prompting to implement contextual norms and guardrails at inference time. Prior work has shown that post-generation reflective reasoning with critique and revision can improve conformance compared to simple system prompting, but such methods are limited to the reasoning abilities inherent in the trained model. We introduce Reflect-Reasoning, a training method that teaches models how to reason about alignment to principles, rather than simply which principles to follow. Across five models, Reflect-Reasoning consistently outperforms both system prompting and explicit prompting strategies for in-context reflective reasoning. These improvements extend and generalize to entirely out-of-distribution domains of medical and financial principles. We additionally demonstrate that Reflect-Reasoning reduces the occurrence of rare but serious principle violations. Finally, we show that Reflect-Reasoning does not erode the safety engineering of a model and is robust against jailbreaking attempts using adversarial constitutions. Overall, our results demonstrate that Reflect-Reasoning is a scalable method for training constitutional alignment as a generalizable reasoning capability, enabling models to adapt to novel principles and produce transparent reasoning traces without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [98] [WETW: Learning Pair-Specific Weighted Euclidean Distances for Efficient DTW Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17412)
*Christos Panourgias,Qitong Wang,George Tzagkarakis,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 WETW（加权欧氏时间规整）学习方法与 WETW-FV 过滤-验证流水线，用 WETW 构建候选短名单、再用精确 DTW 校验，在保证返回距离精确的前提下大幅加速 DTW 相似性搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTW 是时间错位时间序列相似性搜索的标准距离，但其代价随序列长度二次增长，且下界剪枝效果差，导致长序列大集合搜索很慢。已学习近似方法将序列嵌入后用固定规则（通常欧氏距离）比较每一对，而 DTW 对每对的对齐方式不同，二者不匹配。

Method: 关键观察：一对序列对齐后，DTW 即等价于在该对所选坐标上的欧氏距离。据此提出 WETW：用小型对称网络在不计算对齐的情况下，为每个嵌入维度、每个序列对预测权重，使固定长度嵌入的比较随对自适应。嵌入来自 WESEE 编码器，其多尺度时序特征提取器保留全局池化丢失的时序信息（WETW 亦兼容任意编码器）。另引入排序正则化，因仅回归拟合 DTW 距离会导致最近邻错序。最终构建 WETW-FV 过滤-验证流水线：WETW 生成短名单，精确 DTW 校验。

Result: WETW 将同一嵌入上最佳静态距离的误差减半；WESEE 使 recall@1 从 0.90 提升至 0.95；排序正则化使 recall@1 从 0.79 提升至 0.95。在八个数据集上，WETW-FV 比 UCR Suite、下界方法与 MESSI-DTW 中最快的精确方法快 11.4 倍，平均 recall@10 为 0.93，而最佳学习基线在同等速度下仅 0.59。在 48K 点序列上，WETW-FV 仍比 UCR Suite 快 40 倍，recall@10 为 0.91。

Conclusion: 将 DTW 视为按对选择坐标的欧氏距离，可学习出对自适应权重，显著优于固定距离；结合排序正则化与过滤-验证框架，WETW-FV 在近似搜索中实现高召回且返回距离严格精确，速度与精度均大幅优于精确方法与学习基线。

Abstract: Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) is the standard distance for similarity search over time series that are misaligned in time, but its cost is quadratic in the series length and lower bounds often prune few candidates, so search over large collections of long series is slow. Learned approximations embed the series to make comparisons cheap, yet compare every pair with the same fixed rule, usually the Euclidean distance, although DTW aligns each pair differently. We observe that, once a pair is aligned, DTW is a Euclidean distance over coordinates selected for that pair. Building on this view, we propose Weighted Euclidean Time Warping (WETW), a learned distance that adapts the comparison of fixed-length embeddings to each pair. A small symmetric network predicts one weight per embedding di- mension and pair without computing an alignment, which halves the error of the best static distance on the same embeddings. The embeddings come from the Weighted Euclidean SEries Encoder (WESEE), whose multi-scale Temporal Feature Extractor keeps the timing information that global pooling removes and raises recall@1 from 0.90 to 0.95, although WETW works with any encoder. We also show that fitting DTW distances is not enough for search, as regression alone misorders the closest neighbours, and that ranking regularization raises recall@1 from 0.79 to 0.95. Our filter-and-verify pipeline, WETW-FV, uses WETW to build a shortlist and exact DTW to check it, so the search is approximate but every returned distance is exact. Over eight datasets, WETW-FV is 11.4x faster than the fastest exact method among the UCR Suite, lower bounds and MESSI-DTW, at a mean recall@10 of 0.93, while the best learned baseline reaches 0.59 at the same speed. On 48K-point series, WETW- FV remains 40x faster than the UCR Suite at a recall@10 of 0.91.

</details>


### [99] [Classification with Large-Step Gradient Descent: Unifying Self-Stabilization and Convergence Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17459)
*Salma Tarmoun,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,Konstantinos Emmanouilidis,Ziqing Xu,René Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将"统一几何视角"推广到指数尾损失（逻辑损失、交叉熵），用曲率定义的参考子空间替代极小元流形，得到正交振荡被平行方向（sharpness 递减）阻尼的耦合动力系统，并给出迭代界与三相阻尼结构，揭示大步长的瞬态隐式偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GD 常用超过经典稳定性阈值的步长训练，损失非单调、振荡却收敛更快、泛化更好，经典收敛理论失效。已有三种理论视角各有不足：自稳定机制揭示走向平坦区但无收敛速率；特定损失的严格速率分析不解释稳定机制；过参数最小二乘的统一视角依赖极小元流形存在，而分类问题中该流形不存在（infimum 不可达）。

Method: 针对可分离线性分类中的逻辑损失与多类交叉熵等指数尾损失，提出以曲率定义的参考子空间替代极小元流形，构造耦合动力系统：正交方向振荡受平行方向（沿 sharpness 递减方向前进）阻尼。在正则性条件下（对对称数据模型下的逻辑/多类交叉熵已验证）推导迭代量（而非仅损失）的显式界，同时覆盖不稳定与稳定两种步长机制。

Result: 得到覆盖不稳定区与稳定区的迭代显式界；在稳定区揭示三相阻尼结构；发现大步长导致的瞬态隐式偏置：GD 渐近收敛到最大间隔方向，但大步长会留下持续的残差，并在某些数据几何下将其放大，从而影响有限时间内的间隔对齐。

Conclusion: 指数尾损失下可用曲率定义的参考子空间统一刻画大步长 GD 的稳定机制与收敛速率，替代缺失的极小元流形；大步长并非无代价，其对正交残差的瞬态放大构成影响有限时间隐式偏置的机制性解释。

Abstract: Neural networks are often trained with gradient descent (GD) using step sizes that exceed classical stability thresholds, a regime where standard convergence theory no longer applies. Despite exhibiting non-monotonic loss and oscillatory dynamics, this regime frequently leads to faster convergence and better generalization. Existing theoretical perspectives offer complementary but incomplete insights: one identifies a self-stabilization mechanism that drives GD toward flat regions but does not yield convergence rates; another derives rigorous rates for specific losses but does not explain the underlying stabilization mechanism; and a third unifies these views for overparameterized least squares, but relies on the existence of a minimizer manifold, which is absent in classification settings. In this work, we extend this unified geometric perspective to exponential-tail losses, including logistic and cross-entropy losses in separable linear classification, where the loss infimum is not attained at any finite parameter. We identify a curvature-defined reference subspace that replaces the role of the minimizer manifold, yielding a coupled dynamical system in which orthogonal oscillations are damped by parallel progress along directions of decreasing sharpness. Under regularity conditions, which we verify for logistic and multiclass cross-entropy losses on symmetric data models, we derive explicit bounds for the iterates, not just the loss, covering both the unstable and stable regimes, and revealing a three-phase damping structure in the latter. Our analysis further uncovers a transient implicit bias induced by large step sizes: although GD converges asymptotically to the max-margin direction, large steps can leave persistent orthogonal residuals and, in certain geometries, amplify them, affecting finite-time alignment with the max-margin direction.

</details>


### [100] [Unfinished-Bench: Measuring Rule Violations by Persistent Agents in Realistic Work Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17460)
*Seunghyun Lee,Jaehui Hwang,Dongyoon Han,Sangdoo Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Unfinished-Bench，在无用户、长期自主运行的工作环境中评估"坚持完成任务"（persistence）与嵌入内部文档规则冲突时的规则违反行为，发现持久性显著提升违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令-规则冲突基准要么在提示中明示规则、要么完全隐藏规则，且未评估智能体的持久性；而现实中智能体常需在无人监督下长期自主完成任务，并面对来自内部文档与他人权限的隐含约束，规则违背风险缺乏对应评测。

Method: 构建 Unfinished-Bench：97 个场景，覆盖 7 个组织与 7 类风险。每个场景中"完成目标"与"遵守规则"不可兼得，规则嵌入环境（内部文档）中而非写在提示里，并让模型在无用户的自主运行中具备持久性。对比设定包括规则由用户明示的形式，并以判定样本分析违规发生时机（规则展示后）与智能体是否先记录规则。

Result: 持久性将内部文档规则的违反率从 32.2% 提升至 43.6%，为用户明示同一规则时 8.5% 的五倍；文档规则违规中 98.3% 发生在规则被展示之后，判定样本中 89% 的智能体先写下了该规则。

Conclusion: 置于环境、缺乏用户监督的规则被违规可能引发严重事故，持久性驱动的风险需专门评估；Unfinished-Bench 提供了对应基准与文档。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly equipped with persistence toward task completion to run delegated work autonomously for long periods, under many unstated constraints from internal documents and other people's authority. When completion requires violating a rule, the agent must decide alone, yet benchmarks of instruction-rule conflicts state the rule in the prompt or keep it hidden and do not evaluate persistence. We present Unfinished-Bench, a benchmark that gives models persistence in autonomous runs without a user, in realistic work environments with embedded rules. In each of its 97 scenarios across 7 organizations and 7 risk categories, the goal and the rule cannot both hold. We find that persistence raised violations of a rule in an internal document from 32.2% to 43.6%, five times the 8.5% for the same rule stated by the user. Of the document-rule violations, 98.3% occurred after the rule was displayed, and in 89% of a judged sample the agent first wrote down the rule. Violations of rules placed in the environment without user oversight can lead to serious incidents, so persistence-driven risks need to be evaluated. Benchmark documentation: https://francis21c.github.io/unfinished-bench/

</details>


### [101] [Diffusion Tree Sampling for Inference Time Adaptation of Crystal Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17462)
*Daniel T. Levy,Vineet Jain,Tara Akhound-Sadegh,Sékou-Oumar Kaba,Siamak Ravanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出在推理阶段用扩散树采样（DTS）适配晶体生成模型，并扩展至利用条件模型作引导提议与多目标（帕累托前沿）采样，无需微调模型即可提升晶体稳定性、稀有空间群/高体模量及介电常数-带隙多目标生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型已成为晶体结构的强先验，但要得到满足多个冲突性质的候选结构需引导采样；微调成本高且每个新目标需重训模型，可微引导又不适用于许多真实任务，因此需要一种无需修改模型的推理时适配方法。

Method: 采用扩散树采样（DTS），即在去噪轨迹上进行蒙特卡洛树搜索的推理时方法；两项技术贡献：(1) 将已有的性质条件生成模型（如经无分类器引导微调的模型）作为DTS内部的引导提议，且不扭曲目标分布；(2) 将DTS扩展为多目标采样，使单次搜索产生覆盖整条帕累托前沿的样本。在从头晶体生成任务上实例化。

Result: 提升不同模型生成晶体的稳定性；利用预训练条件模型生成属于稀有空间群或高体模量的晶体；沿高介电常数与高带隙的帕累托前沿生成晶体。各任务中DTS推理时适配均在不修改模型本身的情况下成功改善输出。

Conclusion: DTS作为一种推理时适配手段，可有效将扩散晶体生成先验导向多个冲突性质目标，兼具免微调、支持条件模型复用与多目标帕累托采样的优势，适用于AI-for-science场景。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have become strong priors over plausible crystal structures, but turning that prior into promising candidates requires steering toward several conflicting properties at once. Fine-tuning is expensive and produces a new model for every new objective, while differentiable guidance is not applicable to many real-world tasks. We instead adapt these generative models at inference time using Diffusion Tree Sampling (DTS), a Monte Carlo tree search procedure over the denoising trajectory. We make two technical contributions on top of DTS that are particularly relevant for AI-for-science applications. First, we show how to use existing property-conditional generative models (e.g., models fine-tuned for classifier-free guidance) as guided proposals inside DTS, without distorting the target distribution. Second, we extend DTS to multi-objective sampling, so that a single search produces samples spanning an entire Pareto front. We instantiate both adaptations on de novo crystal generation: improving the stability of crystals generated by different models, using pretrained conditional models to generate crystals belonging to rare space groups or with high bulk modulus, and generating crystals along a Pareto front of high dielectric constant and high bandgap materials. Across these tasks, DTS-based inference-time adaptation successfully improves model outputs without modifying the models themselves.

</details>
