We also introduce head-scoped client-level DP, which protects the classification head and alone reduces the achievable $\varepsilon$ by 200 times at a matched round budget; combined with fewer communication rounds it reaches $\varepsilon\!\approx\!10.4$ with utility not statistically distinguishable from non-private training.

</details>


### [137] [Reasoning Models Confabulate to Defend Injected Beliefs About Their Own Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17958)
*Yang Ou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在激活空间注入一个提示文本中不可见的"off-topic概念形成痕迹"，可以切断推理模型对其自身请求的信念锚定：模型会把注入话题当作真实任务去推理、汇报和作答，且警告、提供真相、可视化注意力与电路归因均无法有效纠正，构成对CoT可监控性的具体限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控与自我报告被视为AI监督的抓手，其隐含前提是模型对"自己被要求做什么"的描述锚定于真实请求。作者要检验这一锚定是否可被切断，即模型的自我报告能否被与输入文本无关的内部编辑所劫持。

Method: 采用激活编辑（steering/activation injection）：向推理模型内部注入一个提示文本中不可见的、关于无关话题的"forming-trace"，形成跨6个模型（覆盖不同规模、架构与训练配方）的模型-概念单元测试；测量强制引用回述（forced quote-back）、最终回答、警告干预效果；并借助注意力分析与电路归因做机理解释。

Result: 强制引用回述在48个模型-概念单元中有47个返回注入话题；在4个最终俳句干净的模型上，注入答案在99%的生成中出现；即便给出点明真实话题的警告，真实话题回答率仍低于5%；以干净token提供真相时，模型会编造"用户可能搞混并自我纠正"之类的解释，行为与CoT同时被污染。可解释性手段失效：编辑后模型对真实请求的注意力反而更强，但因果上惰性；注入内容完整地沿真实任务电路传播，故注意力图与电路归因也读作"在任务上"。同一编辑还改变了请求的语言与格式而不只是话题。

Conclusion: 注入所触及的是模型对自身输入的指涉，所激起的是模型对该指涉的辩护。这是CoT可监控性的一条具体边界：自我报告并非可靠地由真实请求锚定，内部可解释性工具也不必然揭示被劫持。有效防御需针对"对自身输入的指涉"这一环节，而非仅依赖CoT文本或归因可视化。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring and self-report are proposed as levers for AI oversight, on the assumption that a model's account of what it is doing is anchored to what it was asked. We show this anchor can be cut. An activation edit invisible in the prompt text, the injected forming-trace of an off-topic concept, overwrites a reasoning model's belief about its own request: across six models spanning scale, architecture, and recipe, the injected topic becomes the task it reasons about, reports back, and answers: the forced quote-back returns the injected topic in 47 of 48 model-concept cells, and on the four models with clean final haiku the injected answer follows in 99% of generations. Warnings barely move it: even one naming the true topic yields under 5% true-topic answers. Handed the truth in clean tokens, the model confabulates a story about the user ("maybe they got confused and corrected themselves") and answers with the injection anyway, its behavior and chain-of-thought corrupted together: a concrete limit on CoT monitorability. Opening the model up offers no reprieve: it attends to the true request even harder after edits, yet that attention is causally inert, and the injected content rides intact task circuits, so attention maps and circuit attributions read as on-task too; the same edit redirects the request's language and format, not only its topic. What the injection reaches is the model's reference to its own input; what it elicits is its defense of it.

</details>


### [138] [DeltaVisionX: A Low-Cost Vision-Guided Automation System Using YOLOv11 and PLC-Based Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17959)
*Lara Sobh,Marc Nahhas,Francisco Katach El Halabi,Michel Owayjan,Fadi Elias Sleiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出低成本工业自动化系统 DeltaVisionX，将 YOLOv11 实时视觉检测与 Delta DVP-SE PLC 气缸分拣执行结合，通过编码器同步实现 80 件/分钟吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在用低成本、本地可开发的自动化方案替代人工分拣，降低制造环节的劳动力依赖并提升效率。

Method: 自建并标注、增强数据集训练 YOLOv11 检测输送带目标；检测结果传入 Delta DVP-SE PLC 驱动气动活塞分拣；采用编码器跟踪实现检测与执行机构的同步。创新点在于编码器同步的视觉—PLC 集成，而非检测模型本身。

Result: YOLOv11 达到 mAP 0.97，单帧推理约 100 ms；编码器同步保证检测与执行可靠对齐，吞吐量达每分钟 80 件。

Conclusion: 证明经济型本地化自动化可有效减少人工依赖、提升制造效率；后续将引入高速 Delta 机器人与基于 EtherCAT 的伺服控制以支持高级抓取放置任务。

Abstract: This paper presents DeltaVisionX, a low-cost industrial automation system that integrates real-time computer vision with PLC-based actuation for object classification and sorting. The system uses the YOLOv11 (You Only Look Once) deep learning model to detect objects on an operating conveyor belt and communicates detection results to a Delta DVP-SE PLC, which actuates pneumatic pistons for sorting. A custom dataset was created, labeled, and augmented to train the YOLOv11 model, achieving a mean average precision of 0.97 with real-time inference of 100 ms per frame. Encoder-based tracking ensures reliable synchronization between detection and actuation, supporting a throughput of 80 objects per minute. Results show that affordable, locally developed automation can reduce labor dependency and improve manufacturing efficiency. The novelty of the proposed system lies in its encodersynchronized vision-PLC integration rather than in the detection model itself. Future work includes integrating a highspeed Delta robot with EtherCAT-based servo control for advanced pick-and-place operations.

</details>


### [139] [Analyzing Learning-Dynamics Across Loss and Prediction Choices for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17967)
*Dongwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文解耦去噪生成模型中"预测空间"与"损失空间"的影响，证明固定预测目标下的损失空间切换等价于时间步重加权，并借助秩-m线性瓶颈的误差下界揭示瓶颈容量与预测目标的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去噪生成模型可预测x、ε或v，损失也可在这三种空间中度量，共九种组合虽代数等价却在去噪精度与样本质量上表现差异显著，此前缺乏对预测空间与损失空间各自作用的定量理解。

Method: 理论分析：固定预测目标时，证明更换损失空间精确等价于时间步重加权，并给出该重加权影响生成质量的启发式分析；推导秩-m线性瓶颈压缩D维噪声输入时噪声与速度预测存在D-m的不可约平方误差下界，而干净数据预测可利用低维数据流形结构；结合与预测相关的误差转换导出瓶颈容量的必要条件。实验：在CIFAR-10、Imagenette、ImageNet-256上使用U-Net与JiT验证。

Result: 实验支持理论预测：在压缩瓶颈下，预测目标的选择显著影响去噪精度与样本质量，且噪声预测的误差与理论下界高度吻合。

Conclusion: 结果定量刻画了架构瓶颈与预测目标、损失选择之间的交互机制，为去噪生成模型中的预测/损失空间设计与瓶颈容量选择提供理论依据。

Abstract: Denoising generative models predict clean data ($x$), noise ($ε$), or velocity ($v$), while training losses can be measured in any of these spaces. Although the resulting nine combinations are algebraically related, they can yield substantially different denoising accuracy and sample quality. We disentangle the effects of prediction and loss spaces. For a fixed prediction target, we show that changing the loss space is exactly equivalent to timestep reweighting and provide a heuristic analysis of how this reweighting affects generation quality. We then show that a rank-$m$ linear bottleneck compressing a $D$-dimensional noisy input imposes an irreducible squared-error floor of $D-m$ on noise and velocity prediction, whereas clean-data prediction can exploit low-dimensional data structure. Combining this bound with prediction-dependent error conversion yields distinct necessary conditions on bottleneck capacity. Experiments with U-Net and JiT across CIFAR-10, Imagenette, and ImageNet-256 support these theoretical predictions. Under compression, prediction choice strongly affects both denoising accuracy and sample quality, with noise-prediction errors closely matching the theoretical lower bound. Our results provide a quantitative understanding of how architectural bottlenecks interact with prediction and loss choices in denoising generative models.

</details>


### [140] [When Does Oversampling Help? A Decision Framework for Imbalanced Data Streams Under Label Delay and Concept Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17981)
*Ahmed Awad,Anirudh Sengar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在类别不平衡、概念漂移与标签延迟并存的数据流中，持续重评估（re-evaluation）本身即可恢复延迟带来的损失，流式过采样（oversampling）的边际价值在真实数据上几乎不可检测，方法选择应依据流特性动态重估而非沿用合成基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式过采样器通常在"测试即训练"（即时标签）假设下评估，其在联合类别不平衡、概念漂移与标签延迟条件下的行为尚未被系统研究。论文提出核心问题：在标签延迟的不平衡数据流中，引入流式过采样是否值得，还是持续重评估早期预测已足以补偿延迟成本。

Method: 构建以三条轴组织的决策框架——漂移密度、少数类可学习性、延迟相对细化窗口的比例，并操作化为"恢复通道"（重评估）与"少数类覆盖通道"（过采样）之间的竞争；在 6 个合成流与 6 个真实流上评估 15 个分类器，进行过采样与重评估的广泛实验对比。

Result: 重评估能在漂移活跃流上恢复延迟导致的损失，但在平稳流上无效果；过采样相对重评估的边际贡献仅在一种真实数据配置上具备统计显著性；合成流得出的成本—性能帕累托推荐无法迁移到真实数据，真实数据上的前沿按漂移机制分群。

Conclusion: 流式过采样在多数带标签延迟的场景中并非必要，重评估是主要收益来源。方法选择应基于具体数据流画像（漂移密度、可学习性、延迟/窗口比）并在流演化过程中持续重估，而不应直接继承合成基准的结论。

Abstract: For imbalanced data streams with delayed labels, is it worth adding a streaming oversampler, or does continuous re-evaluation of earlier predictions already recover the cost of the delay? We address this through an extensive experimental comparison of streaming oversampling and continuous re-evaluation under joint class imbalance, concept drift, and label delay. Streaming oversamplers are typically evaluated under the test-then-train assumption of immediate labels; their behaviour under these conditions, assessed through re-evaluation, has not been systematically studied. We close this gap with a decision framework that predicts when oversampling adds value over re-evaluation, organised around three axes: drift density, minority-class learnability, and delay relative to the refinement window, and operationalised as a competition between a recovery channel (re-evaluation) and a minority-coverage channel (oversampling). Evaluating $15$ classifiers on $6$ synthetic and $6$ real streams, we find: re-evaluation recovers delay-induced loss on drift-active streams but is silent on stationary ones; oversampling's marginal contribution over re-evaluation is statistically detectable in only one real-data configuration; and cost--performance Pareto recommendations from synthetic streams do not transfer to real data, where fronts partition by drift regime. Method selection should therefore be conditioned on the stream's profile and re-estimated as it evolves, not inherited from synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [141] [RiverScan: Learning to Accumulate on River Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18005)
*He Zhu,Zhiyi Gao,Youhuan Yang,Han Quan,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于面积加权选择性状态空间递归的河流空间算子 RiverScan，按拓扑序递归聚合上游状态，实现大流域径流预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大流域径流预测需刻画异质性局地水文响应及其向下游的累积；河网连通性提供天然结构，但有效利用需解决如何组合不同面积子流域贡献的问题，作者将其形式化为"学习累积"。

Method: RiverScan 为空间算子：以拓扑序递归地把局地时间表征与累积上游状态结合；用汇水面积比例刻画子流域规模，用可学习保留（retention）控制上游信息向下游的持续程度，即选择性状态空间递归。

Result: 在多瑙河流域 608 个站点上，整体预测性能优于所评估的时间与图基线；结构控制与消融实验表明，按水文累积对齐的方式组合异质子流域表征显著提升径流预测，且正确拓扑带来的增益沿河网层级递增。

Conclusion: 物理结构化的累积是水文建模的有效归纳偏置，支持发展更细粒度、全分布式的 AI 水文模型。

Abstract: Accurate runoff prediction across large river basins requires models to capture heterogeneous local hydrological responses and their accumulation downstream. River-network connectivity provides a natural structure for this task, but using it effectively requires learning how to combine contributions from subcatchments of different sizes. We frame this challenge as learning to accumulate and introduce RiverScan, a spatial operator based on area-weighted selective state-space recurrence. RiverScan recursively combines local temporal representations with accumulated upstream states in topological order. Contributing-area fractions account for subcatchment size, while learned retention controls how upstream information persists downstream. Experiments on 608 stations in the Danube basin show that RiverScan outperforms the evaluated temporal and graph baselines in overall predictive performance. Structural controls and ablations demonstrate that composing heterogeneous subcatchment representations in a manner aligned with hydrological accumulation substantially improves runoff prediction, with the benefits of correct topology growing along the river-network hierarchy. These findings establish physically structured accumulation as an effective inductive bias for hydrological modeling and motivate finer-grained, fully distributed AI-based hydrological models.

</details>


### [142] [Sample-Efficient Control of Magnetoconvective Flows via Differentiable Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18008)
*Jannis Becktepe,Dmitry Krasnov,Nils Thuerey,Jörg Schumacher,Sebastian Peitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可微GPU加速MHD求解器φ-PICT，用于主动流动控制，支撑基于梯度的换热优化与逆设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MHD流动在聚变包层冷却等工程中至关重要，主动流动控制（AFC）可优化换热等关键性能；机器学习已推动AFC发展，但其在MHD流动中的应用仍基本空白，缺乏高效且可微的求解工具。

Method: 构建面向AFC的可微、GPU加速MHD求解器φ-PICT；将其集成到FluidGym环境中，使基于梯度的优化算法可由端到端微分直接获取目标对控制量的梯度。

Result: 在MHD基准算例上完成验证并表现出高计算效率；换热提升超过25%，且样本效率高；逆设计精度较黑盒方法高约\(10^3\)倍。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 可微GPU加速求解器能有效释放梯度型AFC在MHD流动中的潜力，为聚变包层冷却等场景的换热优化与逆设计提供高效工具。

Abstract: Magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) fluid flows are central to demanding engineering applications, most prominently fusion blanket cooling, where flow control is a promising tool to optimize key properties such as heat transfer. While active flow control (AFC) has recently advanced through machine learning, its application to MHD flows remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present $φ$-PICT, a novel differentiable GPU-accelerated MHD solver targeted at AFC. We validate $φ$-PICT against MHD benchmarks and demonstrate its high computational efficiency. Integrated into FluidGym, $φ$-PICT enables sample-efficient gradient-based algorithms to improve heat transfer by $>25$% and allows optimizers to achieve $10^3\times$ higher accuracy in inverse design than black-box methods. $φ$-PICT is publicly available at https://github.com/upb-sciml/phiPICT.

</details>


### [143] [Conflict, Feasibility, Faithfulness, Displacement: Four Quantities That Come Apart in Federated Dual-Cone Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18014)
*Grzegorz Gwardys,Karol Wojciechowski,Mateusz Gwardys,Bartosz {Ś}widerski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文以UPGrad（一种只改变FedAvg更新方向、在一阶意义上不增加任何客户端损失的聚合器）检验FedFV、FedGH、FedMGDA+等方法"通过多目标梯度聚合消除客户端更新冲突"的合理性，指出"冲突、加权均值的可行性、上传更新对客户端梯度的忠实性、更新位移"这四个被混为一谈的量其实彼此分离；实验显示增益仅在全参与下出现、在保留未用数据后消失，且均匀权重与随机旋转可解释部分效应，公平性效应经多重性校正后不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FedFV、FedGH、FedMGDA+等联邦学习方法以"多目标梯度聚合"消除客户端更新之间的冲突为核心依据，但该依据缺乏控制变量式的严格检验。作者希望在仅改变FedAvg更新方向、其余条件不变的前提下，量化该理由中各个概念的实际作用，判断性能与公平性收益究竟来自"冲突消除"本身还是其他因素。

Method: 提出/采用UPGrad作为对照聚合器：其更新在一阶意义上不增加任何客户端的损失，且只改变FedAvg的更新方向。将原论证中被视为一体的四个量拆解为：客户端更新间冲突、其加权均值的可行性、传输更新对客户端梯度的忠实性、更新位移。实验设置包括Dirichlet划分（改变浓度以调节异质性）、全参与与部分参与对比、CIFAR-10与Oxford-IIIT Pet两个数据集，并设置对照基线：均匀客户端权重（对冲突不敏感）、匹配的随机旋转、保留协议未使用数据的复评，以及公平性与多重性校正分析。

Result: Dirichlet划分下UPGrad在全参与时使CIFAR-10精度提升+0.78 pp（15个随机种子中14个，p<0.001）；但在Oxford-IIIT Pet上，一旦保留协议原先未使用的数据，其+0.52 pp增益消失（仅+0.07 pp）。仅改变Dirichlet浓度时，增益随异质性增强而增大，但冲突最强的划分反而没有增益。对冲突不敏感的均匀客户端权重即可达到CIFAR-10的同等增益并超过Pets增益，而匹配的随机旋转两者皆不能；经多重性校正后无公平性效应存活；在CIFAR-10上该一阶保证并未真正作用于客户端的梯度。

Conclusion: "多目标梯度聚合消除冲突"这一理由经不起控制变量检验：冲突、可行性、忠实性与更新位移是可分离的量，观测到的收益并不能归因于冲突消除，且对参与方式、数据使用与异质性设置高度敏感。作者据此提出今后此类评估应报告的指标与协议，以提升结论的可复现性与可信度。

Abstract: FedFV, FedGH and FedMGDA+ aggregate client updates with multi-objective gradient aggregators to remove conflict between them. We examine this rationale with UPGrad, which increases no client's loss to first order, in a comparison that changes only the direction of FedAvg's update. Four quantities the rationale treats as one -- conflict between client updates, feasibility of their weighted mean, faithfulness of the transmitted updates to the clients' gradients, and displacement of the update -- come apart. Under Dirichlet partitioning UPGrad improves CIFAR-10 accuracy by +0.78 pp (14/15 seeds, p < 0.001), only with full participation; on Oxford-IIIT Pet its +0.52 pp gain vanishes (+0.07 pp) once the data our protocol left unused are kept. Varying only the Dirichlet concentration, the gain grows with heterogeneity, yet the split with the most conflict shows none. Uniform client weights, blind to conflict, match the CIFAR-10 gain and exceed the Pets gain; a matched random rotation does neither. No fairness effect survives multiplicity correction, and on CIFAR-10 the guarantee does not reach the clients' gradients. We state what evaluations should report.

</details>


### [144] [RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$: On-Device Reinforcement Learning Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18035)
*Zexin Li,Xinwei Gao,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$ 是面向嵌入式 GPU 上端侧强化学习的运行时系统，将 actor 与 learner 的耦合从“停机等待或策略漂移”的二选一，转化为可调参数：以刷新上界约束两次刷新之间 actor 的发布次数，并以带松弛感知准入的 GPU 许可机制，在任意基础调度器之上优先保障 actor 并利用空隙时间进行训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧 RL 在同一嵌入 GPU 上与其他负载共享资源，同时周期性地跑 actor 与 learner。由于 learner 生成 actor 的权重，现有设计要么阻塞 actor（牺牲实时性），要么允许其策略漂移（牺牲正确性），二者都无法兼顾。作者希望为这一耦合关系提供显式的、可调的控制手段。

Method: 提出 RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$ 运行时，包含两个核心机制：(1) 刷新上界（refresh bound），限制两次刷新之间 actor 的发布次数；(2) 带 slack 感知准入的 GPU 许可（GPU permit），在任意基础调度器之上优先保障 actor，并将其余空闲时间用于训练。并在两块 Jetson 板卡上以真实刷新成本进行评测。

Result: 在真实刷新成本下，对一个 16.8M 参数策略，RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$ 将截止期违约率控制在 1% 以下；而无该机制时违约率为 85%–100%。

Conclusion: 将 actor–learner 耦合显式参数化、并以许可式准入叠加在现有调度器之上，可在共享 GPU 的端侧环境中同时维持实时性与训练推进，大幅降低策略更新的时延违约。

Abstract: On-device reinforcement learning runs a periodic actor and a learner on one embedded GPU shared with other workloads. Because the learner produces the actor's weights, existing designs stall the actor or let its policy drift. We present RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$, an on-device runtime that makes this coupling a tunable parameter: a refresh bound limits actor releases between refreshes, and a GPU permit with slack-aware admission prioritises the actor and fills spare time with training atop any base scheduler. Under the real refresh cost on two Jetson boards, RL$^{\mathrm{RT}}$ keeps a 16.8 M-parameter policy's deadline misses below 1%, against 85 to 100% without it.

</details>


### [145] [Enrichment-Oriented Virtual Screening of Perovskite Solar Cells through Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18043)
*Yezeyuan Zheng,Ziyang Hu,Maojun Sun. Ruijian Han,Jian Huang,Guanhua Chen,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以冻结的TabPFN为代理模型、结合强化学习生成配方的虚拟筛选框架，用于钙钛矿太阳能电池配方优先级排序；在1,846个器件数据上，top-10选择将平均PCE由19.68%提升至23.74%，top-100短名单中PPO/RPPO的邻域支持命中率达23%/24%（约为纯随机的8倍），但结果仅支持"面向富集的优先级排序"，不支持实验性能、种子稳健性或同等预算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钙钛矿太阳能电池的实验优化受限于吸收层组分、器件层与工艺的组合多样性，逐一试错代价高；因此在制备前通过虚拟筛选对配方进行优先级排序具有吸引力，需要一种能在高性能实验样本邻域内富集候选的筛选方法。

Method: 将配方生成建模为虚拟筛选任务：使用冻结的Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network（TabPFN）作为代理模型指导强化学习策略生成配方，并以890个实测器件构成的参考池进行基于邻域的评估；采用五折评估（1,846个器件），将三种学习策略与六种基线在每次运行3,000次代理评估下比较，以邻域支持命中率、PCE提升及与已知组分族的相似度匹配为指标。

Result: 五折评估显示top-10选择使平均PCE从19.68%升至23.74%；在K=100的top-100短名单中，PPO与RPPO的邻域支持命中率分别为23%和24%（为纯随机2.9%的7.9倍与8.3倍），而表现最好的基线（L-SHADE与Ax BO并列）为10%（3.4倍）；增益伴随较弱的支持比例；9,000个配方中65.4%在相似度0.80下匹配某一组分族，相似度0.90时降至11.7%。

Conclusion: 结果支持面向富集（enrichment-oriented）的优先级排序，即能在高性能实验邻域内有效富集候选配方；但不足以证明实验性能提升、种子稳健性或匹配预算下的优势。

Abstract: Experimental optimization of perovskite solar cells is constrained by combinatorial diversity in absorber compositions, device layers, and processing, which makes virtual screening attractive for prioritizing recipes before fabrication. We develop a surrogate-guided reinforcement-learning framework that casts recipe generation as virtual screening, enriching candidate shortlists near high-performing experimental neighbors. A frozen Tabular Prior-data Fitted Network (TabPFN) guides recipe generation, while a reference pool of 890 measured devices enables neighborhood-based assessment. Five-fold evaluation on 1,846 devices shows top-ten selection raises mean power conversion efficiency (PCE) from 19.68\% to 23.74\%. Three learned policies are compared with six baselines under 3,000 surrogate evaluations per run. In top-100 shortlists, Proximal policy optimization (PPO) and recurrent PPO (RPPO) achieve neighborhood-supported hit rates of 23\% and 24\% (7.9- and 8.3-fold Pure Random's 2.9\%), versus 10\% (3.4-fold) for the baselines with the highest supported hit rate (L-SHADE and Ax BO, tied) at $K = 100$. Gains coexist with weak-support fractions. Of 9,000 recipes, 65.4\% match a composition family at similarity 0.80, falling to 11.7\% at 0.90. Results support enrichment-oriented prioritization but not experimental performance, seed robustness, or matched-budget advantage.

</details>


### [146] [Learning nonlinear interactions and cross-feature couplings with product-unit residual networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18074)
*Ziyuan Li,Uwe Jaekel,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出复数乘积单元残差网络（CV-PURe），通过将乘法乘积单元与残差连接结合来显式建模跨特征耦合，并在合成交互基准与真实数据集上系统评估预测精度与输入级交互恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多层感知机（MLP）只能隐式捕获非线性特征交互，导致表示纠缠，损害鲁棒性与可解释性；因此需要一种能显式建模跨特征耦合且优化稳定的网络结构。

Method: 构建复数乘积单元残差网络（CV-PURe），将乘法型乘积单元与残差连接整合以显式刻画特征耦合并稳定训练；在交互驱动的合成基准与真实数据集上，采用可复现协议联合评估预测性能与输入级交互恢复，并进行五折数据划分与五个训练种子的重复实验；通过PURe两种变体的成对消融、噪声与学习曲线评估及推理耗时测量进行深入分析。

Result: CV-PURe在六个科学基准中的四个上取得最低平均RMSE，相对MLP降低Friedman误差53.9%；其Friedman交互图中已知交互对获得总强度的60.7%（MLP为31.0%）；在含三阶、四阶乘积项及20个干扰项的合成任务上，RMSE由0.1376降至0.0797（相对复数MLP），并在每次重复中恢复全部九个真实成对投影；Bike Sharing与Parkinsons Telemonitoring实验将预测提升归因于时间、日历与季节上下文的贡献；噪声与学习曲线实验显示在扰动输入和有限监督下预测增益持续存在，同时量化了推理计算开销。

Conclusion: 显式乘积单元与残差结构的结合可在保持优化稳定的同时提升预测精度与交互可解释性，并在有限监督和数据扰动条件下保持稳健，是标准MLP在交互建模任务上的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Understanding nonlinear feature interactions is crucial in science and engineering, yet standard multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) often capture such interactions only implicitly, leading to entangled representations that can impair robustness and interpretability. We investigate complex-valued product-unit residual networks (CV-PURe) that integrate multiplicative product units with residual connections to explicitly model cross-feature couplings while stabilizing optimization. We conduct a systematic evaluation on an interaction-driven synthetic benchmark and real-world datasets and evaluate prediction together with input-level interaction recovery under a reproducible protocol. Across five data splits and five training seeds, CV-PURe achieves the lowest mean RMSE on four of six scientific benchmarks, reducing Friedman error by 53.9% relative to MLP. Its Friedman interaction maps assign 60.7% of total strength to the known interacting pair, compared with 31.0% for MLP. On a synthetic task containing third- and fourth-order products and 20 distractors, CV-PURe reduces RMSE from 0.1376 to 0.0797 relative to a complex-valued MLP and recovers all nine true pairwise projections in every training/sampling repetition. Bike Sharing experiments connect improved prediction to the contribution of temporal, calendar, and seasonal context through paired ablations of both PURe variants. Parkinsons Telemonitoring extends the context analysis to repeated measurements. Noise and learning-curve evaluations demonstrate sustained predictive gains under perturbed inputs and limited supervision, while inference measurements quantify computational cost. This article extends our ICCS 2026 paper, Modeling Nonlinear Feature Interactions with Product-Unit Residual Networks (doi:10.1007/978-3-032-29921-5_6).

</details>


### [147] [Deep Latents, Long Memory: Latent persistence explains temporal correlations in transformer activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18089)
*Francesco Cagnetta,Santiago Acevedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 作者提出用层激活的自相关函数（相隔lag位置激活的平均点积）来定量刻画LLM表征的“抽象”程度，发现自相关衰减尾巴随层数加深而变长，且深层潜在特征持续跨越更长上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “隐层表征随深度抽象程度提高”这一观点缺乏可操作的抽象性定义，难以定量检验。作者基于“序列的自相关反映生成过程结构”的原理，提出以自相关作为可度量的替代指标。

Method: 定义层自相关函数为两个相隔lag的上下文位置上激活的平均点积；在具有受控层级潜在结构的合成数据与自然数据上测量自相关尾部的跨层变化；在真实文本中以稀疏自编码器(SAE)特征作为潜在变量代理，检验其持续性(persistence)与自相关的关系；并通过消融持久的SAE特征验证因果性。

Result: 合成与自然数据中自相关尾巴均随层数加深而增长；在合成数据中该增长源于编码控制长距离输入的抽象潜在变量，潜在编码的持续性可预测自相关的定性结构并解释大部分实证指标；在真实文本中，SAE特征的持续性与自相关幅度跨数据集、跨模型强相关，且持续时间和自相关都随深度增加；消融持久SAE特征后自相关下降。

Conclusion: 自相关为表征抽象性提供了可操作、可定量的度量；深层潜在特征在更长上下文跨度上保持持续，这支持了LLM深度方向抽象层级递增的观点，并表明特征持续性是其机制来源。

Abstract: The hidden layers of Large Language Models (LLMs) are believed to encode representations of the data whose abstraction increases with depth. However, in the absence of an operational notion of abstraction, this statement is hard to test quantitatively. Here, we propose an alternative view, building on the principle that the correlations of a sequence reflect the structure of the process that generated it. We define the autocorrelation function of a layer as the average dot product between its activations at two context positions separated by a lag. Latent variables encoded persistently along the context create slowly decaying tails in the autocorrelation, and we find these tails to grow across layers in both synthetic and natural data. In synthetic data with controlled hierarchical latent structure, this growth stems from the encoding of abstract latent variables, which govern long stretches of the input. Indeed, the persistence of latent codes predicts the qualitative structure of autocorrelations and accounts for most of the empirical measures. In real text, we take sparse-autoencoder features as proxies for latent variables, and find that their persistence correlates strongly with the magnitude of autocorrelations across datasets and LLMs. In particular, the persistence time increases with depth, as do autocorrelations, implying that deeper latent features persist for longer context spans. We complement this analysis by explicitly ablating the persistent sparse-autoencoder features and observing a consequent decrease in the autocorrelations.

</details>


### [148] [Efficient Poincaré Convolutions through Patch-Statistics Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18125)
*Lukáš Jochymek,Markéta Vašinková,Petr Gajdoš*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"因子化"Poincaré卷积层：裁剪后的得分仅依赖每个图像块的平方范数及其与滤波器的内积，可用原生卷积加单通道盒式滤波直接计算，无需将重叠图像块映射到Poincaré球面，训练速度提升1.4–2.0倍、峰值显存降低38–55%，且精度不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HNN++中已发布的Poincaré卷积实现需先把每个重叠图像块映射到Poincaré球面再计算得分，带来额外计算与显存开销，训练成本高。

Method: 证明裁剪后的得分对每个图像块仅通过两个统计量（平方范数、与滤波器的内积）依赖；这两个量可由原生卷积与单通道盒式滤波直接计算，从而实现精确等价的因子化层，避免构造球面值图像块。

Result: 单精度输出与梯度与双精度评估的一致性不劣于显式参考实现；RTX A6000上Poincaré U-Net与ResNet-20完整训练更新比两种显式实现中最快者快1.5–1.8倍（比跳过映射的显式实现快1.2–1.4倍）；V100上ResNet-20加宽8倍仍保持优势；每个任务15次配对训练运行，更新快2.0倍与1.8倍，分割/分类最终质量无一致性变化；U-Net扩展研究中峰值显存下降38–55%；替换官方PyTorch Hyperbolic U-Net卷积后训练步快1.4倍、显存减半。

Conclusion: 利用图像块统计量评估既有层，可在所测模型与软件栈中显著降低Poincaré卷积网络的训练成本，且保持数值精度与模型质量。

Abstract: Published implementations of the Poincaré convolution in Hyperbolic Neural Networks++ (HNN++) map every overlapping image patch to the Poincaré ball before evaluating its score. We show that the clipped score depends on each patch only through two statistics, its squared norm and its inner products with the filters, which a native convolution and a single-channel box filter compute directly. The factorized layer therefore evaluates the specified score exactly, without constructing ball-valued patches, and its single-precision outputs and gradients agree with double-precision evaluations at least as closely as those of the explicit reference. On an RTX A6000 under two versions of JAX, complete training updates of a Poincaré U-Net and a Poincaré ResNet-20 are 1.5 to 1.8 times faster than the faster of two explicit implementations that map patches to the ball (1.2 to 1.4 times against an explicit implementation that skips this mapping); on a V100, the gain over the gather-based one persists as the ResNet-20 is widened eightfold. In 15 paired training runs per task, updates are 2.0 and 1.8 times faster than a reference that follows the published execution order, with no consistent change in final segmentation or classification quality, and peak memory falls by 38 to 55% in a U-Net scaling study. Replacing the convolutions of the official PyTorch Hyperbolic U-Net makes its training step 1.4 times faster with half the memory. Evaluating an existing layer from its patch statistics thus makes Poincaré convolutional networks cheaper to train in the tested models and software stacks.

</details>


### [149] [When Copying Is Hard to Beat: A Negative Result in Clinical Assessment Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18142)
*Marius Fracarolli,Stefan Riezler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告一个负面结果：在ICU次日临床评估预测任务上，所评测的模型相对强持续性基线（persistence）没有稳定优势，说明此前结论高度依赖于文档流程、输入截断、观测掩码与解码规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床次日评估预测常被报告为正收益，但医生可直接查看前一日问卷答案，文档存在"持续性"先验；需要严格检验模型是否真正超越持续性基线，并厘清结论对评估设计的敏感性。

Method: 在29,715个ICU"日对"、45个条目的数据上，采用患者不重叠（patient-disjoint）划分。对比生理信息引导的门控模型与单行临床规则／持续性基线；设置三种评估变体：①生理窗口延至上一表单参考时刻，②排除由父条目推导出的68.5%单元格，③二者结合；并评测三个模型家族共四个检查点、一个微调模型，以及类别化读出（categorical read-out）与state-only/forecast-only解码的对比。

Result: 门控模型相对单行临床规则在条目宏F1上仅+0.0019 [+0.0006,+0.0031]，但准确率下降；生理窗口截断至上一表单参考时刻后无优势（+0.0001 [-0.0006,+0.0008]）；排除父条目推导单元格后亦无优势，两者结合则略有劣势。四个检查点均不及持续性基线，微调模型仅在两处单元格上不同。类别化读出使"变化单元格"准确率提升约八倍，但state-only读出总体得分高于forecast-only解码，并与转移矩阵相当。

Conclusion: 在文档持久性主导的场景下，模型未建立可靠的超越持续性基线的证据；结论的方向与显著性取决于文档流程、输入截断点、观测掩码及解码规则，未来临床预测研究应显式控制这些设计选择并报告持续性基线。

Abstract: We report a negative result in next-day clinical assessment forecasting: the evaluated models show no consistent advantage over strong persistence-based baselines. Clinicians can view the previous day's questionnaire answers, a plausible source of documentation persistence. Predicting 45 items on 29,715 ICU day-pairs with patient-disjoint splits, a physiology-informed gated model improves item-macro-F1 over a one-line clinical rule by +0.0019 [+0.0006,+0.0031], while losing accuracy. No macro-F1 advantage is established when the physiology window ends at the previous form's reference time (+0.0001 [-0.0006,+0.0008]), or when scoring excludes the 68.5% of cells derived from parent answers; combining both changes yields a small disadvantage. Four checkpoints across three families underperform persistence, and a fine-tuned one differs from it on only two cells. A categorical read-out raises changed-cell accuracy eightfold, but a state-only read-out scores higher overall than forecast-only decoding and matches a transition matrix. This negative result highlights how the documentation process, input cutoff, observation mask and decoding rule shape conclusions about forecasting recorded assessments.

</details>


### [150] [STAMP: Manufacturing-Informed Generative Design and Shape Optimization of Sheet Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18144)
*Jihoon Kim,Jun Hyeok Song,Wonho Ha,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAMP将制造几何条件直接嵌入板件形状表示（材料布局+参考曲面深度图），从而无论训练集大小，所有生成与优化结果均满足条件；用边界条件扩散生成布局，用预测器梯度优化曲面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成形板件的生成式设计需在探索布局、提升性能的同时保持几何制造可行性（如不可过切、厚度固定），而现有方法常依赖惩罚项或后处理，且受训练数据规模限制。

Method: 以材料布局加"垂直于拉延方向的参考曲面高度图"表示板件，法向给定名义厚度；该表示天然排除参考曲面过切并固定厚度，无需制造惩罚项。采用边界条件扩散模型生成备选布局，并利用预测器梯度引导曲面优化，同时保留外形轮廓、开口与指定固定区域。在CarHoods10k汽车发动机罩内板上验证。

Result: 在存储值数量相当的条件下，深度图重建精度优于点云与体素基线；5个训练设计中有1/3的训练运行可产生新颖多样布局；仅用500个设计（6.5%）即达全数据模型的布局多样性。11个留出设计族中，基于预测器在现有数据中筛选的FEA变形与最优值平均差0.18 mm（随机选择为1.59 mm），全部11族均优于随机。10%深度变化约束下，生成与现有板件平均变形降低24.8%，几何计算的质量平均增加3.2%。

Conclusion: 将制造约束纳入形状表示可使工程师在该"制造知情"设计空间内直接探索与优化板件，兼顾可行性与性能提升。

Abstract: Generative design of formed sheet panels must preserve geometric manufacturing conditions while exploring layouts and improving performance. STAMP embeds these conditions directly in the shape representation. Each panel is described by its material layout and a reference surface with one height at each occupied position in a plane perpendicular to the drawing direction. Nominal thickness is prescribed along the normal. The representation allows curved surfaces, ribs, and openings. It excludes reference-surface undercuts and keeps nominal thickness fixed without manufacturing penalty terms. Because these conditions belong to the representation, every generated and optimized panel satisfies them regardless of training-set size. Boundary-conditioned diffusion generates alternative layouts, and predictor gradients guide a surface optimization that retains outlines, openings, and designated fixed regions. We demonstrate STAMP on automotive hood inner panels using CarHoods10k. With a comparable number of stored values, depth maps reconstruct surfaces more accurately than point-cloud and voxel baselines. From only five training designs, one of three training runs yields novel and varied layouts. A generator trained on 500 designs, 6.5% of the training set, already reaches the layout variety of the full-data model. Across 11 held-out design families, predictor-based selection among existing dataset designs yields an average gap of 0.18 mm to the best available FEA deformation, versus an expected 1.59 mm for random selection. It does better than random selection in all 11 families. At a 10% depth-change bound, a network not used in the search predicts an average deformation reduction of 24.8% for six generated and existing panels, with a 3.2% average mass increase computed from geometry. Engineers can thus explore and optimize panels within this manufacturing-informed design space.

</details>


### [151] [Which Model, For Which Variable? Per-Variable Forecast Combination in Sparse ICU Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18149)
*Marius Fracarolli,Stefan Riezler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在五个ICU数据库、94个生理通道的24小时预测任务上，不存在对所有变量都最优的单一架构，按变量加权集成可稳定超越最佳单模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测论文通常只报告单一最佳架构，但作者认为这一提问方式掩盖了变量间差异：不同变量的最优模型不同，单一架构的结论在个体化变量层面并不成立。

Method: 基于MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV、eICU、HiRID和UMM五个ICU数据库，对94个通道做24小时预测，使用14个模型的候选池，并按变量拟合组合权重；进一步扩展候选池加入逐通道梯度提升树、ARIMA与ETS，比较学习型加权、秩次调和加权与等权平均。

Result: 逐变量组合在五个数据库上均优于最佳单模型，MSE改善6.1–11.0%（均值8.7%）；增益集中于数据最稀疏处（最稀疏三分位通道改善11.9%，实验室指标7.5%，最稠密三分位仅3.1%）。每变量取前五模型的秩次调和加权（不拟合权重）可恢复约五分之四增益。扩展池后梯度提升树成为验证集选出的最佳单模型，逐变量组合仍提升2.9–5.9%（四个数据库显著），同成员的等权平均则无效。

Conclusion: 学习型加权优于简单平均，但前提是权重受到约束；模型优劣排序随数据库规模变化，论文同时给出了"何时选哪个模型"的经验规则。

Abstract: Clinical forecasting papers typically report a single best architecture. We show that on multivariate ICU physiology this question conceals variable-specific differences: no architecture is best on every variable, and which model wins changes from variable to variable. Across five ICU databases (MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, eICU, HiRID, and UMM (University Medicine Mannheim), a German single-center ICU) we forecast 94 channels over a 24-hour horizon with a pool of 14 models, and combine them with weights fitted per variable. On test, the combination beats the best single model on all five databases by 6.1-11.0% mean squared error (mean 8.7%). The gain is concentrated where clinical data is thinnest: the sparsest third of channels improves by 11.9% and laboratory values by 7.5%, against 3.1% for the densest third. Rank-based harmonic weighting of the best five models per variable, with no fitted weights at all, recovers about four-fifths of the gain. Extending the pool afterwards with a per-channel gradient-boosted tree, ARIMA and ETS makes the tree the validation-selected best single model on all five databases; per-variable combination still improves on it by 2.9-5.9% (significant on four of five), while equal weighting of the same members does not. As on univariate benchmarks, learned combination beats simple averaging - but only once the weights are constrained. We also report which model to prefer when, and show that the ordering changes with database size.

</details>


### [152] [On the Limits of Sufficient Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18163)
*Klaus Weinbauer,Peter F. Stadler,Thomas Gärtner,Sagar Malhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出了一个统一的形式化框架，刻画结构化数据（图、字符串等）上分类器是否存在"充分解释"（sufficient explanations），并给出可计算的统计量以界定任务层面可实现的最大充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 充分解释（选取输入的一部分即可保证预测标签）已在图、字符串、图像等多种模态中被广泛采用，但缺乏统一的形式化分析，尤其不清楚在何种条件下充分解释才存在。

Method: （i）定义由实例空间、分类器与select关系构成的通用解释形式化体系，select关系规定每个实例可被选作解释的子实例或特征子集；（ii）证明充分解释器的存在性完全由分类器与select关系的交互决定；（iii）将分析扩展到概率充分性与删除充分性等实用变体；（iv）基于形式化分析设计统计量，用于界定给定任务可实现的最大充分性。

Result: 在语言、蛋白质与分子分类数据集上评估这些统计量，发现大多数任务上完全充分性不可达。

Conclusion: 由于所提出的界对任意解释器均成立，它们提供了任务层面的绝对参照，可用于在绝对意义上评判任意解释器的充分性。

Abstract: We formally characterize the conditions under which sufficient explanations exist for classifiers on structured data such as graphs and strings. We focus on post-hoc explainers that select parts of the input as sufficient explanations. An explanation is sufficient if the selected part alone ensures the predicted label. Sufficient explanations have been widely adopted across data modalities, including graphs, strings, and images. However, a unified formal analysis of sufficient explanations remains missing. We bridge this gap with four main contributions: (i) We define a general explanation formalism consisting of an instance space, a classifier, and a select relation. The select relation specifies which potential sub-instances or feature subsets can be selected as explanations from each instance; (ii) We show that the existence of sufficient explainers is fully characterized by the interplay between the classifier and the select relation; (iii) We extend this analysis to practically relevant variants of sufficiency, including probabilistic sufficiency and deletion sufficiency; (iv) Based on the formal analysis, we devise statistics that bound the maximum achievable sufficiency for a given task. We evaluate these statistics on language, protein, and molecular classification datasets and find that full sufficiency is unattainable on most of them. Since these bounds hold for every explainer, they provide a task-level reference against which any explainer's sufficiency can be judged in absolute terms.

</details>


### [153] [MarathonTS: a benchmark of long synthetic time series from window assembly and long-series generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18190)
*Ilyas Varshavskiy,Sergey Shulgin,Shuhrat Khalilbekov,Bonu Boboeva,Azizjon Azimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MarathonTS，首个在同一协议下比较"短窗拼接"与"长序列直接生成"两族方法的长合成时间序列开放基准，覆盖9个数据集、10个随机种子、83项保真度指标、14项下游任务效用、记忆性与计算成本，并以独立统计分析阶段给出排名与研究问题结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多合成时间序列应用需要远长于生成模型训练窗口的序列，而现有工作缺乏对"窗口拼接"与"长序列生成器"两族方法的统一、可复现对比。

Method: 构建统一协议：所有方法在9个开放小时级数据集上以10个种子生成等长序列；采用12组共83项保真度指标对照训练集、验证集与时间外分割三个真实参考；以仅用合成数据训练、真实数据测试（TSTR vs TRTR）的14项分类与回归任务衡量效用；并评估对训练数据的记忆性与计算开销；统计分析作为独立阶段读取公开表格，给出各切片排名、带检验与不确定性的研究问题答案及预设确认性族的结果。

Result: 在主实验中，方法在两个确认性单元上均存在差异；窗口的选择比接缝处理更重要，后者未提升序列质量；无任何方法的TSTR效果能匹敌真实序列；排行榜顶层在其他硬件上可复现，但其中精确名次不可复现。

Conclusion: MarathonTS为长合成时间序列研究提供了统一、可复现的评测基准；代码、数据哈希与全部结果公开，支持后续研究复用与验证。

Abstract: Many applications of synthetic time series require a series that is much longer than the windows on which a generative model is trained. Such a series can be assembled from a pool of short generated windows, which requires deciding which windows are taken, in what order, and how their seams are processed, or it can be produced directly by a long-series generator. This study presents MarathonTS, to our knowledge the first open benchmark that compares both families under one protocol. Every method produces a series of the same length on 9 open hourly datasets with 10 seeds. The series is evaluated by its fidelity to the real data (83 metrics in 12 groups, against three real references: train, validation and an out-of-time split), by its utility (models are trained on the synthetic series only and tested on real data, TSTR against TRTR, in 14 classification and regression tasks), by its memorisation of the training data and by its computational cost. The statistical analysis is carried out as a separate stage that reads the published tables and gives the rankings of every method in every slice, the answers to the research questions with tests and uncertainty, and the results of a pre-specified confirmatory family. In the main run, the methods differ in both confirmatory cells; the choice of windows matters more than the processing of their seams, which does not improve the series, and no method trains the downstream models as well as the real series. The top tier of the leaderboard is reproduced on other hardware, whereas the exact places within it are not. For the reproducibility of the study, the code, the data hashes and all results are publicly available (links below).

</details>


### [154] [T2ST: Data-Efficient Spatio-Temporal Foundation Models via Temporal Data and Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18199)
*Yue Xu,Kaiyun Wei,Wenying Duan,Zichen Xu,Shuai Ma,Xiaoxi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出T2ST数据高效训练框架，用有限时空观测加辅助时间序列、并从冻结的时间序列基础模型做知识蒸馏，仅用10%数据即可使时空基础模型达到与全量训练相当或更优的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空基础模型（STFMs）需要大规模训练语料，但同步观测与空间元数据获取和整理成本高，数据稀缺；相比之下时间序列数据丰富易得，且每个站点的观测本身构成时间序列，因此希望用丰富的时间序列数据弥补时空数据不足。

Method: 核心洞察：时间模式学习可借助大量外部时间序列，空间依赖学习仍锚定真实时空观测。据此：检索匹配序列强化稀疏观测的时间模式，筛选可靠的互补序列扩展时间覆盖；采用时序关系蒸馏对齐历史patch间的关系，并用多尺度预测蒸馏在细、粗时间分辨率上迁移预测行为，实现异构架构间的知识迁移。

Result: 仅使用原始时空数据的10%，在6个长期与12个短期预测数据集上，STFMs的整体预测性能与全量数据训练的对应模型相当或更优。

Conclusion: 为时空基础模型的数据高效训练提供了新范式：以外部时间序列基础模型为知识源，可在时空观测稀缺条件下显著降低数据需求并保持性能。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal foundation models (STFMs) require large-scale training corpora, yet synchronized observations and spatial metadata are costly to acquire and curate, limiting the availability of suitable data. By contrast, time-series data are abundant and easier to collect. Since observations at each location form a time series, this motivates a natural question: can abundant time-series data enhance STFMs when spatio-temporal observations are scarce? To bridge this gap, we propose T2ST, a data-efficient training framework that augments limited spatio-temporal observations with selected auxiliary time series and transfers knowledge from a frozen time-series foundation model. Our key insight is that temporal pattern learning can benefit from abundant external time series, while spatial dependency learning remains grounded in real spatio-temporal observations. Guided by this insight, T2ST retrieves matched sequences to reinforce sparsely observed temporal patterns and selects reliable complementary sequences to broaden temporal coverage. Temporal relation distillation aligns relationships among historical patches, while multi-scale prediction distillation transfers forecasting behavior at fine and coarse temporal resolutions, enabling knowledge transfer across heterogeneous architectures. Using only 10\% of the original spatio-temporal data, T2ST enables STFMs to achieve overall forecasting performance competitive with or superior to their full-data counterparts across six long-term and twelve short-term forecasting datasets. These results establish a new paradigm for data-efficient training of spatio-temporal foundation models. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/T2ST-65F3/.

</details>


### [155] [NeoResearch: Literature- and Experience-Guided Autonomous Time Series Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18235)
*Ze Guo,Mingyue Cheng,Xiaoyu Tao,Shuo Yu,Shangtong Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeoResearch：以LLM为研究者的文献与经验引导的自主研究框架，发现轻量、领域特定的时间序列预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建有效的时间序列预测模型需大量专家知识、网络科学文献证据与迭代实验；将知识与研究经验转化为模型设计需保留来源语境并在任务特定假设下验证适用性。

Method: 设计专家启发的双循环研究流程：假设驱动的架构探索循环 + 基于实验反馈的迭代训练与超参数优化循环；智能体自动从网络挖掘科学文献，抽取建模机制、经验证据与适用条件，形成任务落地的可检验架构假设；构建分层经验记忆，将文献发现与实验结果关联并固化为条件性经验，支持跨任务复用。

Result: 在10个数据集上取得有竞争力的性能；消融/验证支持双循环、文献抽取与记忆复用各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM智能体结合文献知识与实验经验，可自主发现轻量级领域特定预测模型，且经验可跨任务迁移复用。

Abstract: Developing effective forecasting models for diverse time-series domains requires substantial expert knowledge, evidence from scientific literature on the web, and iterative experimentation. However, translating this knowledge and research experience into model designs requires preserving source context and testing applicability under task-specific assumptions. In this paper, we propose NeoResearch, a literature- and experience-guided autonomous research framework that leverages large language models (LLMs) as researchers to discover lightweight, domain-specific forecasting models. Specifically, NeoResearch establishes an expert-inspired dual-loop research workflow, coupling hypothesis-driven architecture exploration with iterative training and hyperparameter optimization through experimental feedback. To enable knowledge-grounded discovery, the agent autonomously mines scientific literature from the Web, extracting modeling mechanisms, empirical evidence, and applicability conditions to formulate task-grounded, testable architecture hypotheses. Furthermore, a hierarchical experience memory links literature findings to experimental outcomes and consolidates conditional lessons, guiding architecture discovery and training optimization while supporting reuse across forecasting tasks. Experiments on ten datasets show competitive performance and support dual loops, literature extraction, and memory reuse. Our code is available.

</details>


### [156] [Small Is Not Dead: Distance-Aware Cluster Revival for e-means](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18238)
*Eyal Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文为e-means（FAISS中的mini-batch k-means）的"死簇复活"机制增加基于中心间距离的第二判据，避免误删覆盖稀有孤立小群体的簇，在不增加可测运行时间的前提下显著降低此类数据的测试误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: e-means每五个mini-batch删除运行点数低于均值十分之一的簇并用其中心分裂大簇，该机制在消融实验中对误差影响最大。但现有规则只看簇大小，无法区分"冗余的小簇"与"覆盖稀有孤立点群的小簇"，导致在此类数据上反复删除代价高昂的中心。

Method: 在计数条件之外加入距离条件：仅当小簇中心相对另一中心足够近（以运行估计的均方误差为尺度）时才删除。理论依据是当中心等于其点均值时，该平方距离是删除中心所引起的每点平均平方误差增量的上界。代价为每五个mini-batch对小簇做一次最近中心搜索，无可测运行时间增加。属CPU概念验证。

Result: 在e-means论文的embedding基准上从不阻止删除，结果与FAISS完全一致；在开发阶段未使用的含小型孤立群体数据集上，两轮迭代后测试误差相对基于计数的复活降低约10%–80%，并保持k-means++初始化的优势；其他评测数据集上与"开启/关闭复活"中较优者的差距约在1%以内。文中还报告了两个分析所预测的失效模式。

Conclusion: 以中心距离为补充判据可低成本修正死簇复活规则的盲点，在保持原有基准性能不变的同时改善含稀有孤立群体的数据；GPU实现留待后续工作。

Abstract: e-means (Bessa et al., 2026) is a recent mini-batch k-means algorithm, now part of the FAISS library, that reaches the error of Lloyd's algorithm about an order of magnitude faster on large embedding datasets. Every five mini-batches, it removes every cluster whose running point count falls below a tenth of the average and reuses its centre to split a large cluster. In the authors' ablation, removing this mechanism, called dead-cluster revival, raises the error more than removing any other component added to the moving-average update. The rule, however, considers only cluster size: it cannot distinguish a cluster that is small because it is redundant from one that covers a rare, isolated group of points, and on data with such groups it repeatedly removes centres whose removal is costly. We add a second condition, based on the distance between centres: a small cluster is removed only if its centre is close to another centre, relative to a running estimate of the mean squared error. When a centre equals the mean of its points, this squared distance is an upper bound on the average increase in squared error, per point of the cluster, caused by removing the centre. The condition costs one nearest-centre search for the small clusters every five mini-batches and adds no measurable running time. On the e-means paper's embedding benchmarks, it never prevents a removal, so the results are identical to those of FAISS. On datasets with small isolated groups not used during development, it reduces the test error after two epochs by roughly 10% to 80% relative to count-based revival, and it preserves the advantage of k-means++ initialization; on the other evaluation datasets, it stays within about 1% of the better of revival enabled and disabled. We also report two failure modes that the analysis predicts. This is a CPU proof of concept; GPU implementations are left to future work.

</details>


### [157] [Neural Classifiers as Error-Correcting Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18239)
*Sergey Salishev,Anton Makarov,Oleg Granichin,Renata Avros,Zeev Volkovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出统一框架，将监督深度分类视为Shannon意义下纠错码的解码：交叉熵优化使softmax分类器对齐最大似然解码，logits映射为到类码字的Hamming距离，并由此推导量化、蒸馏与正则化等新准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作对深度分类器的训练目标（交叉熵）与所学表示几何（如Neural Collapse）缺乏统一的理论解释；作者希望用一个编码理论视角把损失函数、决策边界稳定性与潜空间几何联系起来。

Method: 将类别对应为码字，把logits解释为到类码字的Hamming距离；证明交叉熵优化等价于最大似然解码，从而解释KL、Jeffreys等散度目标的出现及决策边界的几何稳定性；并把Neural Collapse与LeJEPA的几何刻画为各向同性噪声下的最优星座；指出深度网络在特征空间中隐式学习有效码本。

Result: 建立交叉熵与最大似然解码的对齐关系；给出标准散度目标与决策边界稳定性的几何解释；识别Neural Collapse、LeJEPA为各向同性噪声下的最优解码星座；区别于经典ECOC，说明深度网络隐式学习码本；据此导出无损量化、改进知识蒸馏和基于码冗余的架构正则化准则；并推广到自监督编码器。

Conclusion: 该编码理论视角为现代神经网络中监督分类与自监督表示的几何提供了统一的解释框架，并可直接转化为量化、蒸馏与正则化的实用设计准则。

Abstract: A unified framework is proposed that characterizes supervised deep classification as the decoding of error-correcting codes in the Shannon sense. Cross-entropy optimization is shown to align softmax classifiers with maximum-likelihood decoding, where logits map to Hamming distances to class-specific codewords. This correspondence explains the emergence of standard divergence objectives (e.g., Kullback-Leibler, Jeffreys) and provides a geometric basis for the stability of decision boundaries. Furthermore, the geometries of Neural Collapse and LeJEPA are identified as optimal constellations for decoding under isotropic noise. Unlike classical Error-Correcting Output Codes, deep networks are shown to learn effective codebooks in the feature space implicitly. This insight is leveraged to derive novel criteria for lossless quantization, improved knowledge distillation, and architectural regularization based on code redundancy. Finally, this perspective is extended to self-supervised encoders, offering a cohesive explanation for the geometry of latent representations in modern neural models.

</details>


### [158] [ParetoM$^3$: Learning on the Pareto Set under Preference Guidance via Min-Max-Min Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18252)
*Pei Tang,Songtao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究偏好引导的多目标优化：在多个非凸目标的弱Pareto集上最小化预设偏好函数，提出统一的惩罚型min-max-min（M³）问题及单循环一阶算法ParetoM³，并给出收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中各目标本质冲突，需要从Pareto集中选出符合偏好的折中解；现有方法难以在单一框架内同时兼顾偏好优化与弱Pareto最优性约束，且理论保证不足。

Method: 利用在弱Pareto集上取零的merit函数（价值函数），构造惩罚型min-max-min公式（M³问题），将偏好优化与弱Pareto最优性约束统一到单一目标中；据此设计单循环一阶算法ParetoM³求解其驻点。理论分析基于局部Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（KL）假设，提供非渐近收敛率，并证明所采用的驻性概念在既有假设下可退化为近似偏好驻性与近似KKT条件等现有最优性准则。

Result: 在局部KL假设下证明：当惩罚参数足够大时，ParetoM³可求得M³问题的ε-驻点，同时该点对原多目标问题近似弱Pareto最优，并具有显式非渐近收敛率；驻性概念与已有最优性准则相容。在合成基准、图像分类、回归与数学推理实验中，ParetoM³能稳定获得偏好的弱Pareto最优解且实用性能强。

Conclusion: ParetoM³以单一目标统一了偏好优化与弱Pareto最优性约束，算法结构简单（单循环一阶）、理论保证明确、实证效果良好，为偏好引导的多目标优化提供了兼具简洁性与理论严谨性的方案。

Abstract: Learning across multiple inherently conflicting objectives requires selecting desirable trade-offs from the Pareto set. In this work, we study preference-guided multi-objective optimization, where a predefined preference function is minimized over the weak Pareto set of multiple nonconvex objectives. Leveraging a merit function that vanishes on the weak Pareto set, we propose a penalty-based min-max-min formulation, termed the M$^3$ problem, which unifies preference optimization and the enforcement of weak Pareto optimality in a single objective. We further develop ParetoM$^3$, a single-loop first-order algorithm for computing stationary solutions of the resulting M$^3$ problem. Under a local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz assumption, we prove that ParetoM$^3$ finds an $ε$-stationary point of the M$^3$ problem that is also approximately weakly Pareto optimal for the original multi-objective problem when the penalty parameter is sufficiently large, with explicit non-asymptotic convergence rates. We also show that this stationarity notion recovers existing optimality criteria, including approximate preference stationarity and approximate Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions, under the same assumptions used in prior works. Experiments on a synthetic benchmark, image classification, regression, and math reasoning demonstrate that ParetoM$^3$ reliably attains preferred weak Pareto optimal solutions while achieving strong practical performance. Code is available at https://github.com/tpgoodboy/ParetoM3.

</details>


### [159] [Right Token, Right Time: From Per-Token Gating to Trajectory-Aware Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18274)
*Nima Eshraghi,Lovedeep Gondara,Sagarika Suresh,Xiaotong Xu,Jithin Pradeep,Fanny Chevalier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将SAE激活引导由"逐token全施加"重构为"固定预算下的逐token分配"问题，提出两种门控：无记忆的对比特征投影(CFP)与闭环自适应伯努利引导(CLABS)，在匹配预算下实现最大行为偏移并最好地保持流畅性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE引导在每个生成token上都施加干预，使表征偏离训练分布、损害生成质量。作者认为"在生成的哪些位置施加干预"是决定性控制维度，但既有方法未将其作为唯一变量加以隔离研究。

Method: 将引导建模为固定可引导token预算下的逐token分配问题，使各方法仅在token选择上不同。提出两种门控：CFP通过稀疏可解释的特征投影，无记忆地选择与目标行为对齐度最低的token；CLABS将分配视为闭环反馈控制，跟踪生成已累积的对齐程度，在轨迹落后处消耗预算。并在线性引导-响应模型上推导保证闭环稳定性的显式增益区间，分析积分项对稳态跟踪偏差的削减作用。

Result: 在匹配预算下，CFP与CLABS在各类预算门控中取得最大行为偏移；CLABS在预算最紧、分配最关键时表现最强，同时最能保持生成流畅性；理论分析给出CLABS的稳定性增益区域并证明积分项降低稳态偏差。

Conclusion: 干预施加的时机（where/when along generation）是SAE引导中被忽视但决定性的控制轴；在统一预算分配框架下，无记忆与轨迹感知两类门控均优于预算内基线，其中闭环设计在紧预算与流畅性保持上最具优势。

Abstract: Activation steering via sparse autoencoders (SAEs) enables behavioral control of large language models without task-specific fine-tuning. Yet, standard methods apply the steering signal at every generated token, displacing representations from the distribution the model was trained on and eroding generation quality. We show that \emph{when} along a generation the intervention is applied is a decisive axis of control. We cast steering as a per-token allocation problem under a fixed budget of steerable tokens, so that methods differ only in which tokens they select, making allocation the sole variable. Within this framework we introduce two gates. The first, Contrastive Feature Projection (CFP), is memoryless and selects the tokens least aligned with the target behavior through a sparse, interpretable feature projection. The second, Closed-Loop Adaptive Bernoulli Steering (CLABS), is trajectory aware. It treats allocation as closed-loop feedback control, tracking the alignment a generation has accumulated so far and spending budget where the trajectory falls behind. On a linear steering-response model we derive an explicit gain region that guarantees closed-loop stability of CLABS and show that its integral term reduces the steady-state tracking offset. Empirically, CFP and CLABS achieve the largest behavioral shift among budgeted gates at matched budgets, with CLABS strongest under the tight budgets where allocation matters most, while best preserving fluency.

</details>


### [160] [Clean Tickets, Wrong Orders: Catching Silent Errors with a Language Model in Your Browser Tab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18284)
*Umar Ahmed,Maxim Dunaev,Mohammad Nour Al Awad,Sergey Ivanov,Ivan Khodnenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一个微调的0.8B模型通过WebAssembly在浏览器本地将顾客点单文本解析为结构化订单，离线评分并与服务器评估高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型做订单结构化解析时存在两类失败：语法破损（易被拒）与"静默错误"——生成格式合法但用户从未下的订单，后者更危险。作者希望提供一个可交互的演示，让用户直观体验、寻找并诱发此类静默错误，同时保证订单文本不出设备。

Method: 将llama.cpp编译为WebAssembly，使0.8B微调模型完全在浏览器标签页内推理；页面边输入边解析订单；将PIZZA基准的匹配器移植到TypeScript对每张小票打分；对菜单外内容返回"?"以模拟开放网络；并提供让访客主动寻找或制造静默错误的交互。

Result: 在全部1,357条PIZZA测试订单上，浏览器端部署模型与服务器评估的输出一致率为99.0%，精确匹配相同（0.882）；并报告了在3台消费级笔记本上的延迟。

Conclusion: 该演示证明小型语言模型可在纯浏览器端完成订单结构化，几乎无损复现服务器端评估表现，并为研究"静默错误"提供了可交互、隐私友好的开放平台。

Abstract: A small language model that turns a typed order into structure can fail in two ways. It can print something broken, which any system rejects. Or it can print a clean, well formed order that nobody placed. Our companion work calls the second kind a silent error, and it is the dangerous one. We present a web demo in which a fine-tuned 0.8B parameter model runs entirely inside the visitor's browser tab, through llama.cpp compiled to WebAssembly. No order text leaves the device. The page parses orders as the visitor types, scores every ticket with the PIZZA benchmark's own matcher ported to TypeScript, refuses anything off the menu with "?" so the model can face the open web, and lets visitors hunt for silent errors or try to cause one. Run in the browser on all 1,357 PIZZA test orders, the deployed model writes the same output as the server evaluation for 99.0% of them and reaches the same exact match (0.882); we also report its latency on 3 consumer laptops. Demo: https://wrong.cuyor.com

</details>


### [161] [Emulating physics-based XCO2 retrievals across OCO-2 viewing modes with compact neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18333)
*Xianlei San,Chengxing Zhai,Hui Su,Kuai Dai,Shuangchen Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于SVD光谱压缩与紧凑多层感知机(MLP)的神经网络代理模型，用以高效模拟OCO-2卫星CO2柱平均干空气摩尔分数(XCO2)的全物理反演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于卫星光谱的全物理反演计算代价高昂，难以满足大规模、多模式、长时序业务的CO2柱浓度产品需求。

Method: 按波段对OCO-2光谱做奇异值分解(SVD)压缩，得到光谱得分(spectral scores)；将得分、先验信息与观测几何输入紧凑MLP，预测“全物理反演值−业务先验”的差值，再加回先验得到XCO2估计。训练集为2015–2019年观测，验证集为2020年。

Result: 在2021–2025年测试中，相对ACOS业务产品的年平均RMSE为：海洋耀斑0.89 ppm、陆地耀斑1.35 ppm、天底1.43 ppm；对东亚天底观测（采用已发表的谱Transformer筛选准则），其年平均误差低于该模型2020–2022年报道值。

Conclusion: 紧凑神经网络结合压缩光谱可在多观测模式与多年份上高效近似全物理反演，具备业务化替代潜力。

Abstract: Operational full-physics retrieval of the column-averaged dry-air mole fraction of carbon dioxide from satellite spectra is computationally demanding. We use singular value decomposition to compress spectra from the Orbiting Carbon Observatory-2 separately by band. Compact multilayer perceptrons use the resulting spectral scores, prior information, and viewing geometry to predict the difference between the full-physics retrieval and its operational prior. Adding the predicted difference to the prior yields an estimate of the retrieved mole fraction. The models were trained on 2015-2019 observations and validated on 2020 observations. In 2021-2025 tests, the mean annual root-mean-square errors relative to the Atmospheric Carbon Dioxide Observations from Space retrieval product were 0.89, 1.35, and 1.43 parts per million for ocean glint, land glint, and nadir observations, respectively. On East Asian nadir observations screened using the published criteria for the spectral Transformer, our model yielded a lower mean annual error than reported for that model in 2020-2022. These results show that compact neural networks using compressed spectra can efficiently emulate the full-physics retrieval across multiple viewing modes and years.

</details>


### [162] [DECODE: Decomposable Environments for COntrolled Sequential-Decision Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18367)
*Diego Silva Prado,Bruno Caldas de Souza,Tiago Amaral Penteado de Jesus,Ricardo Guimarães Herrmann,Cristiano Breuel,Cristiano Bevitori Maffia De Oliveira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DECODE 是一个用于在部分观测序列决策环境中对求解器性能进行受控干预测试的基准：其催收生成器提供五个共享已知动态矩阵、仅改变记忆参数与过程噪声的环境，通过指定状态输入、响应输入与规划期限的对照实验检验改进假设。在确定性基础环境上，规划期限由 1 个月延长至 6 个月可使两个梯度提升求解器得分提高 15–17 个百分点；最佳求解器仍低于最优 15.6 点，一组指定干预先降 6.3 点、替换为真实还款响应后升 15.6 点，净恢复约 9 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当序贯决策系统未达最优时，其分数本身并不能揭示哪些修改能改进决策。论文旨在构建一个基准，使关于求解器性能差距来源的假设可以通过受控实验（而非仅凭总体得分）得以检验：在已知动态与显式信息条件下，系统性地改变规划规则或可用信息，观察对决策质量的影响。

Method: DECODE 的消费者信贷催收生成器提供五个部分观测环境，共享已知的动态矩阵，仅变化记忆参数与过程噪声。实验在保持求解器其余部分固定的前提下，改变选定的输入或规划规则；每次比较明确指定求解器的状态输入、响应输入与规划期限。基础确定性环境经过挑选，使隐藏状态下一月与六月规划之间存在较大差距。随后对最佳分级求解器施加一个指定的干预序列：三项对状态输入及其搜索中评估方式的修改，再将拟合的还款模型替换为真实响应（以隐藏借款人特征评估）。

Result: 在确定性基础环境上，规划期限从 1 个月延长到 6 个月，使两个梯度提升求解器的得分相对于精确最优（以隐藏状态与借款人特征计算）提高 15 至 17 个百分点；即便在六月期限下，最佳分级求解器（其还款响应由日志拟合）仍比最优低 15.6 点。指定干预序列中，三项状态输入及其评估方式的改变使得分下降 6.3 点；将拟合还款模型替换为真实响应后上升 15.6 点，净恢复约 9 点。

Conclusion: 由于中间效应依赖于还款模型与改变的顺序，这些干预不能把性能差距唯一归因于彼此独立的成因。在此限度内，DECODE 使关于求解器性能的假设能够在已知动态与明确信息条件下通过指定干预得到可检验的验证。

Abstract: When a sequential decision system falls short of an optimum, its score does not reveal which modifications would improve its decisions. DECODE is a benchmark for testing such modifications through controlled experiments on planning and available information. Its consumer-credit collections generator provides five partially observed environments that share known dynamics matrices and vary a memory parameter and process noise. The experiments change selected inputs or planning rules while keeping the rest of the solver fixed. Each comparison specifies the solver's state inputs, response inputs and planning horizon. On the deterministic base environment, which was selected for a large gap between six-month and one-month planning with the hidden state, extending planning from one to six months increases the scores of two gradient-boosted solvers by 15 to 17 percentage points of the exact optimum, computed with the hidden state and borrower traits. Even at this horizon, the best graded solver, whose repayment response is fitted on logs, remains 15.6 points below that optimum. In a specified intervention sequence, three changes to its state inputs and their evaluation during search lower its score by 6.3 points, and replacing its fitted repayment model with the true response, evaluated with hidden borrower traits, then raises it by 15.6, a net recovery of nine of those points. Because the intermediate effects are conditional on the repayment model and the order of the changes, they do not uniquely assign the gap to separate causes. Within these limits, DECODE makes hypotheses about solver performance testable through specified interventions under known dynamics and explicit information conditions.

</details>


### [163] [f-Divergence Self-Play for Tabular Anomaly Detection via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18370)
*Hoang Tran Vuong,Linh Ngo Van,Dang Nguyen,Thin Nguyen,Phuoc Nguyen,Mehrtash Harandi,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiSPaT：基于f-散度最小化的自博弈微调框架，通过让LLM生成伪异常样本并与判别器对抗，收紧对正常数据的分布刻画，在表格异常检测上超越LLM基线、深度学习与经典无监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据混合数值、类别与文本等异构特征，正常性表征难学；现有将行序列化为文本并用LLM的方法仅依赖一次性监督微调，对"正常性描述"的信号约束不足。

Method: 提出自博弈微调框架DiSPaT。理论上以f-散度最小化为基础，推导出训练目标与"真实正常分布—模型生成分布"差距缩小之间的紧近似。训练采用交替优化：每轮当前策略生成合成样本作为伪异常，评论家判别器学习区分伪异常与真实正常样本，该信号驱动策略更新，使模型分布逐步逼近平稳的正常数据分布。

Result: 在多个基准上，DiSPaT持续优于先前的LLM方法、深度学习方法以及经典无监督检测器。

Conclusion: 通过自博弈对抗式微调替代一次性SFT，可有效增强LLM对正常数据分布的表征能力，为异构表格异常检测提供了有理论支撑的新范式。

Abstract: Anomaly detection in tabular data poses significant challenges due to heterogeneous feature types-mixing numerical, categorical, and textual attributes, which complicate learning meaningful representations of normality. Recent work has applied large language models (LLMs) to this problem by serializing table rows as text sequences, yet these approaches rely on one-shot supervised fine-tuning that offers limited signal to tighten the model's description of normality. We propose DiSPaT, a self-play fine-tuning framework that strengthens the model's understanding of normal data. Building on the theoretical foundation of f-divergence minimization, we derive a tight approximation connecting our training objective to reducing the distributional gap between real normal data and model-generated samples. DiSPaT operates through an alternating optimization: at each iteration, the current policy generates synthetic samples that serve as pseudo-anomalies, while a critic discriminator learns to distinguish these from real normal samples; this signal drives policy updates that progressively align the model distribution with the true normal-data distribution. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DiSPaT consistently outperforms prior LLM-based methods, deep learning approaches, and classical unsupervised detectors for tabular anomaly detection.

</details>


### [164] [Where Tree Ensembles Decide: Structural Local Explanation with Decision Predicate Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18382)
*Sylvio Barbon Junior,Luca Longo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出局部决策谓词图（local DPG），将样本执行的树规则汇总为可验证的计算记录，并从中选出"关键谓词"——估计最能将预测推向目标类的已执行谓词，为用户提供具体阈值及共享该阈值的树。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树集成在表格数据上准确度高，但单次预测的计算分散在数百条决策规则中。现有解释方法存在空缺：特征归因与反事实解释关注特征影响或输入变化，未说明预测对哪些树阈值敏感；结构解释则压缩规则、丢失其背后的树。

Method: 构造local DPG：聚合样本执行的所有规则，形成带类别支持、能复现每次被解释预测的可验证记录；在执行的谓词中依据"跨越后使模型最强烈移向目标类"的估计准则选取关键谓词，无需外部解释器。

Result: 在16个数据集、四类集成模型上，关键谓词区分不同预测邻居的能力优于随机谓词与最近阈值谓词。单次跨越在46–69%的错误预测中恢复真实类别，而将同一规则施加于TreeSHAP排名第一的特征仅29–50%；在植入故障实验中，46–71%的错误中被定位到故障阈值，随机仅6–21%。

Conclusion: 对关键谓词的无训练编辑未能降低未见数据上的误差，但关键谓词通过指明"预测可在何处被改变"，对归因与反事实解释形成补充。

Abstract: Tree ensembles are among the most accurate models for tabular data, yet the computation behind a single prediction is spread over hundreds of decision rules. Feature attributions and counterfactuals explain such predictions through the influence of features or a change of the input, leaving open which of the thresholds applied by the trees a prediction responds to, while structural explanations compress these rules and lose the trees behind them. To address this gap, we propose a local Decision Predicate Graph (local DPG), which gathers the rules executed for a sample into a verifiable record of the computation, with a class support that reproduces every explained prediction. From these rules we select the critical predicate, the executed predicate whose crossing is estimated to move the model most strongly toward a chosen class, which offers the user a concrete threshold and the trees that share it, without an external explainer. Across 16 datasets and four ensemble families, it separates differently predicted neighbours better than random and nearest-threshold predicates. A single crossing recovers the true class in 46-69% of wrong predictions, against 29-50% when the same rule is applied to the feature ranked first by TreeSHAP, a measure close to the selection criterion, and on planted faults it finds the faulty threshold in 46-71% of the resulting errors, against 6-21% at random. Although training-free edits of these predicates did not reduce the error on unseen data, the critical predicate complements attributions and counterfactuals by naming where a prediction can be changed.

</details>


### [165] [WeatherDreamer: Learning a World Model from Multimodal Earth Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18393)
*Kate Duffy,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Thomas J. Vandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeatherDreamer 是一个直接从18个全球观测系统端到端学习"观测→潜在状态→逐小时递推→预报"完整循环的世界模型，无需再分析资料，可在1块GPU上1.5分钟生成逐小时全球分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习天气预报模型精度已接近业务全球预报，但仍依赖由独立且昂贵的资料同化（DA）系统生成的分析场（通常6小时更新一次）；而多数观测到观测模型省略了递推滤波循环，无法像DA那样在稀疏不规则观测间携带背景状态。

Method: 构建世界模型WeatherDreamer：从观测推断潜在状态，用学习到的动力学逐小时向前推演，每小时用新观测对状态进行校正，并能预测任一观测系统在任意位置的测量值；训练数据为18个全球观测系统，不使用再分析数据。

Result: 在留出站点上，其集合预报在12小时内对温度、露点、风的技巧超过NOAA GEFS，且校准优于GEFS与ECMWF集合；降水、云顶高度、太阳辐照度（推理时从未作为输入）在短时效上达到或超过业务产品水平。相对GSI首猜场，地面站短期偏差在10%以内、湿度敏感微波通道在25%以内，但温度探测通道偏差最高达5倍。模型在未给定观测误差的情况下，学到的更新隐含误差接近已知仪器/反演误差；并能分离观测几何与大气状态，恢复83–96%的临边变暗效应，生成无缝星下点影像。

Conclusion: 同化与预报可以直接从观测中联合学习；WeatherDreamer证明以学习方式替代传统DA流程具有可行性，并具备高计算效率。

Abstract: Machine learning weather models now rival operational global forecasts but still start from analyses produced by separate, expensive data assimilation (DA) systems, which for global models are typically updated every six hours. Observation-to-observation models avoid this dependence, but most omit the recursive filtering cycle that lets DA systems carry a background state through sparse, irregular observations. We introduce WeatherDreamer, a world model that learns this cycle end-to-end from 18 global observing systems, without reanalysis. It infers a latent state from observations, carries it forward hour by hour with learned dynamics, corrects it each hour with new observations, and can predict what any of its observing systems would measure at any location. At held-out stations, its ensembles beat NOAA's Global Ensemble Forecast System (GEFS) for temperature, dew point and wind out to 12 h and are better calibrated than GEFS and the ECMWF ensemble. Precipitation, cloud-top height and solar irradiance, never inputs at inference, are inferred with skill matching or exceeding operational products at short leads. Against the first guess of NOAA's Gridpoint Statistical Interpolation (GSI) DA system, WeatherDreamer's short-range departures are within 10% of GSI's at surface stations and within 25% for moisture-sensitive microwave channels, but up to five times larger for temperature-sounding channels. Never given observation errors, its learned update assumes errors close to known instrument and retrieval errors of microwave sounders and scatterometer winds. WeatherDreamer also separates viewing geometry from atmospheric state, recovering 83-96% of observed limb darkening and producing seamless nadir-view imagery. Producing an hourly global analysis in about 1.5 minutes on one GPU, WeatherDreamer shows that assimilation and forecasting can be learned together directly from observations.

</details>


### [166] [Deep Neural Networks Training Efficiency: Does Grokking Modify its Dynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18396)
*Eduardo Cueto-Mendoza,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究 grokking（明显过拟合后延迟出现的分布外泛化）是否改变深度网络的训练效率（以单位能量所获精度衡量），结论是 grokking 对整体训练效率动态没有实质影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking 现象被广泛观察到，但其对训练效率（每单位能量换取的精度）的影响尚不清楚；作者希望检验 grokking 转换是否在效率曲线上产生可察觉的偏离。

Method: 采用模块算术与 MNIST 两个经典设定，在 16/32/64 位精度下比较两个网络模型；用 Orthograd 施加不同强度以考察是否能诱发 grokking；短时训练至 500 epoch、长时训练至 20,000 epoch，并分析效率随训练的变化曲线。

Result: 短时实验中，逐步增强的 Orthograd 未能诱发 grokking，效率平滑下降；长时实验中 Orthograd 诱发了 grokking，效率曲线近似单调，在 grokking 转换处无明显局部上升。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，grokking 不实质性改变整体训练效率动态。

Abstract: We study whether grokking, the delayed emergence of out-of-distribution generalization after apparent overfitting, alters training efficiency in deep neural networks, where efficiency is measured as accuracy per unit energy. Using two canonical settings, modular arithmetic and MNIST, we analyze two neural network models under 16-, 32-, and 64-bit precision and use Orthograd to test whether grokking produces noticeable deviations from the typically decreasing dynamics of training efficiency. We consider both short training runs of up to 500 epochs and long training runs of up to 20,000 epochs, and in both cases identify grokking as a sharp improvement in test accuracy after a sustained train-test gap. In the short-horizon experiments, applying Orthograd at progressively stronger levels during early training does not induce grokking, and efficiency remains smoothly decreasing. In the long-horizon experiments, Orthograd induces grokking, but the efficiency curves remain approximately monotonic and show no appreciable local increase at the grokking transition. These results suggest that, in our setting, grokking does not materially alter overall training-efficiency dynamics.

</details>


### [167] [Fuse-PINN: Combining Finite Element Methods and Physics-Informed Neural Networks to enable massively parallel simulations of non-linear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18409)
*Stanislas Pamela,Nicolas Pailliez,Emmanuel Franck,Victor Michel-Dansac,Laurent Navoret,Vignesh Gopakumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于有限元法的神经网络公式（FEM-NN）并实现为 Fuse-PINN 框架，配合能量自然梯度下降优化器，在多 GPU HPC 上实现近完美并行扩展，反驳"PINNs 无法扩展"的观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINNs 在 PDE 建模中发展迅速，但普遍受限于难以在高性能计算架构上扩展，尤其面对大空间域、湍流动力学等非线性物理模型；现有演示多局限于简单方程、少变量、短时程的玩具模型，学界甚至形成"PINNs 无法扩展"的成见。

Method: 提出数学上稳健的有限元法神经网络（FEM-NN）公式，并将其集成到模块化的综合框架 Fuse-PINN 中；该公式为 PINNs 在基于 GPU 的 HPC 系统上提供可扩展的并行化基础；同时实现能量自然梯度下降（Energy Natural Gradient Descent）优化器以实现高精度收敛。

Result: 在磁约束聚变相关的物理模型上进行了验证，包括具有细尺度动力学的非线性对流模型；在多 GPU 运行中，随问题规模增大实现了近完美的扩展性。

Conclusion: 结果证明 PINNs 可以在 HPC 架构上有效扩展，能够处理大空间域上的非线性湍流类动力学问题，从而反驳了"PINNs 无法扩展"的论断，并为大规模科学计算中的 PINNs 应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Research in the domain of Physics-Informed Neural Networks applied to PDE modelling has advanced significantly in recent years, providing a wide ecosystem of novel methods. However, one frequent limitation is the inability of PINNs to scale out on High-Performance Computing architectures to address more ambitious challenges, particularly non-linear physics models with turbulent dynamics in large spatial domains. Typical studies restrain demonstrations to toy models with simple physics equations, few variables, and rarely any complex non-linear dynamics evolved over long timescales. Part of the AI community has even adopted the idea that "PINNs don't scale". This work proves otherwise. We present a mathematically robust formulation of Finite-Element-Method for Neural Networks (FEM-NN) implemented into a comprehensive, modular framework: Fuse-PINN. This formulation provides the basis for a sclable parallelisation of Physics-Informed Neural Networks on GPU-based HPC systems. Furthermore, an Energy Natural Gradient Descent optimiser is implemented to allow high-precision convergence. Fuse-PINN is demonstrated on physics models relevant to Magnetic Confinement Fusion, including non-linear convective models with fine-scale dynamics. Near-perfect scaling is achieved on multi-GPU runs with increasing problem size.

</details>


### [168] [PACT: A Decoder-Confidence-Guided Two-Round Receiver for Joint Channel and Phase-Noise Estimation in Sub-THz OFDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18450)
*Ruilin Wang,Xiaodai Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种由译码器置信度引导的两轮接收机（PACT估计器＋带状LMMSE均衡＋神经归一化最小和译码），联合估计频域信道与时域相位噪声，显著降低编码子THz OFDM的码块错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子THz OFDM接收同时受频率选择性衰落与振荡器相位噪声影响，单一传统估计或均衡难以同时应对两类损伤，且现有学习型方法缺乏译码反馈利用。

Method: 两轮接收架构：复数域PACT估计器通过交互的复值分支联合估计频域信道与等效时域PN；结合带状线性最小均方误差（LMMSE）均衡与神经网络归一化最小和（NMS）译码。第二轮利用译码器产生的伪导频与连续星座置信度，经PACT输入与注意力机制进行精化，两轮参数共享。

Result: 在Eb/N0=30 dB下，相对最优学习型基线，两个基准场景的码块错误率分别约降低70%与57%；冻结权重测试显示方法可跨信道/PN模型、载频及FFT/带宽配置适用。

Conclusion: 译码置信度引导的两轮联合估计-均衡-译码框架能有效抑制子THz OFDM的衰落与相位噪声，并具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Frequency-selective fading and oscillator phase noise (PN) jointly impair coded sub-THz OFDM reception. We propose a decoder-confidence-guided two-round receiver combining a {pilot-aware complex transformer} (PACT) estimator, banded linear minimum mean-square error equalization, and neural normalized min-sum decoding. PACT jointly estimates the frequency-domain channel and effective time-domain PN through interacting complex-valued branches. Decoder-derived pseudo-pilots and continuous constellation-aware confidence guide second-round refinement through PACT inputs and attention, with parameters shared across rounds. At $E_b/N_0=30$~dB, the PACT receiver achieves approximately $70\%$ and $57\%$ lower code-block error rates than the best-performing learning-based baseline in the two benchmark settings, respectively. Frozen-weight tests further demonstrate applicability across channel/PN models, carrier frequencies, and FFT/bandwidth configurations.

</details>


### [169] [Black-Box Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Models via Ensemble-of-Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18453)
*Wang Ma,Debarun Bhattacharjya,Junkyu Lee,Nhan H Pham,Harsha Kokel,Qiang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两级集成(双层)不确定性估计框架：内层为固定提示的随机解码，外层为保持语义的提示扰动，在嵌入空间用全协方差定律精确分解认知不确定性(EU)与偶然不确定性(AU)，仅需黑盒采样，并提供三项理论支撑与跨基准实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠使用 LLM 需要区分"提示本身固有的歧义"与"模型真实知识缺口"的不确定性估计。贝叶斯与深度集成方法原则上可实现 aleatoric/epistemic 分解，但在 LLM 规模上计算代价过高且需白盒访问；现有黑盒方法只能输出单一标量，无法做此区分。

Method: 构建两级集成：内层为固定提示的随机解码，外层为保持语义的提示扰动；在连续嵌入空间度量变异性，用全协方差定律给出精确的"扰动内/扰动间"分解，从而估计 AU/EU，只需黑盒采样访问。理论贡献(i)嵌套蒙特卡洛的有限样本偏差恒等式及闭式偏差校正的 EU 估计;(ii)贝叶斯桥接界，界定扰动总体 AU/EU 与贝叶斯目标之间的差距;(iii)分离性结果，识别任何仅依赖一致性的黑盒方法必然漏检、而基于扰动的 EU 可检测的一类失效模式。

Result: 在五个短问答基准、四个指令微调 LLM(跨两个模型家族、两种规模)上，该估计器与强白盒基线相当；在相同生成预算下，与领先黑盒方法在错误排序上持平，同时能分解 AU 与 EU。受控配对干预显示 AU 与 EU 分别选择性响应提示歧义与模型知识缺口这两类可操作失效来源；在 NQ 长答案split上的长文试点表明该框架可不经再训练扩展到 claim 级事实性评估。

Conclusion: 该两级集成在仅需黑盒访问的前提下，实现了可与白盒方法竞争并优于单标量黑盒方法的 AU/EU 分解估计，且有理论保证与可解释的失效归因，可扩展至长文本事实性评估。

Abstract: Reliable use of large language models (LLMs) requires uncertainty estimates that separate ambiguity intrinsic to a prompt from genuine knowledge gaps. Bayesian and deep-ensemble methods deliver this aleatoric/epistemic decomposition in principle, but are computationally prohibitive at LLM scale and require white-box access. Existing black-box methods, in contrast, summarize to a single scalar that cannot make the distinction. We introduce a two-level ensemble that bridges this gap. Stochastic decoding of a fixed prompt forms the inner ensemble; meaning-preserving semantic perturbations of the prompt form the outer ensemble. Variability is measured in a continuous embedding space, and the law of total covariance gives an exact within-/between-perturbation decomposition to estimate aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty (AU/EU); it requires only black-box sampling access. On top of this estimator, we contribute three theoretical results that ground the framework: (i) a finite-sample bias identity for nested Monte Carlo with a closed-form bias-corrected epistemic estimator; (ii) a Bayesian bridge bounding the gap between perturbation-population AU/EU and Bayesian targets; and (iii) a separation result identifying a class of failure modes that any consistency-only black-box method provably misses while perturbation-based EU detects. Across five short QA benchmarks and four instruction-tuned LLMs spanning two families and two scales, our estimator is competitive with strong white-box baselines, and at a matched generation budget it matches leading black-box methods on error ranking while decomposing AU and EU; controlled paired interventions show that AU and EU respond selectively to distinct, actionable sources of failure: prompt ambiguity and model knowledge gaps; and a long-form pilot on the NQ long-answer split shows the framework extends to claim-level factuality without retraining.

</details>


### [170] [Sample-efficient prediction of high-entropy alloy strength through outcome-aware robust sequential learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18462)
*Ahmed Shoyeb Raihan,Md Habibor Rahman,Ashish Nimbarte,Imtiaz Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ROSE-HEA框架，以结果感知的鲁棒序列实验设计在小样本预算下高精度预测高熵合金抗拉强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵合金抗拉强度（UTS）的准确预测可指导成分与工艺决策，但拉伸实验数据获取成本高、实验难度大。

Method: 构建样本高效的ROSE-HEA采集策略：基于候选UTS结果对预测更新及互补高斯过程（GP）描述支撑度的预期影响进行估计，再在参考域上重新评估剩余最坏情况期望预测风险以筛选信息量最大的候选。回溯评估使用501条HEA记录，与EI、UCB贝叶斯优化、随机GP采样及八种常规回归器在30次匹配运行（共享初始观测与测试条件）下对比。

Result: 从25个初始观测出发、额外采集100个响应，ROSE-HEA取得最低平均RMSE（152.84 MPa）与最高平均R²（0.7443）；相较EI与UCB，平均RMSE分别降低6.16%与6.37%。所选观测在六个回归器族上均提升预测并带来更广的设计空间覆盖；以125个观测即超越最强常规回归器的平均预测性能（后者需185个），训练样本减少32.4%。

Conclusion: 结果感知的鲁棒序列学习方法可在受限训练预算下提升高熵合金UTS预测性能。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of high-entropy alloy (HEA) strength can guide composition and processing decisions; however, obtaining tensile-test data for HEAs can be costly and experimentally demanding. This study proposes Robust Outcome-aware Sequential Experimentation for High-Entropy Alloys (ROSE-HEA), a sample-efficient framework for predicting ultimate tensile strength (UTS) from alloy composition and processing conditions. Its acquisition policy anticipates how possible UTS outcomes update predictions and the support for complementary Gaussian process (GP) descriptions. It then reassesses the worst-case expected predictive risk remaining across a reference domain to select informative candidates. A retrospective evaluation on 501 HEA records compares ROSE-HEA with expected-improvement (EI) and upper-confidence-bound (UCB) Bayesian optimization (BO), random GP sampling, and eight conventional regressors across 30 matched runs with shared initial observations and test conditions. Starting from 25 initial observations and acquiring 100 additional responses, ROSE-HEA achieves the lowest mean root-mean-square error (RMSE), at 152.84 MPa, and the highest mean R^2, at 0.7443. Mean RMSE is reduced by 6.16% and 6.37% relative to EI and UCB, respectively. The selected observations improve prediction across six regressor families and provide broader design-space coverage. ROSE-HEA also surpasses the strongest conventional regressor's mean predictive performance using 125 rather than 185 observations, corresponding to 32.4% fewer training observations. These findings demonstrate the potential of outcome-aware robust sequential learning to improve UTS prediction in HEAs under constrained training budgets.

</details>


### [171] [Asymptotic Margins and Feature Confinement in Conic Classification Heads under Softmax Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18507)
*Omer Nezih Gerek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究锥函数分类头在softmax交叉熵训练下是否使特征保持有界。作者证明：每个类的区域被一个旋转二次曲面界定，其离心率为某学习比率的倒数；并引入可由二阶锥规划计算的渐近间隔。类损失的下确界为零当且仅当其渐近间隔为正，此时损失仅在特征发散时趋于零；当间隔为负时损失是强制的，极小值点落在显式球内。由于softmax只依赖分数差，有界区域并不约束特征，因此有界区域不限制特征。该结论可推广到其他范数（含多面锥函数）。在CIFAR-10/100上，67个训练好的锥头共1210个单元全部具有正间隔，而原型头仅有10个单元中的9个有界，符合理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥函数分类头为每个类构造有界特征区域，通常用于拒识未见类样本。作者关心这种几何有界性是否在softmax交叉熵训练下依然成立，即这些区域能否真正约束特征不发散，从而厘清"有界类别区域"与"特征有界性"之间的关系。
